Case studies evaluate three differently scaled LLMs on IEEE 39- and 118-bus scenarios. These results validate the framework ability to detect, diagnose, and correct task-conditional faithfulness failures.

</details>


### [175] [LLM-Assisted Ontology Engineering and Construction of a French Legal Knowledge Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24551)
*G{é}nesis Montenegro,Mokhtar Boumedyen Billami,Catherine Faron,Fabien Gandon,Pierre Monnin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个两阶段的LLM辅助工作流，用于将法国维护法规转化为本体论和法律知识图谱，实验显示该方法能产生稳健的结构化输出并显著减少重复实体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 维护法规是复杂的法律文本，在处理特定案例时难以利用，且难以集成到操作系统中。因此需要一种方法将法规文本结构化，以便于实际应用和系统集成。

Method: 采用两阶段LLM辅助工作流：第一阶段基于SEMLEG核心本体进行本体工程，包括从分层语料库样本中开放提取类型化实体和三元组、通过基于嵌入的融合进行标签规范化、归纳候选对象属性及其签名（域和范围）；第二阶段利用生成的本体指导全语料库的封闭式三元组提取和RDF图谱构建。

Result: 使用GPT-4.1和mistral-large-2512进行的实验显示：结构化输出稳健，类对齐接近完整，融合后重复实体和谓词显著减少；少于20%的三元组引入未见过的属性，但较低的精确签名合规率揭示了现有谓词的新域-范围组合。

Conclusion: 研究结果指出谓词规范化和新观察到的关系签名验证是工业维护环境中的关键精炼步骤，为法规文本的结构化应用提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Maintenance regulations are complex legal texts that are difficult to exploit when addressing a specific case and challenging to integrate into operational systems. This paper presents a two-stage LLM-assisted workflow for French maintenance regulations: ontology engineering from a SEMLEG-based core ontology, followed by construction of an ontology-grounded French legal knowledge graph. The first stage consists in the open extraction of typed entities and triples from a stratified corpus sample, the normalization of labels through embedding-based fusion, and the induction of candidate object properties with their signature (domain and range). The second stage uses the resulting ontology to guide the closed extraction of triples and RDF graph construction over the full corpus. Experiments with GPT-4.1 and mistral-large-2512 show robust structured outputs, near-complete class alignment, and a substantial reduction of duplicated entities and predicates after fusion. Fewer than 20% of triples introduce unseen properties, while lower exact signature compliance reveals new domain-range combinations for existing predicates. These results point to predicate normalization and the validation of newly observed relation signatures as key refinement steps for industrial maintenance settings.

</details>


### [176] [Hierarchical Group-Conditional Conformal Risk Control for Selective Prediction in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24562)
*Murilo Salem,Luísa Böhm,Daniel Pontes,Anderson Ferrugem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了HG-CRC框架，通过层次化分组条件控制解决传统CRC在子群体风险保障上的失效问题，在ARC数据集上实现了零违反率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型服务于异构群体，但传统保形风险控制(CRC)仅提供边际风险保证，无法保证各子群体的风险约束，导致在群体分布变化时频繁违反预算约束（47%的试验中出现违规）。

Method: HG-CRC是一种事后校准框架，在用户定义的群体层次结构上同时执行风险保证。采用Bonferroni校正处理层次节点，并使用"叶节点优先"策略——优先使用最细粒度的阈值，当细粒度节点未认证或拒绝示例时回退到粗粒度节点。仅需预留校准集，无需重新训练模型。

Result: 在ARC Challenge上，高准确率模型(Qwen3-4B, Llama-3.1-8B)实现经验0%违反率和WGER=0；500次bootstrap试验表明这些零值为经验上界（真实率≤0.6%）。MMLU-Pro上表现因模型而异：完全弃权或保持低WGER。参与成本比全局CRC高22-37个百分点。消融实验表明层次深度对预算清零至关重要：移除难度层次后违反率回升至约11%。

Conclusion: HG-CRC成功解决了子群体风险保障问题，在保持理论保证的同时实现了良好的实证表现。层次结构设计对性能关键，Bonferroni校正虽对理论保证必要，但在节点较少时实证影响有限。

Abstract: Large language models serve heterogeneous populations structured by domain, topic difficulty, and linguistic style. Conformal risk control (CRC) gives rigorous marginal risk guarantees for selective prediction with abstention, but marginal guarantees do not imply per-group ones: a model can meet the population budget while systematically over-exposing subgroups to errors. Under mild shift in group composition, standard CRC violates the budget in up to 47% of trials.
  We propose HG-CRC (Hierarchical Group-Conditional CRC), a post-hoc calibration framework enforcing simultaneous risk guarantees across all nodes of a user-defined group hierarchy. It applies a Bonferroni correction over nodes and a leaf-first policy that uses the most specific applicable threshold, falling back to coarser nodes when a finer one is uncertified or rejects the example. It needs only a held-out calibration set, with no retraining.
  We evaluate on three models (Qwen3-4B, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, Gemma-3-4B) and two benchmarks (ARC Challenge, MMLU-Pro) across eight configurations probing IID generalization, heterogeneity, mixture/domain/prompt/difficulty shift, label noise, and quantization. Main result: HG-CRC reaches an empirical 0% violation rate and WGER=0 on ARC Challenge for high-accuracy models (Qwen3-4B, Llama-3.1-8B). At 500 bootstrap trials these zeros are empirical upper bounds (true rate up to 0.6%), not certified. Results are benchmark-specific: on MMLU-Pro these models abstain entirely or (Llama) retain WGER=0.014. Gemma-3-4B, poorly calibrated here, degrades gracefully by abstaining. Participation cost vs. global CRC is 22 to 37 points. Ablations show hierarchical depth clears the budget: removing difficulty level returns violations to about 11%. Bonferroni is needed for the theoretical guarantee, though its empirical effect matters only with many nodes.

</details>


### [177] [TRACE-CTI: Auditable Post-Extraction Governance of TTP Claims with Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24563)
*Federico Valletta,Giacomo Longo,Enrico Russo,Alessio Merlo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACE-CTI是一个CTI提取声明的治理框架，通过保留证据、溯源和验证历史，实现了从低精度(25.3%)到高精度(90.6%)的可信映射筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心日益依赖自动化将网络威胁情报报告映射到MITRE ATT&CK，但提取器输出存在错误且往往缺少证据、溯源和验证历史，导致难以判断单个映射是否可信。

Method: 提出TRACE-CTI框架：保留运行级预测，聚合为配置级图断言，将去重佐证具体化为共识断言，仅暴露符合策略验证依据的图断言。框架保留原生证据粒度、完整提取溯源、版本化信任决策和非破坏性撤销历史。在65篇报告、5,303句子的两个公开CTI语料库上评估，使用2×3检索器-生成器矩阵，增量摄入六个图版本。

Result: 所有配置无需模式修改即可纳入；溯源路径完整，操作范围互斥。跨生成器族配置对比同族配置展现更大输出多样性。将配置支持从k≥1提升到六配置一致时，金标准对齐精度从25.3%升至90.6%，召回率从88.2%降至16.3%。图可直接回答关于溯源、信任、版本、依赖、分歧和审查队列的七个问题，而最小化扁平输出无法完整回答。

Conclusion: 结果支持对提取的TTP声明进行显式、可审计的治理；观察到的佐证轨迹是描述性的，不确立统计独立性或因果模型族效应。

Abstract: Security Operations Centers increasingly rely on automated mapping of Cyber Threat Intelligence reports to MITRE ATT&CK, yet extractor outputs remain fallible and are often stored without the evidence, provenance, and validation history needed to decide whether an individual mapping should be trusted. We present TRACE- CTI, a post-extraction claim-governance framework that preserves run-level Predictions, aggregates them into configuration-level GraphAssertions, materializes setup-deduplicated corroboration as ConsensusAssertions, and exposes only GraphAssertions backed by policy-compliant validation grounds. The framework retains native evidence granularity, complete extraction provenance, versioned trust decisions, and non-destructive revocation history. We evaluate TRACE-CTI on two public CTI corpora comprising 65 reports and 5,303 sentences, using a controlled 2 x 3 matrix of retrievers and generator families, incrementally ingested across six GraphVersions. All setups are incorporated without schema modification; provenance paths remain complete, operational scopes remain disjoint, and every trusted GraphAssertion has an active qualifying validation ground. Cross-generator-family setup pairs exhibit greater output diversity than same-family pairs. At the final graph state, increasing setup support from k >= 1 to six-setup unanimity raises gold-aligned precision from 25.3% to 90.6%, while recall decreases from 88.2% to 16.3%. The graph also directly answers seven questions about provenance, trust, versioning, dependency, disagreement, and review-queue that the evaluated minimal flat output cannot fully answer without enrichment or reprocessing. These results support explicit, auditable governance of extracted TTP claims; the observed corroboration trajectory is descriptive and does not establish statistical independence or a causal model-family effect.

</details>


### [178] [DSCH-Loss: A Dynamic Semantic Channel Objective for Deep Semantic Hashing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24567)
*Tobias J. Bauer,Christian Riess,Daniel Loebenberger,Christian Bergler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出动态语义通道哈希（DSCH）方法，通过动态调整语义通道大小和位置避免损失景观不连续性，在40个任务中35个达到最优，mAP提升最高达1.75个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预定义语义通道的损失函数存在损失景观不连续性问题，导致优化困难；同时传统评估指标未考虑哈希码距离离散性带来的检索排序歧义问题。

Method: 设计动态语义通道哈希（DSCH）损失函数，采用动态大小和位置的语义通道避免损失景观不连续性；引入tie-aware mAP作为评估指标解决样本检索排序歧义问题。

Result: 在两个流行数据集和两种模型架构上进行的40个跨模态和单模态检索任务中，DSCH在35个任务上取得显著更高的tie-aware mAP分数，覆盖四种哈希码长度，相比次优方法mAP提升最高达1.75个百分点。

Conclusion: DSCH方法有效解决了语义哈希中的损失景观不连续性问题，在跨模态和单模态检索任务中显著优于现有state-of-the-art方法，证明了动态语义通道设计的有效性。

Abstract: Semantic hashing methods for generating short binary hash codes that allow efficient approximate nearest neighbor search in high-dimensional data spaces have gained extensive consideration in recent years. Deep learning-based methods offer better semantic capturing capabilities than traditional approaches relying on manual feature engineering. Moreover, they enable a data-driven approach to semantic hashing across diverse data modalities, yielding high-quality cross-modal hash codes within a shared Hamming space. Previous work investigated the properties of this Hamming space and introduced a loss function based on predefined so-called semantic channels with fixed width and Hamming distances derived from label similarities. However, this formulation also introduced discontinuities into the loss landscape, complicating optimization. Based on these observations, we propose a newly designed loss function, Dynamic Semantic Channel Hashing (DSCH), using dynamically sized and positioned semantic channels in order to avoid loss landscape discontinuities. Furthermore, we endorse the use of tie-aware Mean Average Precision (mAP) as evaluation metric as it addresses the ambiguity in sample retrieval ordering, which emerges from the discreteness of hash code distances. Finally, multiple experimental settings conducted on two popular datasets and incorporating two different model architectures provide strong evidence that training using the DSCH objective outperforms training using other state-of-the-art loss functions. In a total of 35 out of 40 cross-modal and intra-modal retrieval tasks, models trained with DSCH achieve significantly higher tie-aware mAP scores across all four tested hash code lengths, showing compelling results across model architecture and used dataset. The mAP score uplifts are consistent and amount up to 1.75 percentage points compared to the respective second best.

</details>


### [179] [LLM-SoccerArena: Benchmarking LLMs on Real-World Predictions in Sports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24573)
*Jonas Schröder,Jonas Schweisthal,Oliver Müller,Markus Weinmann,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LLM-SoccerArena，一个面向未解决事件的前瞻性实时基准测试平台，用于评估LLM预测真实体育赛事结果的能力，并通过2026年世界杯的大规模评估提供了关于LLM预测性能的新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试通常是静态和回顾性的，无法测试LLM在不确定性下综合信息预测未来事件的能力。由于LLM越来越多地支持关于不确定未来事件的决策，因此需要一种能够评估LLM预测真实世界结果能力的方法。

Method: 引入LLM-SoccerArena前瞻性实时基准测试平台，包含三个核心组件：（1）前瞻性实时基准测试协议；（2）公开开源平台；（3）因子基准测试设计及赛事相关问题。平台自动记录带时间戳、经验证的对未解决事件的预测，以及提示、模型版本、工具追踪和成本。因子设计沿四个维度变化：模型版本、信息访问权限、提示策略和预测时间跨度。

Result: 通过对2026年FIFA世界杯的大规模评估（七个LLM对所有104场比赛和15个赛事相关问题进行预测），发现具有网络访问权限的LLM表现优于无网络访问权限的模型，但优势较小（Brier评分仅提高0.023）。平台提供了对信息访问、提示策略和预测时间跨度等维度的详细模型性能分析。

Conclusion: LLM-SoccerArena为未解决事件的前瞻性基准测试提供了灵活、开源的平台，为最先进LLM的预测性能提供了新的证据。平台将持续更新，可直接应用于未来的国家和国际赛事及联赛比赛。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly support decisions about uncertain future events, yet evaluating their ability to forecast real-world outcomes remains difficult. In particular, existing benchmarks are typically static and retrospective, and therefore cannot test how information is synthesized by LLMs to predict future events under uncertainty. We introduce LLM-SoccerArena (https://llm-soccerarena.com), a prospective live benchmark that evaluates how well LLMs forecast real-world sports events before the outcomes are known. LLM-SoccerArena provides (1) a prospective live benchmark protocol, (2) a public open-source platform, and (3) a factorial benchmark design together with tournament-related questions (e.g., which team will win). LLM-SoccerArena automatically records timestamped, schema-validated forecasts of unresolved events, together with prompts, model versions, tool traces, and costs. The factorial design varies along four dimensions: (1) model version (e.g., GPT-5.5, Claude Opus 4.8); (2) information access; (3) prompting strategy, and (4) forecast horizon. We demonstrate LLM-SoccerArena through a large-scale evaluation of the 2026 FIFA World Cup, in which seven LLMs generated forecasts for all 104 matches and 15 tournament-related questions. We provide a detailed analysis of model performance across information access, prompting strategy, and forecast horizon. As a result, LLM-SoccerArena provides new evidence about the forecasting performance of state-of-the-art LLMs. For example, LLMs with web access outperform those without, but only by a small margin (i.e., a 0.023 improvement in Brier score). Overall, LLM-SoccerArena provides a flexible, open-source platform for prospective benchmarking of unresolved events. LLM-SoccerArena will be continuously updated, and can be directly applied to future national and international tournaments and league competitions.

</details>


### [180] [SIREN: Towards End-to-End Extreme-Weather Early Warning with Experience-Grounded LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24588)
*Hang Ni,Weijia Zhang,Fan Liu,Mengqian Lu,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了SIREN，一个基于历史案例经验的LLM智能体框架，用于自动化端到端的极端天气预警，在包含19个任务的SIREN-Bench基准测试中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的极端天气预警工作流程依赖专家，成本高昂、劳动密集且难以扩展；而当前的LLM智能体研究仅关注孤立的科学任务，忽视了实际预警过程中相互依赖的任务链。

Method: 1. 构建SIREN-Bench基准，包含600个问答实例，覆盖19个任务、4个独立预警程序和端到端预警链；\n2. 提出SIREN框架，集成异构天气证据和工具的智能体执行环境；\n3. 设计通过检索、技能蒸馏和预测建模利用历史案例的智能体套件。

Result: 实验表明现有天气智能体框架在SIREN-Bench上存在显著能力差距；SIREN在独立预警程序和端到端预警链任务上均显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: SIREN成功实现了自动化端到端极端天气预警，填补了现有研究在实际预警流程中的空白，展示了LLM智能体在复杂天气预警任务中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Early warning of extreme weather is essential for mitigating the societal, economic, and environmental risks posed by hazardous weather events. However, expert-centered warning workflows are costly, labor-intensive, and difficult to scale throughout the warning-to-action process. Although recent advances in Large Language Model (LLM) agents have enabled the automation of weather-related tasks, existing studies remain centered on isolated scientific tasks and overlook the chain of interdependent processes required for operational extreme-weather early warning. To bridge this gap, this study investigates automated end-to-end extreme-weather early warning through LLM agents. We first develop SIREN-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 600 question-answer instances across 19 tasks, and covering four individual warning procedures and an end-to-end warning chain. Evaluation on SIREN-Bench reveals substantial capability gaps in existing weather agent frameworks. This motivates us to develop SIREN, an experience-grounded agent framework inspired by experts' use of historical cases, which combines an agentic execution environment integrating heterogeneous weather evidence and tools with a family of agent harnesses that exploit historical cases through retrieval, skill distillation, and predictive modeling. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SIREN outperforms weather-agent baselines on both individual warning procedures and end-to-end warning chains.

</details>


### [181] [Artificial Intelligence and Innovation Ecosystem: Evolutionary Developments, Challenges, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24589)
*Zhimin Zhang,Chengzhen Ma,Jia Chai,Rongxin Zhan,Huansheng Ning,Lingfeng Mao,Dan Zhang,Suiping Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入人工智能创新生态系统(AIIE)概念，从物理空间、社会空间和思维空间分析AI对创新生态系统的影响，并通过演化视角验证其可行性，为未来研究提供新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创新生态系统的发展为经济一体化和协同进步提供了新范式，而人工智能的快速发展加速了数字化、信息化和智能化进程。探索AI如何利用其内在特性影响创新生态系统的发展轨迹，成为值得深入研究的重要课题。

Method: 首先综合创新生态系统的概念框架，将其分解为物理空间、社会空间和思维空间的体现；从空间视角引入人工智能创新生态系统(AIIE)概念，探讨AI赋予创新生态系统的特性；采用演化视角分析AI在不同AIIE发展阶段所扮演的角色；通过企业发展案例验证AIIE定义的可行性、有效性和合理性。

Result: 成功定义并验证了人工智能创新生态系统(AIIE)概念的可行性和合理性，明确了AI在创新生态系统中的独特贡献及其在不同发展阶段的作用，为理解AI与创新生态系统的融合提供了理论框架。

Conclusion: 在承认AI内在局限性的基础上，从四个视角分析了AIIE未来面临的潜在挑战，为人工智能创新生态系统的进一步发展指明了新的研究方向，填补了该领域的研究空白。

Abstract: The development of the Innovative Ecosystem (IE) presents a new paradigm for economic integration, collaborative advancement, and shared achievements. The rise of Artificial Intelligence (AI) has significantly accelerated the global processes of digitization, informatization, and intelligence. Exploring how AI can leverage inherent characteristics to influence the development trajectory of IE is a topic that warrants further investigation. Given AI's increasing prominence and role within IE, the paper analyzes this new form, examining both AI's unique contributions to IE and its potential challenges. Firstly, the paper synthesizes the conceptual frameworks surrounding IE, decomposing them into manifestations in physical, social, and thinking spaces. Furthermore, the concept of Artificial Intelligence IE (AIIE) is introduced from a spatial perspective, with an exploration of the characteristics AI contributes to IE. Subsequently, the paper employs an evolutionary perspective to analyze the roles provided by AI during different development periods of AIIE. The paper then verifies the feasibility, effectiveness, and rationality of the AIIE's definition and analyzes AIIE development from an evolutionary perspective using enterprise development examples. Finally, acknowledging AI's inherent limitations, the paper examines potential challenges facing AIIE in the future from four perspectives, aiming to identify new research avenues for the further development of AIIE.

</details>


### [182] [Efficiency Matters in Autonomous Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24647)
*Haiqian Yang,Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出评估AI自主研究系统的新维度——搜索效率，引入AUC指标和流体搜索方法，实现接近 oracle 性能的自适应搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI自主研究系统评估过于关注最终结果质量，忽视了搜索效率。随着AR从低成本验证领域（数学、编程）扩展到需要昂贵物理实验的现实科研场景，以最小预算达成高质量结果变得至关重要。

Method: 1. 提出使用帕累托前沿曲线下面积（AUC）作为搜索效率评估指标，与传统最终结果质量相结合；2. 系统比较爬山法、束搜索、树搜索、进化搜索等多种搜索算法在12个系统优化任务上的表现；3. 引入流体搜索（fluid search）方法，使用portfolio bandit算法动态分配固定评估预算给搜索过程森林。

Result: 1. 没有单一搜索结构在所有任务中保持最高效率；2. 搜索效率与最终结果质量是两个独立的性能维度，最终表现最好的方法可能在搜索过程中消耗更多预算；3. 流体搜索在所有任务上达到最高整体搜索效率，性能接近于预先知道每个任务最佳搜索结构的 oracle 方法。

Conclusion: 搜索效率应作为AR系统评估的重要维度，搜索效率与结果质量是可分离的性能指标。自适应的流体搜索方法能实现接近oracle性能的搜索效率，为AR系统的高效求解提供了新思路。

Abstract: AI-driven autonomous research (AR) systems are becoming increasingly effective across a broad range of tasks. Their performance, however, is still evaluated primarily by the quality of the final outcome. In this paper, we argue that the efficiency of the solution-search process is an equally important but often overlooked dimension of performance. A strong AR system should not only produce high-quality results, but also reach them with as small a budget as possible. Search efficiency will become increasingly important as AR expands from domains with inexpensive verification, such as mathematics and coding, to real-world scientific settings in which solution evaluation may require costly physical experiments. To capture this dimension, we propose evaluating AR systems using the area under the curve (AUC) of the Pareto frontier, alongside final outcome quality. We compare several families of search algorithms, including hill climbing, beam search, tree search, and evolutionary search, across twelve systems-optimization tasks. We find that no single search structure is consistently the most efficient. We also show that search efficiency and final outcome quality are distinct performance dimensions: a method that eventually achieves the best result may nevertheless improve slowly and consume substantially more evaluation budget before reaching that result. Because the most effective search policy is generally unknown in advance, we introduce an adaptive procedure called fluid search, which uses a portfolio bandit to dynamically allocate a fixed evaluation budget across a forest of search processes. Across the evaluated tasks, fluid search achieves the highest overall search efficiency, closely matching the performance of a per-task oracle that is given the best search structure for each task in advance.

</details>


### [183] [Reason-Mediated Behavioral Models for Auditing LLM Social Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24649)
*Atharva Pandey,Gautam Jajoo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新的社会模拟器评估框架，通过对比人类和LLM生成的理由状态，发现LLM虽然能匹配最终结果，但其推理过程往往不准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型语言模型社会模拟器评估仅关注模拟结果是否匹配人类结果，这过于宽松。一个模拟器可能匹配最终答案但使用错误的推理模式，因此需要评估模拟器的推理过程是否与人类一致。

Method: 1. 进行94人防晒霜概念测试，收集受访者对三个产品概念的评估和开放式理由；2. 将理由映射为有符号的理由状态Z（正号支持采用，负号阻碍采用）；3. 验证人类理由是否有助于预测购买意愿；4. 测试LLM能否在未见人类理由或结果的情况下模拟相同的理由状态。

Result: 人类理由显著提升了对购买意愿的样本外预测能力。然而LLM模拟的理由较为脆弱：它们听起来合理，但经常复述概念板内容，而非恢复受访者的接受或拒绝路径。

Conclusion: 提出了一个社会模拟器的评估框架。理由状态本身虽不能识别自然因果效应，但提供了一个可解释的测试方法，用于判断模拟器的陈述理由是否与人类证据一致。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as social simulators, including as synthetic survey respondents. Most evaluations ask whether simulated outcomes resemble human outcomes. We argue that this is necessary but too weak: a simulator can match the final answer while using the wrong rationale-derived reason pattern. We study this problem through a 94-person sunscreen concept test in which each respondent evaluated three product concepts and wrote open-ended rationales. We map those rationales into signed reason states $Z$, where positive signs support adoption and negative signs block it. This gives a practical audit: holding respondent descriptors $D$, category context $K$, and concept treatment $X$ fixed, do human rationale-derived reasons help predict behavior $Y$, and can an LLM simulate the same reason state without seeing the human rationale or outcome? Human rationale-derived reasons substantially improve held-out prediction of purchase intent. LLM-simulated reasons are more brittle: they often sound plausible, but frequently echo the concept board rather than recover the respondent's acceptance or rejection path. The paper contributes an evaluation framework for social simulators. Reason states do not identify natural causal effects by themselves, but they provide an interpretable test of whether a simulator's stated reasons align with human evidence.

</details>


### [184] [Eviction as Estimation: A Fixed-Lag Smoothing View of Test-Time Memory, and When Measuring Beats Accumulating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24667)
*Maruthi Vemula,Neeraj Praneeth Gajula*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了固定滞后平滑（fixed-lag smoothing）方法来管理语言模型的工作记忆，通过测量“已证实效用”（demonstrated utility）来决定保留哪些记忆项。该方法在控制性实验中表现优异，但在自然文本基准测试中与现有方法性能相当，主要因为自然文本中模型对大多数token预测正确，导致该方法退化为传统的累积注意力方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型工作记忆管理方法（如StreamingLLM、H2O、SnapKV）都存在局限性：它们要么在item到达时立即决策（基于过去信息），要么基于对未来使用情况的猜测。Belady的离线最优算法虽然理想，但需要知道完整的未来信息，在实际中不可用。作者希望找到一个介于在线决策和离线最优之间的中间方案，通过有限步延迟来获得更好的决策质量。

Method: 作者将记忆项保留问题重构为隐藏信号的估计问题，引入commit lag参数H来刻画不同方法：在线滤波器和预测器在H=0时提交决策，Belady离线最优方法则拥有完整未来信息。作者提出固定滞后平滑方法，等待有限步观察哪些items在未来被实际使用，然后基于“已证实效用”（demonstrated utility）进行决策。具体实现为RMM（训练无关策略），是H2O的严格泛化形式，当测量均匀时恰好退化为H2O。该方法将Belady不可观测的未来请求转化为可从模型自身读取的信息。

Result: 在控制性实验环境中，当重用是内生且时间分离时，已证实效用方法能比累积注意力更好地识别有用记忆，有限内存能表现出接近大内存的性能。然而在NVIDIA KVPress框架下的第三方基准测试中，与SnapKV、H2O、StreamingLLM相比：在单轮问答任务上RMM与H2O性能相当，在流式多轮设置中RMM输给了H2O和SnapKV。原因在于：自然文本中模型对大多数token预测正确，按正确性加权注意力几乎不改变结果，除非重用模式清晰且内生，而标准基准测试不涉及这种情况。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献不在于提出新的最优方法，而是提供了一个理论框架和对测量方法何时优于累积方法的清晰认识。已证实效用方法的有效性高度依赖于数据特性：当存在清晰的内生重用模式时显著优于传统方法，但在自然文本场景下优势基本消失。这揭示了记忆管理方法设计需要考虑具体应用场景的数据特征，而非追求通用的最优解。

Abstract: A language model with a bounded working memory must repeatedly decide which stored items to keep. Every deployed method decides the moment an item arrives, from the past (StreamingLLM, H2O) or from a guess about the future (SnapKV). We recast the choice as an estimation problem on a hidden signal, whether an item will be reused, placing existing methods on one axis, the commit lag $H$: online filters and learned predictors commit at $H=0$, while Belady's offline optimum sits where the whole future is known. The missing regime in between, fixed-lag smoothing, waits a bounded number of steps, observes which items a correct near-future prediction attended to, and only then commits. This measurement, demonstrated utility, turns Belady's unobservable future request into something we read off the model itself. We instantiate it as a training-free policy, RMM, a strict generalization of H2O that reduces to it exactly when the measurement is uniform. In controlled settings where reuse is endogenous and separated in time, demonstrated utility identifies used memory far better than accumulated attention, and a small bounded memory behaves like a much larger one. But on independent third-party benchmarks, run inside NVIDIA's KVPress harness against its own SnapKV, H2O, and StreamingLLM implementations, the advantage mostly disappears: RMM is on par with H2O for single-turn question answering and loses to both H2O and SnapKV in a streaming multi-turn setting. The cause is simple: on natural text the model is correct about most tokens, so weighting attention by correctness barely changes it, and demonstrated utility collapses onto accumulated attention unless reuse is sharp and endogenous, which standard benchmarks do not exercise. Our contribution is the framework and an honest map of when measuring beats accumulating, not a new state of the art.

</details>


### [185] [ERUnderstand: Evaluating Vision-Language Models on Structured ER Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24707)
*Ali Ansari,Yasmin Mohammadi,Farnoush Nili,Parsa Esmaeilkhani,Longin Jan Latecki,Eduard Dragut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发布了首个大规模ER图结构化理解基准ERUnderstand，包含2,960个图表。评估显示VLM在基础元素表现良好（F1>0.74），但在弱实体、多值属性等复杂元素上性能急剧下降。推理增强模型提升15-25%但仍受复杂度影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实体关系图(ERD)是概念数据库设计的核心，但通常仅以渲染图像形式存在而非机器可读模式，这限制了AI辅助数据库工程的发展。缺乏标准化的大规模基准来评估视觉语言模型对ER图的结构化理解能力。

Method: 构建ERUnderstand基准，包含2,960个ER图，来源于教育资料、真实模式和合成示例，覆盖多种领域、符号体系、复杂度级别和扩展实体关系(EER)结构。每个图表配对标准化的机器可读表示，用于细粒度评估模式元素。系统评估最先进的视觉语言模型(VLMs)并分析推理增强模型的效果。

Result: 常见ERD元素恢复可靠（F1>0.74），但在弱实体上性能急剧下降（F1最低0.28），多值属性（0.14 F1）和N元关系（0.07 F1）表现很差。推理增强模型整体性能提升15-25%，但仍对语言先验和图表复杂度增加敏感。

Conclusion: ERUnderstand提供了评估概念数据库模式多模态理解的标准化基准，揭示了当前VLM在复杂ER元素理解上的显著局限性。基准、数据集、评估工具包和生成代码已公开，为未来研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Entity-Relationship Diagrams (ERDs) are central to conceptual database design, yet they are typically available only as rendered images rather than machine-readable schemas, limiting AI-assisted database engineering. We introduce ERUnderstand, the first large-scale benchmark for structured understanding of ER diagrams, comprising 2,960 diagrams collected from curated educational sources, real-world schemas, and synthetically generated examples spanning diverse domains, notations, complexity levels, and Extended Entity-Relationship (EER) constructs. Each diagram is paired with a standardized machine-readable representation for fine-grained evaluation of schema elements. Evaluating state-of-the-art Vision-Language Models (VLMs), we find that while common ERD elements are recovered reliably (F1 > 0.74), performance drops sharply on weak entities (as low as 0.28 F1), multivalued attributes (0.14 F1), and N-ary relationships (0.07 F1). Reasoning-augmented models improve overall performance by 15-25% but remain sensitive to linguistic priors and increasing diagram complexity. ERUnderstand provides a standardized benchmark for evaluating multimodal understanding of conceptual database schemas. The benchmark, dataset, evaluation toolkit, and generation code are publicly available at https://github.com/salinaria/ERUnderstand.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Fast Iterative Dual-Output-Aware LUT Mapping for Fracturable FPGA Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22756)
*Sheng Lu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种迭代式双输出感知LUT映射框架，集成到ABC映射器中，在延迟、面积流和精确面积优化流程中交替进行单输出切割选择和双输出匹配，相比vanilla ABC平均减少34.96%和23.39%的LUT面积，相比最佳已有方法实现15.8倍加速并进一步减少5%深度和1%面积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代FPGA采用可分割LUT，每个LUT可实现单个大输入函数或共享物理LUT站点内的两个小函数。传统技术映射流程通常在单输出LUT映射后才执行双输出打包，这阻碍了潜在的配对机会影响切割选择。

Method: 提出迭代双输出感知LUT映射框架，集成到Berkeley ABC映射器的延迟、面积流和精确面积优化流程中；交替进行单输出切割选择和有界双输出匹配；使用基于稀疏支持的索引高效生成候选对；针对参数化架构、依赖和输出特定时序约束进行验证；通过启发式评分匹配过程进行选择；通过兼容性感知切割成本调整将配对信息反馈到后续映射轮次；为保持时序精度，输入的物理联合仅用于架构合法性检查，每个输出保留自己的逻辑时序支持。

Result: 在EPFL组合基准测试套件上，在两个代表性架构模型下，相比vanilla ABC平均减少34.96%和23.39%的LUT面积指标；与最佳已报告方法相比，实现15.8倍加速，同时进一步减少约5%深度和1%面积。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架成功将双输出感知集成到LUT映射流程中，显著改善了面积和延迟性能，同时大幅提升了运行效率，证明了迭代式双输出映射方法的有效性。

Abstract: Modern field-programmable gate arrays employ fracturable lookup tables (LUTs), each of which can implement either a single large-input function or two smaller functions within a shared physical LUT site. Conventional technology-mapping flows typically perform dual-output packing only after single-output LUT mapping, thereby preventing potential pairing opportunities from influencing cut selection. This paper presents an iterative dual-output-aware LUT-mapping framework integrated into the delay, area-flow, and exact-area optimization passes of the Berkeley ABC mapper. The proposed method alternates between single-output cut selection and bounded dual-output matching. Candidate pairs are efficiently generated using a sparse support-based index, validated against parameterized architectural, dependency, and output-specific timing constraints, and selected through a heuristic score-based matching procedure. The resulting partner information is then fed back into subsequent mapping rounds through a compatibility-aware cut-cost adjustment. To preserve timing accuracy, the physical union of the inputs is used only for architectural legality checking, while each output retains its own logical timing support. Experiments on the EPFL combinational benchmark suite under two representative architecture models demonstrate average reductions of 34.96% and 23.39% in the reported LUT-area metric relative to vanilla ABC. Compared with the best previously reported method, the proposed framework achieves a 15.8 times speedup while further reducing depth by approximately 5% and area by 1%.

</details>


### [187] [Benchmarking LLMs for Verilog Design Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22759)
*Angshuman Chakravertty,Rahul Koshti,Buddhi Prakash Sharma,Vinay Chamola*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可复现的基准测试平台，用于评估开源大语言模型在Verilog RTL生成任务中的表现，通过包含语法修复和形式验证的端到端流程，将代码语法有效性提升至70.43%，仿真通过率达到51.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型代码生成评估主要依赖于pass@k指标，缺乏对硬件描述语言（HDL）代码正确性和可综合性的端到端工具链验证，因此需要建立规范的基准测试平台来评估LLMs在Verilog RTL生成方面的能力。

Method: 构建了一个包含50个精心设计任务（组合逻辑、时序逻辑、有限状态机和混合设计）的可复现基准测试平台。评估流程包括约束提示、后处理和语义感知的迭代优化，通过Verilator编译、Icarus Verilog仿真、波形分析、形式等价验证以及基于抽象语法树（AST）的修复来验证生成代码。

Result: 在12个基准测试和1,610次运行中，评估了三个不同规模的开源模型（Llama-3-8B、StarCoder2-7B和TinyLlama-1.1B）。该流程将语法有效性从0%提升至平均70.43%，仿真通过率达到51.8%。值得注意的是，TinyLlama（1.1B参数）实现了最高的单模型语法有效性80.0%，其功能正确性与8B模型相当。

Conclusion: 该平台和数据集已开源，为硬件设计工作流中生成式AI的可复现评估提供了基础。研究表明通过适当的工具链验证和迭代优化流程，可以显著提升LLMs在Verilog代码生成方面的表现，即使是较小的模型也能取得优异成果。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show promise in code generation, but their capabilities to produce correct, synthesizable hardware description language (HDL) code still remain to be properly benchmarked. Existing evaluations are primarily relying on pass@k metrics and lack proper end-to-end toolchain validation. This paper presents a reproducible benchmarking platform that evaluates open-source LLMs on Verilog RTL generation across 50 curated tasks consisting of combinational, sequential, finite state machine (FSM), and mixed designs. The pipeline consisting of constrained prompting, post-processing, and semantic-aware iterative refinement with waveform analysis, formal equivalence verification, and Abstract Syntax Tree (AST)-based repair validates the generated code via Verilator compilation and Icarus Verilog simulation. Across the 12 benchmarks and the 1,610 total runs evaluating three models of different sizes (Llama-3-8B, StarCoder2-7B, and TinyLlama-1.1B), the pipeline improved syntax validity from 0% to a 70.43% average and simulation pass rate to 51.8% across three open-source models. Most notably TinyLlama (1.1B parameters) achieved the highest individual syntax validity at 80.0%, with functional correctness comparable to the 8B model. The platform and dataset are open-source, enabling reproducible evaluation of generative AI for hardware design workflows.

</details>


### [188] [DRC-Aid: Design-Rule Correction via Agentic Framework utilizing Inference-Time Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22761)
*Anushka Mukherjee,Kang He,Kaushik Roy*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出DRC-Aid框架，通过验证引导的搜索自动化修复设计规则违规（DRVs），在FreePDK45布局上实现约92.5%的DRC清洁且LVS等效修复成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布局中设计规则违规（DRVs）的修复需要迭代执行几何编辑和验证循环，这是一个耗时且复杂的过程。现有方法缺乏自动化且效率低下，需要一种能够自动执行局部DRC修复并保证电气拓扑完整性的方法。

Method: 提出DRC-Aid闭环智能体框架，将局部DRC修复形式化为验证引导的搜索问题。确定性规则引擎将违规转换为有限的几何编辑菜单，大型语言模型（LLM）评估局部几何上下文选择编辑操作，采用预算约束的深度优先搜索和回溯策略。Calibre验证工具提供即时反馈确保几何合规性和电气拓扑完整性，全局记忆库防止循环重探索。

Result: 在FreePDK45布局测试中，DRC-Aid在约92.5%的情况下实现DRC清洁且LVS等效的修复，总违规减少率达约98%。与随机策略（54.4%）和确定性启发式策略（83.3%）相比，LLM选择策略表现最优，且在六个及以上违规的复杂案例中优势进一步扩大。

Conclusion: DRC-Aid成功实现了高成功率的自动化DRC修复，验证了将LLM与验证工具结合用于约束搜索空间的可行性。该方法有效平衡了修复效率与质量，为集成电路物理设计自动化提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Resolving Design Rule Violations (DRVs) in layouts entails an iterative loop of geometric edits and verification. We present DRC-Aid, a closed-loop agentic framework that automates local DRC repair by formulating it as verification-in-the-loop search. To constrain the combinatorial geometric repair space, a deterministic Rule Engine converts physical verification tool-reported violations into a bounded menu of geometric edits. An off-the-shelf Large Language Model (LLM) evaluates local geometric context to select edits from this menu, with budgeted depth-first search and backtracking. Immediate feedback from verification tools such as Calibre nmDRC/nmLVS enforces geometric compliance and guards against electrical-topology degradation, while a global Memory Bank prevents cyclic re-exploration. Evaluated on FreePDK45 layouts containing DRVs, DRC-Aid achieves DRC-clean, LVS-equivalent repairs in ~92.5% of cases with a ~98% total violation reduction, while residual cases yield partially repaired LVS-equivalent candidates. Under an identical search and verification infrastructure, LLM-based selection outperforms random (54.4%) and deterministic-heuristic (83.3%) policies, with the gap widening on cases with six or more violations.

</details>


### [189] [FusionML: Prefill, Not Decode - Mechanism and Boundaries of CPU+GPU Co-Execution on Unified-Memory Apple Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22785)
*Om Mohite*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 发现并修复了MLX框架调度器导致CPU+GPU并行失效的问题，实现了Transformer prefill阶段1.15-1.38倍加速，但decode阶段无法受益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Apple Silicon SoCs采用统一内存架构共享CPU、GPU和Neural Engine，理论上可通过跨单元分割单个算子来加速Transformer推理，但先前尝试均失败或产生精度混淆的结果，需要找出根本原因并实现真正的加速。

Method: 1) 诊断问题：发现MLX懒执行图调度器在跨流操作时会序列化执行，导致性能下降；2) 实现修复：引入急切物化边界恢复并发性；3) 设计系统：实现逐层、竞争感知的CPU+GPU行分割策略用于Transformer prefill阶段。

Result: 在5种芯片和3代Apple Silicon上验证：单个decoder block prefill加速1.15-1.38倍；完整32层模型保持相同加速比；Qwen2.5-7B实际模型首token时间加速1.18-1.25倍，输出完全一致且不影响decode吞吐量。同时发现：decode阶段无法受益；精度匹配的训练在各芯片上损失0.86-0.97倍；ANE因调度开销过大不适合层粒度操作；内存压力下运行时门控策略会产生自毁效应。

Conclusion: 通过修复框架级调度问题，成功在Apple Silicon上实现了CPU+GPU协同计算的真实加速，为统一内存架构下的异构计算提供了可行方案，同时明确了该方法的适用边界（仅适用于prefill，不适用于decode和训练）。

Abstract: Apple-Silicon SoCs share CPU, GPU, and Neural Engine over one unified memory system, raising the question of whether transformer inference can be accelerated by splitting single operators across units. Prior attempts, including our own, failed or produced precision-confounded wins. We identify the cause: MLX's lazy-graph scheduler \emph{serializes} cross-stream work whenever a CPU-stream operation consumes an unmaterialized GPU result inside one evaluation graph, so a row-split matmul that runs \x{1.38} faster with materialized inputs runs \x{0.66} slower than GPU-only inside a lazy graph; an eager materialization boundary restores concurrency (\x{1.34}). \sys{} implements a per-layer, contention-aware CPU+GPU row split for transformer prefill built on this fix. Evaluated across five chips and three Apple-Silicon generations, community-replicated, the split accelerates Llama-shaped decoder-block prefill by \x{1.15}--\x{1.38}, unchanged at full 32-block depth, and reaches \x{1.18}--\x{1.25} faster time-to-first-token on a real Qwen2.5-7B checkpoint served through stock MLX-LM, with token-identical outputs and unchanged decode throughput. We characterize the boundaries equally carefully: decode cannot benefit, bound by shared bandwidth co-execution does not add; precision-matched training loses \x{0.86}--\x{0.97} on all five chips; ANE dispatch overhead excludes it at layer granularity; and a no-regression runtime gate becomes self-defeating under memory pressure, where probing an alternative mode evicts the active mode's working set. Code, raw results, and generation transcripts are released.

</details>


### [190] [The Sparsity Tax: Weight Sparsity Trade-offs in Event-Driven SIMD and SIMT Neuromorphic Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22790)
*Mattias Westerink,Sameed Sohail,Berend-Jan van der Zwaag,Sabih Gerez,Amirreza Yousefzadeh*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文量化了神经形态计算中的"稀疏税"开销，比较了三种加速器设计在事件驱动推理中的性能，发现SIMT架构在高稀疏度下具有最佳的能耗和性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件驱动的神经形态推理通过仅在脉冲时更新神经元状态来利用激活稀疏性，但权重稀疏性引入了不规则的收集式更新操作，破坏了同步SIMD执行，产生了控制、元数据和内存活动方面的额外开销（即"稀疏税"），需要量化并解决这一问题。

Method: 在同一个神经形态核心中集成并比较三种紧密相关的加速器：(i)基线同步SIMD，(ii)位图门控的Sparse-SIMD（选择性地禁用通道但不压缩权重），(iii)SIMT风格设计（具有每个PE的地址生成和游程编码稀疏权重）。使用GF22FDX+工艺的RTL到门级流程和活动驱动的能耗估算，评估不同训练后剪枝级别下的事件驱动神经网络推理。

Result: 由于SRAM主导面积，核心总面积变化不显著；性能和能耗强烈依赖于稀疏性处理方式：SIMD和Sparse-SIMD展现出接近恒定的吞吐量，Sparse-SIMD因位图和密集存储开销仅实现有限的能耗节省，SIMT在高稀疏度下提供最强的能耗扩展性和显著的加速，但由于元数据读取、负载不均衡和稀疏无关阶段的影响，其增益呈亚线性特征。

Conclusion: 不同的稀疏性处理策略对神经形态加速器的性能和能耗有显著影响，SIMT架构在高稀疏度场景下最具优势，但仍受限于元数据开销和负载均衡问题，为神经形态计算架构设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Event-driven neuromorphic inference exploits activation sparsity by updating neuron state only on spikes. However, weight sparsity introduces irregular gather-style updates that undermine lockstep Single Instruction Multiple Data (SIMD) execution. We call the resulting overheads in control, metadata, and memory activity the sparsity tax. This paper quantifies that tax by comparing three closely related accelerators integrated into one neuromorphic core: (i) baseline lockstep SIMD, (ii) bitmap-gated Sparse-SIMD that selectively disables lanes without compressing weights, and (iii) a Single Instruction Multiple Threads (SIMT) style design with per-PE address generation and run-length coded sparse weights. Using an RTL-to-gates flow in GF22FDX+ and activity-driven energy estimation, we evaluate event-driven neural network inference across varying post-training pruning levels. Results show that total core area changes are insignificant because SRAM dominates area, while performance and energy strongly depend on how sparsity is handled: SIMD and Sparse-SIMD exhibit near-constant throughput, Sparse-SIMD achieves limited energy savings due to bitmap and dense-storage overheads, and SIMT provides the strongest energy scaling and substantial speedups at high sparsity, albeit with sublinear gains due to metadata reads, load imbalance, and sparsity-independent phases. The hardware code for the proposed architectures and experiments is publicly accessible for research purposes.

</details>


### [191] [Reducing Instruction-Fetch Energy in RISC-V for Embedded AI Processing via Dynamic and Static Loop Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22792)
*Wiebren Wijnstra,Sameed Sohail,Berend-Jan van der Zwaag,Sabih Gerez,Amirreza Yousefzadeh*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文在RISC-V处理器中集成动态与静态循环缓存架构，在LeNet-5推理任务中实现指令取指减少48.3%–83.3%、总能耗降低21.5%–35.5%，面积开销<0.2%，并已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式RISC-V处理器在边缘AI推理中广泛应用，能效是核心设计约束；实测显示SRAM指令取指占总能耗40%以上，成为主要能耗瓶颈。

Method: 在NEORV32 RISC-V处理器数据通路中集成两种循环缓存：动态循环缓存运行时自动检测并缓存短向后分支循环；静态循环缓存作为软件管理的热代码指令缓冲区，在启动时预加载任意指令块。两种设计在GlobalFoundries 22nm FDX+工艺（0.5V、250MHz）下实现并综合。

Result: 在LeNet-5卷积神经网络推理负载上评估：动态缓存减少指令取指48.3%、总能耗降低21.5%；静态缓存减少指令取指83.3%、总能耗降低35.5%。两种设计的面积开销均低于SoC总面积的0.2%。

Conclusion: 两种循环缓存架构均显著降低能耗且面积开销极小，静态缓存效果更优，为嵌入式RISC-V边缘AI推理提供了高效、低开销的能效优化方案，完整实现已开源。

Abstract: Embedded RISC-V processors are increasingly deployed for on-device AI inference at the edge, where energy efficiency is a primary design constraint. Instruction fetching from SRAM-based memory is a dominant source of energy consumption in these cores, accounting for over 40\% of total energy in our baseline measurements. This paper presents two loop cache architectures integrated into the datapath of a RISC-V processor: a dynamic loop cache that automatically detects and caches short backward-branch loops at runtime, and a static loop cache that functions as a software-managed hot-code instruction buffer, allowing preloading of arbitrary instruction blocks during the boot sequence. Both designs are implemented in the open-source NEORV32 RISC-V processor and evaluated on a LeNet-5 convolutional neural network inference workload, synthesized on GlobalFoundries 22nm FDX+ technology at 0.5\,V and 250\,MHz. The dynamic cache reduces instruction fetches by 48.3\% and total energy by 21.5\%, while the static cache achieves an 83.3\% fetch reduction and 35.5\% total energy savings. The area overhead of both designs remains below 0.2\% of the full SoC area. The complete implementation is open source.

</details>


### [192] [Multi-primitive in-memory computing for Monte Carlo tree search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22869)
*Tergel Molom-Ochir,Benjamin F. Morris,Yintao He,Archit Gajjar,Giacomo Pedretti,Hai Helen Li,Yiran Chen,Jim Ignowski,Aishwarya Natarajan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种内存计算（IMC）架构，通过阶段到原语分解方法实现了蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的高效硬件实现，在9x9围棋任务上仅消耗约60 mW功率，相比CPU提升96倍能效，相比H100 GPU提升65-2059倍能效，并达到欧洲围棋联合会的评级水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统处理器上运行的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）功耗高达55-300瓦，严重限制了其在边缘设备上的部署。虽然内存计算（IMC）在常规工作负载上能效优异，但此前被认为与不规则的多阶段算法不兼容。

Method: 提出阶段到原语分解方法，将每个算法阶段重新表述为硬件原生的IMC原语：选择、扩展、模拟和反向传播分别映射到内容寻址存储器（CAM）、组合逻辑、阻变随机存取存储器（RRAM）交叉阵列和静态随机存取存储器（SRAM），从而将整个搜索过程保持在芯片上。采用22nm工艺，使用已制造的RRAM阵列参数进行验证。

Result: 在9x9围棋任务上，IMC-MCTS仅消耗约60 mW功率，相比CPU实现96倍能效提升，相比H100 GPU实现65-2,059倍能效提升。性能达到开源围棋引擎（Pachi-UCT和Michi-C）的欧洲围棋联合会评级水平（在样本误差范围内）。同一硬件平台可运行四个AI领域的八个应用。

Conclusion: 证明了内存计算架构可以成功应用于不规则的多阶段算法（如MCTS），实现了显著的能效提升同时保持竞争力性能，为边缘AI决策系统提供了可行的硬件解决方案。

Abstract: Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS) enables artificial intelligence (AI) decision-making, but requires 55-300 W on conventional processors, limiting edge deployment. In-memory computing (IMC) is energy-efficient on regular workloads but has been considered incompatible with irregular multi-phase algorithms. We introduce phase-to-primitive decomposition, which reformulates each algorithmic phase as a hardware-native IMC primitive. Applied to MCTS, selection, expansion, rollout and backpropagation map to content-addressable memory, combinational logic, a resistive random-access memory (RRAM) crossbar and static random-access memory, keeping search on chip. At 22 nm with fabricated RRAM-array parameters, IMC-MCTS consumes ~60 mW for 9x9 Go, achieving 96x energy efficiency over a central processing unit (CPU) and 65x-2,059x over an H100 graphics processing unit (GPU). It reaches a European Go Federation rating within sample-size uncertainty of open-source Go engines (Pachi-UCT and Michi-C). The same substrate runs eight applications across four AI domains.

</details>


### [193] [Magnetic Tunnel Junctions for Timekeeping in Intermittent Computing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23000)
*Nikola Vuk Maruszewski,Jordan Athas,Allison Fleming,Christian Duffee,Eren Yildiz,Saad Ahmed,Yaman Sangar,Pedram Khalili,Josiah Hester*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了FLINT，一种基于磁性隧道结(MTJ)随机保持丢失的新型时间保持器，解决了无电池间歇系统的计时问题，在能耗、精度和稳定性方面显著优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无电池间歇系统长期运行时，电源故障会清除时间状态，导致传感、调度和协调出错。现有基于电容放电的时间推断方法存在三个关键问题：电容容量需随测量范围增大而增加、充放电循环导致电容随时间衰减、估计偏差随部署时间累积。

Method: 设计FLINT时间保持器，利用经过特殊设计的"损坏"磁性隧道结(MTJ)阵列的随机状态丢失来读取经过时间。通过器件几何结构固定衰减时间尺度，使读取能量独立于测量间隔，且不随器件老化漂移。基于21个 fabricated MTJ验证阵列模型，并通过实际设备轨迹驱动的仿真评估完整系统。

Result: 在超过15分钟的离线时间内实现10%以内的跟踪误差，仅消耗1.03 μJ能量，占用面积小于0.1 mm²。与现有工作相比：范围扩展9.2倍，能耗降低11倍。在一年部署期内，相比老化电容时钟减少16-52倍的调度误差。

Conclusion: FLINT成功实现了基于自旋电子器件的时间保持方案，突破了传统电容方法的能量-范围权衡限制，为无电池系统的长期可靠运行提供了稳定、精确且低功耗的时间基准解决方案。

Abstract: Batteryless intermittent systems run unattended for years, but power failures erase timekeeping state, corrupting sensing, scheduling, and coordination. State-of-the-art timekeepers infer elapsed time from capacitor discharge; however, the capacitor must be sized for the longest interval measured (so range, energy, and area grow together), and repeated charge-discharge cycling lowers capacitance over time, biasing every estimate further as the deployment ages. We present FLINT, a timekeeper that reads elapsed time from the stochastic retention loss of an array of "broken" Magnetic Tunnel Junctions (MTJs)---spintronic memory cells engineered to lose state predictably. Because the decay timescale is fixed by device geometry, the energy to read it is independent of the interval measured and does not drift with device age. We validate FLINT's array model against 21 fabricated MTJs, then evaluate the full timekeeper in real-device-trace-driven simulation, showing that it tracks over 15 minutes of off-time within 10% error while consuming only 1.03 $μJ$ and occupying under 0.1 $mm^2$---$9.2\times$ the range at $11\times$ lower energy than prior work. It extends to longer intervals at no added cost, and makes $16-52\times$ fewer scheduling errors than an aging capacitor clock over a one-year deployment.

</details>


### [194] [Decoding the Skew: Distribution-Aware MoE Inference with Adaptive Kernel Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23099)
*En-Ming Huang,An-Cheng Chang,Bai-Cheng Jeng,Shih-Hao Hung,H. T. Kung*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了DA-MoE框架，通过感知路由分布动态选择最优MoE融合内核，在DeepSeek-V3和Kimi K2上实现平均1.16X-1.29X的延迟优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE推理系统使用静态token计数桶选择融合内核，忽略了决定瓦片填充、内存复用和内核效率的逐专家路由分布特性。路由分布会动态变化，静态选择无法保证最优性能。

Method: 提出分布感知框架：(1)引入紧凑的有效专家度量指标；(2)采用基于Dirichlet的逆向建模过程生成可控路由分布进行系统性硬件研究；(3)设计DA-MoE运行时，将实时路由直方图匹配到离线调优的分布，无需CPU-GPU同步即可选择近优融合内核。

Result: 在HumanEval-X服务追踪上，DA-MoE在DeepSeek-V3上实现融合MoE延迟几何平均提升1.16X（峰值1.40X），在Kimi K2上提升1.29X（峰值1.56X）。最优融合内核随路由偏斜和token计数变化而改变。

Conclusion: 路由分布感知的内核选择策略能显著提升MoE推理性能。DA-MoE通过GPU驻留运行时实现了低开销的动态内核调度，为大模型MoE服务系统提供了有效的优化方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference consists of sparse expert GEMMs whose shapes vary with the runtime routing distribution. Existing serving systems typically select fused-MoE kernels using static token-count buckets, ignoring the per-expert routing distribution that determines tile padding, memory reuse, and kernel efficiency. We introduce a distribution-aware framework for modeling and benchmarking MoE inference. The framework combines the compact Effective Experts metric with a Dirichlet-based reverse-modeling procedure that generates controllable routing distributions for systematic hardware studies. Using it, we show that the best fused-MoE kernel changes with routing skew and token count. We further present DA-MoE, a GPU-resident kernel-dispatch runtime for NVIDIA GPUs that matches the live routing histogram to offline-tuned distributions and selects a near-optimal fused-MoE kernel without CPU--GPU synchronization. On HumanEval-X serving traces, DA-MoE improves geomean fused-MoE latency by 1.16X on DeepSeek-V3 and 1.29X on Kimi K2, with peak speedups of 1.40X and 1.56X.

</details>


### [195] [Characterizing Warp Divergence from Pascal to Blackwell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23402)
*Alpin Dale*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文通过实验验证了从Volta架构引入ITS以来，NVIDIA GPU处理warp divergence的行为在不同架构间保持稳定，尽管编译器层面的重聚合机制发生了显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自Volta架构引入独立线程调度（ITS）以来，普遍认为NVIDIA GPU以固定方式处理warp divergence，但这一假设缺乏系统性验证。作者旨在通过跨多代架构的实证研究，揭示ITS引入前后warp divergence处理的实际行为演变。

Method: 结合周期精确的微基准测试、硬件计数器和编译器生成SASS代码的静态分析，对比pre-ITS的Pascal架构与Ampere、Hopper及Blackwell架构，区分稳定行为与架构变化。通过控制性实验分析新的barrier类和指令机制。

Result: 发现所有测试架构中，divergent paths以线性方式序列化，遵循T(k)≈sk模型，无超线性重聚合惩罚；执行效率降至32/k，代价与occupancy无关，谓词执行可消除序列化成本。编译器重聚合机制发生重大变化：Pascal使用SSY/SYNC指令栈，后续架构使用barrier-register指令；超出直接后支配节点的延迟重聚合从Ampere的29例降至Blackwell的2例。Blackwell引入了新机制但多为静态编译器分类。

Conclusion: 尽管NVIDIA的控制流ISA和重聚合机制持续演进，warp divergence仍保持稳定可预测的性能成本模型，这一程序员可见的成本模型实际上早于ITS就已存在。

Abstract: Since Volta introduced Independent Thread Scheduling (ITS), NVIDIA GPUs have been widely assumed to handle warp divergence in a fixed manner. We test this assumption across Ampere, Hopper, and datacenter and consumer Blackwell GPUs, using pre-ITS Pascal as a baseline. Combining cycle-accurate microbenchmarks, hardware counters, and static analysis of compiler-generated SASS, we separate stable behavior from architectural change. Across all tested generations, divergent paths serialize linearly with the number of paths $k$, following $T(k) \approx sk$ with no super-linear reconvergence penalty. Warp execution efficiency falls as $32/k$, the penalty is independent of occupancy, and predication removes the serialization cost. The same behavior appears on Pascal, showing that this programmer-visible cost model predates ITS. The compiler-emitted reconvergence machinery, however, has changed substantially. Pascal uses a per-warp SSY/SYNC instruction stack, whereas later generations use barrier-register instructions. Deferred reconvergence beyond the immediate post-dominator falls from 29 cases on Ampere to 2 on Blackwell. Blackwell also introduces a two-tier convergence-barrier classification, uniform-branch instructions, and explicit partial-mask warp synchronization, none of which appear on Ampere or Hopper. Controlled bit-flip experiments indicate that the new barrier class is a static compiler classification with no observable runtime effect in our tests. Thus, divergence retains a stable and predictable performance cost even as NVIDIA's control-flow ISA and reconvergence mechanisms continue to evolve.

</details>


### [196] [CircuitWeave: Topology-Behavior Alignment for Executable Multimodal RTL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23523)
*Jiahao Feng,Haiyan Qin,Zhiwei Xie,Wang Kang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出CircuitWeave，一个基于契约融合的多模态RTL生成框架，通过分别从原理图和文本中提取拓扑和行为契约，解决现有文本生成RTL中结构关系隐含的问题，在VerilogEval-Human上pass@1达到46.60%，相比纯文本方法提升8.46个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纯文本LLM在从自然语言规范生成RTL代码时存在局限性：即使指定了接口和周期级行为，散文式描述仍会隐含连接性、寄存器边界和状态-输出关系。虽然原理图可以显式表达这些结构关系并与文本的行为约束互补，但简单的图像添加会产生融合挑战：直接多模态解码无法显式分离文本和原理图的证据角色，也无法在代码生成前明确缺失和冲突的约束。

Method: 本文提出CircuitWeave，一个契约中介的多模态框架，包含以下核心步骤：(1)从原理图提取拓扑契约，从文本提取行为契约；(2)融合这两种契约为电路契约，序列化对应关系、缺失证据和冲突；(3)仅基于契约生成RTL。训练采用联合目标函数，监督契约提取、序列化融合、契约条件RTL生成以及从参考RTL反向重构覆盖的契约字段。构建了5,000个可执行验证的包，每个包含规范、生成原理图、结构化契约、参考RTL和自检测试平台，使用训练集通过LoRA适配Qwen模型。

Result: 在VerilogEval-Human数据集上，CircuitWeave达到46.60% pass@1、61.49% pass@5和65.39% pass@10，相比相同适配检查点的纯文本方法分别提升8.46、5.85和2.57个百分点。在RTLLM数据集上，达到40.00%、48.00%和52.00%，在每个截止点都比适配的纯文本条件高出两个百分点。数据集已公开发布于HuggingFace。

Conclusion: CircuitWeave通过显式分离和融合文本与原理图的证据角色，有效解决了多模态RTL生成中的融合挑战，显著优于纯文本方法。这证明了基于契约的多模态融合策略在RTL生成任务中的有效性，为未来的硬件设计自动化研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Text-only LLMs generate RTL from natural-language specifications, but prose can leave connectivity, register boundaries, and state-output relations implicit even when interfaces and cycle-level behavior are specified. Schematics can make these structural relations explicit and thereby complement the behavioral constraints conveyed by text. Yet simply adding an image creates a fusion challenge: direct multimodal decoding does not explicitly separate the evidence roles of text and schematics or make missing and conflicting constraints explicit before code generation.We present CircuitWeave, a contract-mediated multimodal framework that extracts a topology contract from the schematic and a behavior contract from the text. It fuses these records into a circuit contract that serializes correspondences, missing evidence, and conflicts, then generates RTL only from this contract. A joint objective supervises both contracts, serialized fusion, contract-conditioned RTL generation, and reverse reconstruction of covered contract fields from reference RTL.We construct 5,000 executable-qualified packages, each containing a specification, generated schematic, structured contracts, reference RTL, and self-checking testbench, and use the training split to adapt Qwen with LoRA. On VerilogEval-Human, CircuitWeave reaches 46.60% pass@1, 61.49% pass@5, and 65.39% pass@10. These point estimates are 8.46, 5.85, and 2.57 percentage points above those of the same adapted checkpoint without the schematic. On RTLLM, it reaches 40.00%, 48.00%, and 52.00%, two percentage points above the adapted text-only condition at each cutoff.The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/fengjiahao0421/CircuitWeave.

</details>


### [197] [PIMID: A Full-System Simulator with Intricacy and Diversity for Processing-in-Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24196)
*Yuan He,Masaaki Kondo,Galen M. Shipman,Jered B. Dominguez-Trujillo,Shigeki Tomishima,Kazi Asifuzzaman*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了PIMID，一个全系统级PIM模拟器，支持多种内存技术、执行模型和层次结构配置，填补了现有模拟器覆盖范围有限的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PIM模拟器覆盖设计空间有限，通常仅支持单一内存技术、固定PE位置、单一执行模型且止步于设备边界，无法全面探索PIM设计空间。

Method: 开发了PIMID全系统模拟器，支持共享内存和消息传递两种执行模型，覆盖11种内存技术（7种DRAM标准、SRAM和3种非易失性存储器），PE可配置于从子阵列到逻辑芯片的任意层次，支持PE数量和核心模型精度的可配置化，并通过YAML插件接口实现引擎和模型的标准化扩展。

Result: 内存技术差异可导致超过一个数量级的执行时间变化；消息传递下的图遍历存在共享内存模型中不存在的集体通信瓶颈；共享内存卸载在HBM3上节能但由16核主机保持端到端时间优势；最佳主机主存不一定是最佳PIM基底。

Conclusion: PIMID作为统一工具填补了现有PIM模拟器空白，支持完整的端到端时间和能耗分析，为PIM设计空间探索提供了全面且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Processing-in-Memory addresses the memory wall by co-locating computation with memory, but because real PIM hardware remains scarce, simulation is the primary way to explore the PIM design space. Yet existing PIM simulators each cover only part of that space: they typically model a single memory technology, fix processing elements at one level of the memory hierarchy, support a single execution model, and stop at the device boundary. We therefore present PIMID, an execution- and trace-driven full-system simulator that closes these gaps in one tool. PIMID supports both the shared-memory and message-passing execution models, running annotated parallel code in OpenMP and MPI side by side across eleven memory technologies (seven DRAM standards, SRAM, and three non-volatile memories); it places PEs anywhere from subarrays to logic dies, sweeps PE count and core-model fidelity, and prices the in-memory network per technology from measured congestion. Its single-process host-device co-simulation resolves an end-to-end time and energy breakdown (host preparation, device compute, and explicit boundary charges) that device-only tools cannot produce. Across the resulting dual-execution-model dataset, PIMID shows that the memory technology alone moves execution time by more than an order of magnitude and that the best host main memory is not the best PIM substrate; that regular kernels scale superlinearly with PE count as in-memory bandwidth co-scales with compute; that graph traversal under message-passing hits a collective-communication wall absent under shared memory; and that at full-system scope the offload trades time for energy only on the bandwidth-class memory: shared-memory offload saves energy on HBM3 while a 16-core host keeps every end-to-end time win. PIMID's plugin interfaces let new engines and models be added through standardized YAML specifications as PIM technology evolves.

</details>


### [198] [DICE: Detailed Inter-Chiplet End-to-End PHY Modeling for Accurate Chiplet Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24221)
*Rashid Aligholipour,Stefanos kaxiras,Yuan Yao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出DICE，一个在gem5模拟器中实现的运行时PHY建模框架，用于准确捕获chiplet设计中片间链路的动态行为，解决了现有模拟基础设施使用过度简化的固定延迟模型的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着chiplet设计中片间链路带宽和布线密度的增加，链路越来越接近信号完整性极限，对噪声、串扰和信道损耗的敏感性增加，需要更强的链路级可靠性机制（如前向纠错FEC）。然而，现有模拟基础设施通常使用过度简化的固定延迟模型来近似片间链路，忽略了PHY固有的动态运行时依赖行为，包括信道条件变化、迭代解码器收敛、数据包重传和应用动态等，这些因素难以离线确定。研究表明，忽略这些效应会扭曲片间数据包级时序和高层性能指标（如IPC），导致模拟结果偏离实际趋势。

Method: 提出DICE框架，在gem5模拟器中实现原位模拟运行时PHY建模。DICE捕获端到端片间数据路径，包括：QC-LDPC（准循环低密度奇偶校验）编码/解码、PAM4（四电平脉冲幅度调制）调制、有损信道传输、基于LLR（对数似然比）的解调、自适应数据包重发，以及chiplet间的PHY级流控制。

Result: 证明了忽略PHY的动态效应会扭曲片间数据包级时序和高层次性能指标如IPC，导致模拟结果偏离实际趋势。DICE能够准确建模这些效应，提供更真实的片间链路行为模拟。

Conclusion: DICE为chiplet架构研究提供了更准确的模拟基础设施，能够捕获片间链路的真实动态行为，对于评估未来高性能chiplet设计的性能具有重要意义。

Abstract: Scaling monolithic multicores is increasingly constrained by power/thermal limits, yield, and rising manufacturing and testing costs. Chiplet designs address these challenges by partitioning large dies into smaller parts (typically multiple core-complex dies and an I/O die) linked via high-bandwidth physical fabrics (PHY). As bandwidth and wiring density scale, however, these short-reach links are pushed closer to their signal-integrity limits, increasing susceptibility to noise, crosstalk, and channel loss, motivating stronger link-level reliability mechanisms such as forward error correction (FEC). Despite this trend, state-of-the-art simulation infrastructures often approximate inter-chiplet links using oversimplified, fixed-latency models. Such abstractions overlook the inherently dynamic, runtime-dependent behavior of the PHY -- including channel conditions (e.g., signal-to-noise ratio shifts, signal crosstalk, clock jitter), iterative decoder convergence and packet retransmissions, and application dynamics (e.g., LLC-misses that travel across chiplet boundaries) -- all of which are hard to determine offline. We show that neglecting these effects distorts inter-chiplet packet-level timing and high-level performance metrics such as IPC, leading to off-trend simulation results. We present DICE, an in-simulation, runtime PHY modeling in gem5 that captures the end-to-end inter-chiplet datapath, including QC-LDPC encoding/decoding, PAM4 modulation, lossy-channel transmission, LLR-based demodulation, adaptive packet re-sending, and PHY-level flow control between chiplets.

</details>


### [199] [A Heterogeneous Neural Network Accelerator for End-to-End Multitask RF Signal Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24669)
*Zhifan Song,Haralampos-G. Stratigopoulos,Hassan Aboushady*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种异构神经网络加速器，用于多任务射频信号识别，在低延迟和低功耗下实现了高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在嵌入式和边缘设备上高效执行多任务射频信号识别，需要一种能够同时处理自动调制识别、硬件木马隐蔽信道检测和GNSS干扰分类的低延迟、低功耗硬件加速方案。

Method: 设计了一种紧凑的注意力增强卷积神经网络并结合LSDec（可学习流式抽取器）以支持自适应时间下采样和灵活输入长度；在硬件架构上集成双流水线融合卷积-池化引擎和基于DMA的流式传输以减少存储访问和延迟，并通过加速器与SIMD优化CPU内核的协同执行调度来降低资源占用并保持高性能与任务级灵活性。

Result: 在RadioML2018数据集上，信噪比≥4 dB时自动调制识别的平均准确率≥99%；在HT-CC数据集上准确率为90%；在GNSS-Jamming数据集上准确率为99.5%；加速器每帧的端到端推理延迟为98 μs。

Conclusion: 该系统在多任务射频信号识别中兼具高准确率与低延迟，适合低功耗、延迟敏感的嵌入式与边缘应用场景。

Abstract: This paper presents a heterogeneous neural network accelerator for multi-task RF signal recognition, supporting automatic modulation recognition (AMR), hardware-Trojan covert channel (HT-CC) detection, and GNSS jamming classification. We introduce a compact attention-enhanced convolutional neural network (CNN) combined with LSDec, a learnable streaming decimator that enables adaptive temporal downsampling and flexible input lengths. The hardware architecture integrates a novel dual-pipeline, fused convolution-pooling engine with DMA-based streaming to minimize memory traffic and latency. Co-execution scheduling on the accelerator and SIMD-optimized CPU kernels reduces hardware resource usage while preserving high performance and task-level flexibility. Across three datasets, the proposed system achieves $\geq$ 99% average accuracy above 4 dB Signal-to-Noise Ratios (SNRs) on the RadioML2018 dataset for AMR, 90% on the HT-CC dataset, and 99.5% on the GNSS-Jamming dataset. The accelerator sustains an end-to-end inference latency of 98 $μ$s per frame, demonstrating its effectiveness for low-power, latency-critical multi-task spectrum-intelligence applications on embedded and edge devices.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [Semalith v1.4: A Calibrated 184M Safety Classifier Achieving State-of-the-Art Prompt-Injection Detection at 44x Fewer Parameters than Llama-Guard-3-8B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22545)
*Tejasvi C. Addagada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Semalith v1.4是一个184M参数的DeBERTa-v3-base安全分类器，可在单次推理中同时处理提示注入、通用危害和金融监管合规三类安全任务。它在22个外部基准测试中，以44倍更少的参数量，在7/7的提示注入评估和11/18的总基准测试中击败Llama-Guard-3-8B，且在良性代理提示上达到零假阳性率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融服务和代理场景中部署大型语言模型需要安全分类器同时处理提示注入、监管合规和通用危害三类问题，而现有开源护栏模型均无法在单次推理中兼顾这三个方面。

Method: 采用184M参数的DeBERTa-v3-base架构，设计22类分类头（含BENIGN、9种提示注入子类型、通用危害、11个BFSI标签），并配有4类辅助超级类别头。在76,204行数据集上使用联合加权损失进行训练，数据来源于49个公开数据集，经SHA-1去重处理确保与评估集几乎零污染（21/22基准零污染）。

Result: 在22个外部基准测试上：提示注入评估全面优于Llama-Guard-3-8B（7胜），总基准11胜，参数量仅1/44；在208条良性代理提示上假阳性率为0.000（对比模型为0.063）。但在通用危害基准（WildGuardMix、HEx-PHI、HarmBench）上Llama-Guard-3-8B表现更优。文中披露了6个性能弱点。

Conclusion: 该模型填补了单次推理多轴安全分类的开源空白，在提示注入检测和低假阳性率方面优势明显。建议：对话审核场景使用v1.3（ToxicChat F1达0.624）；BFSI标签覆盖或零假阳性率优先级高时使用v1.4。

Abstract: Deploying large language models in financial-services and agentic settings requires safety classifiers that simultaneously handle prompt injection, regulatory compliance, and general harm, a combination no existing open guardrail addresses in a single inference pass.
  Semalith v1.4 is a 184M-parameter DeBERTa-v3-base classifier performing simultaneous three-axis safety classification including prompt injection, general harm, and financial-services regulatory compliance, in a single forward pass. Its 22-class head (BENIGN, nine prompt-injection sub-types, general-harm, eleven BFSI labels) is trained with a 4-class auxiliary super-category head under jointly weighted loss, on a 76,204-row corpus mined from 49 public sources with SHA-1 deduplication against every held-out evaluation set, with 21 of 22 benchmarks at zero contamination (max 0.22%).
  Against Llama-Guard-3-8B on 22 held-out benchmarks, Semalith v1.4 wins every prompt-injection evaluation (7/7) and 11 of 18 benchmarks overall at 44x fewer parameters, with FPR = 0.000 on 208 benign agentic prompts vs 0.063 for Llama-Guard-3-8B. On general-harm benchmarks (WildGuardMix, HEx-PHI, HarmBench), Llama-Guard-3 leads; this complementary split is documented in Section 4. Six measured weak spots are disclosed in Section 6.
  Deployment guidance: v1.3 is recommended for conversational moderation deployments (ToxicChat F1 0.624); v1.4 is recommended when BFSI label coverage or zero-FPR on benign agentic prompts is the priority.

</details>


### [201] [CORVUS: Context Optimization and Reduction Via Underlying Synchronization for LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22711)
*Mingwei Zheng,David OBrien,Siwei Cui,Pardis Pashakhanloo,Rajdeep Mukherjee,Myeongsoo Kim,Sachit Kuhar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CORVUS架构，通过解耦文件读取操作与观察结果来优化LLM编码代理的轨迹管理，显著减少输入令牌和推理周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编码代理采用追加式轨迹架构，将文件读取操作与观察结果紧密耦合，导致文件快照在编辑后过时，引发推理错误和冗余文件重读，造成轨迹膨胀。

Method: 提出CORVUS轨迹架构，通过维护相关文件的同步注册表并将文件读取操作与观察结果解耦，在每个推理周期仅注入文件的当前内容，确保轨迹与代码库状态保持同步。

Result: 在SWE-POLYBENCH_VERIFIED和SWE-BENCH PRO上评估，平均输入令牌减少9-50%，最终提示长度缩短15-32%，推理周期减少达37%，同时保持相当的通过率。

Conclusion: CORVUS架构有效解决了传统追加式轨迹的问题，通过结构化改进实现了更轻量、更高效的轨迹管理，为LLM编码代理提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM coding agents operate by constructing trajectories that accumulate reasoning, tool calls, and results to enable multi-step decision-making. However, the conventional append-only trajectory architecture found in practice tightly couples file-read actions with their observations, capturing snapshots that become permanently fixed in the chronological history. As files change through agent edits or concurrent human modifications, these snapshots become stale, causing reasoning errors and causing agents to redundantly re-read files, with each re-read appending yet another copy to the trajectory. To mitigate this, we propose CORVUS, a novel trajectory architecture that decouples file-read actions from their observations by maintaining a synchronized registry of relevant files and injecting only their current contents at each reasoning cycle. This structural change produces significantly lighter-weight trajectories that remain synchronized with the actual codebase state by construction, eliminating redundant file copies and stale snapshots that bloat conventional trajectories. We evaluated CORVUS on SWE- POLYBENCH_VERIFIED and SWE-BENCH PRO across four LLMs, achieving 9-50% reduction in average input tokens per task, 15-32% shorter final prompts, and up to 37% fewer reasoning cycles while maintaining comparable pass rates.

</details>


### [202] [CausalGate: Causal Importance Distillation for Transformer Module Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22720)
*Kiran Nair,Smriti Regmi,Rodrigue Rizk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CausalGate框架，通过因果干预识别Transformer冗余模块并转化为静态门控，在零运行时开销下实现LLM高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应推理方法依赖观测性启发式指标（如隐藏状态相似度或激活幅度）来丢弃冗余模块，但这些基于相关性的度量无法捕捉对语义准确性至关重要的微妙非线性结构计算，导致性能损失。

Method: CausalGate框架包含校准阶段和优化阶段：(1) 因果干预：隔离单个Attention和MLP子层，将其输出置零，通过最终logit分布的KL散度测量语义损伤；(2) 静态门控蒸馏：使用指数移动平均平滑目标配合可微分成对排序损失，将结构重要性层次蒸馏为全局静态标量门控，消除运行时路由开销。

Result: 在TinyLlama-1.1B、Qwen2.5-3B和Llama-3.1-8B上的语言建模和常识推理基准测试中，CausalGate持续优于主流动态路由和层跳过基线，成功将理论计算节省转化为实际的硬件延迟降低。

Conclusion: CausalGate通过因果干预驱动的模块重要性评估和零开销静态门控机制，有效解决了现有相关性启发式方法的局限性，为LLM高效推理提供了一种实用且高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing adaptive inference methods for Large Language Models rely on observational heuristics, such as hidden-state similarity or activation magnitudes, to drop redundant modules. However, these correlation-based metrics often fail to capture subtle, non-linear structural computations vital for semantic accuracy. We introduce CausalGate, an intervention-guided framework for compute-efficient transformer inference. During a calibration phase, CausalGate isolates individual Attention and MLP sub-layers, zeros out their respective outputs, and measures the exact semantic damage via the Kullback-Leibler divergence of the final logit distribution. To eliminate runtime routing overhead, this structural importance hierarchy is distilled into a global set of static, lightweight scalar gates using an Exponential Moving Average smoothing objective paired with a differentiable pairwise ranking loss. Evaluated on TinyLlama-1.1B, Qwen2.5-3B, and Llama-3.1-8B across language modeling and commonsense reasoning benchmarks, CausalGate consistently outperforms prominent dynamic routing and layer-skipping baselines, translating theoretical compute savings into concrete hardware latency reductions with zero operational overhead.

</details>


### [203] [Progress-conditioned Group Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22724)
*Kaibing Yang,Guangfeng Cai,Shengtian Yang,Shuo He,Yu Li,Mengyi Liu,Pengwei Chen,Jun Xu,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProGPO方法，通过在失败样本组中引入首次访问观测覆盖来解决群体策略优化中的信用陷阱问题，显著提升了LLM智能体在长视野任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体策略优化在训练大语言模型智能体时面临采样不平衡问题：在困难的长视野任务中，重复或低效动作主导了策略的高概率区域，而有效的状态改变动作采样不足。这导致大量全失败的轨迹组，无法提供正确的策略修正方向，形成自增强的信用陷阱。

Method: 提出Progress-conditioned Group Policy Optimization (ProGPO)方法：当组内所有样本的产出奖励为零时，采用首次访问观测覆盖作为补充信号，为访问更多新状态的轨迹或步骤分配更高的相对优势值，因为到达新观测是任务成功的前提条件。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop两个挑战性智能体基准测试上，使用Qwen2.5-1.5/7B-Instruct模型进行实验，ProGPO相对于群体基线方法获得持续改进，在困难任务上提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: ProGPO通过引入进度条件化的策略优化机制，有效打破了信用陷阱的循环，为长视野任务中失败样本组提供了有意义的优化方向，显著提升了LLM智能体的性能表现。

Abstract: Group-based policy optimization has been increasingly used to train large language model (LLM) agents from sparse outcome rewards by comparing trajectories or steps within a group. However, on difficult long-horizon tasks, this comparison can suffer from a sampling imbalance: repeated or low-effect actions dominate the high-probability region of the policy while useful state-changing actions remain under-sampled. This imbalance produces many all-failed rollout groups, where outcome rewards provide no direction for correcting the policy. Together, these effects can form a self-reinforcing credit trap: failure-dominated sampling yields no outcome-based correction, allowing repeated low-effect actions to persist. To break this loop, we propose Progress-conditioned Group Policy Optimization (ProGPO), which uses first-visit observation coverage only when all samples in a group receive zero outcome reward. Specifically, within such groups, ProGPO assigns higher relative advantages to trajectories or steps that visit more new states since reaching new observations is a prerequisite for task success. Experiments on two challenging agentic benchmarks, ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5/7B-Instruct, show that ProGPO consistently improves over group-based baselines, with particularly large gains on hard tasks.

</details>


### [204] [QFedPolyp: A Communication- and Inference-Efficient Federated Learning Framework for Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22743)
*Madan Baduwal,Priyanka Paudel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QFedPolyp框架，通过量化感知训练和低精度模型通信实现高效联邦学习息肉分割，在保护隐私的同时降低通信成本约4倍，推理速度提升1.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心化深度学习需要医院共享敏感医疗数据存在隐私风险；传统联邦学习虽保护隐私但通过传输全精度模型参数带来高通信成本。

Method: 结合量化感知训练与低精度模型通信，各医院在本地私有数据上训练轻量级U-Net并在训练过程中模拟量化，客户端传输量化后的模型参数至中央服务器进行重构和联邦平均聚合。

Result: 全精度联邦训练在Kvasir-SEG上Dice系数为0.910，在CVC-ClinicVideoDB上为0.930；8位统一量化通信降低传输成本约4倍且保持竞争力分割精度；量化模型推理速度比全精度模型快1.5倍。

Conclusion: QFedPolyp实现了隐私保护的协作式息肉分割，降低了通信开销并加快了推理速度，生成的轻量级模型适用于实时临床部署。

Abstract: Background and Objective: Automatic polyp segmentation supports computer-aided diagnosis and early colorectal cancer detec- tion. Centralized deep learning requires hospitals to share sensitive medical data, while federated learning preserves privacy but introduces high communication costs through repeated transmission of full-precision model parameters. We propose QFedPolyp, a communication- and inference-efficient federated learning framework for collaborative polyp segmentation.
  Methods: QFedPolyp combines quantization-aware training with low-precision model communication. Each hospital locally trains a lightweight U-Net on private data while simulating quantization during training. Clients transmit quantized model parameters to a central server, where they are reconstructed and aggregated using Federated Averaging. Evaluation is performed on Kvasir-SEG, CVC-ClinicVideoDB, PolypGen, and BKAI-IGH NeoPolyp.
  Results: Full-precision federated training achieves Dice scores of 0.910 on Kvasir-SEG and 0.930 on CVC-ClinicVideoDB. Uni- form 8-bit communication reduces transmission cost by approximately 4 times while preserving competitive segmentation accuracy. Quantized models also achieve up to 1.5 times faster inference than full-precision models.
  Conclusions: QFedPolyp enables privacy-preserving collaborative polyp segmentation with reduced communication overhead and faster inference. The resulting lightweight models are suitable for real-time clinical deployment.

</details>


### [205] [Learning to Access Computation: Accessibility Plasticity as a Principle of Adaptive Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22748)
*Zhaowen Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出“可及性可塑性”（Accessibility Plasticity）原则，通过调控已有计算单元间的交互与参与关系来适应环境，从而减少参数/能力修改，在序列学习任务中达到与基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络主要通过参数/结构修改进行适应，缺乏将“计算能力”与“计算可及性（关系可通达性）”视为独立自适应变量的视角；因此引入可及性作为新的适应维度，以更高效、低成本地实现适应性。

Method: 提出“可及性可塑性”原则：以关系型操作形式化，将系统适应分为优先调整现有计算单元间的可及性（交互与参与关系），再进行代价更高的能力与结构变更；在序列学习任务上进行概念验证。

Result: 在序列学习任务的验证中，优先进行可及性适应能够减少能力修改（如参数/结构变更），同时维持与基线方法相当的任务性能，表明可及性作为独立自适应维度具有有效性。

Conclusion: 可及性是神经网络适应的独立维度；将可及性修改优先于能力修改可提升适应效率，为未来动态神经网络（关系随环境演化）提供基础。

Abstract: Modern neural networks primarily adapt through parameter modification within predefined computational structures. While recent methods introduce modularity, conditional computation, and parameter-efficient adaptation, they generally do not distinguish computational capability from computational accessibility as separate adaptive variables. This work introduces Accessibility Plasticity, a principle of adaptive computation in which systems adapt not only by changing what computation exists, but also by reorganizing which existing computations can interact and participate. We formalize Accessibility Plasticity through a relationship-based operational realization and establish a reuse-first hierarchy of adaptation, where accessibility modification precedes more costly capability and structural changes. A proof-of-concept evaluation on sequential learning tasks shows that accessibility adaptation can reduce capability modification while maintaining comparable task performance. These results suggest accessibility as a distinct adaptive dimension and provide a foundation for future dynamic neural systems whose computational relationships evolve with changing environments.

</details>


### [206] [Hierarchical Grading in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22757)
*T. Shaska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了分级大语言模型（GLLMs）框架，通过在Transformer表示空间引入分级结构，在保持表达能力、计算复杂度和推理成本的同时，为标准Transformer提供了更优的参数配置方案，并证明了在分层目标上的显著性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer架构中所有参数被统一对待，缺乏对不同层级参数重要性的区分。本文旨在通过引入代数分级结构，为Transformer提供一个理论完备的框架，以识别和利用能够改善模型性能的参数配置，而非将标准架构视为唯一最优解。

Method: 将分级神经网络理论扩展到自回归语言模型，在Transformer的嵌入层、自注意力机制和训练目标中引入加权标量作用；利用几何不变量理论框架，通过Hilbert-Mumford准则判定最优分级方向；基于目标和数据分布的离线估计，通过凸优化求解最优分级参数；训练后将分级参数融入学习参数，编译为标准Transformer。

Result: 证明了分级架构在开凸锥中优于均匀架构，最优分级由两个矩映射的重合点给出；对于层级分层目标，证明了分级先验与均匀先验之间存在极小极大分离，在几何分层条件下，风险差异随层级数指数衰减；最优分级可在训练前通过离线估计求解。

Conclusion: GLLMs框架成功将分级理论应用于大语言模型，提供了超越标准Transformer的理论指导下的架构优化路径，同时保持了与标准Transformer的完全兼容性。几何不变量理论为理解和改进Transformer架构提供了新的数学视角。

Abstract: We introduce Graded Large Language Models (GLLMs), an algebraic framework that equips the representation space of a transformer with a grading and propagates the induced weighted scalar action through embeddings, self-attention, and the training objective. The construction extends the theory of graded neural networks and graded transformers to autoregressive language models while preserving expressive power, asymptotic computational complexity, and inference cost.
  The governing geometric picture is that of geometric invariant theory. The benefit of a grading is expressed by a Kempf--Ness functional on the grading torus; the grades that improve upon the uniform architecture form an open convex cone whose membership is decided by a Hilbert--Mumford-type criterion pairing a grade direction against two measurable profiles of the target and the data; the optimal grades are the coincidence point of two moment maps, given in closed form; and the ordinary transformer appears as a semistable isotropic point on the boundary of the cone: one member of a larger graded family rather than a distinguished optimum.
  Separately, for level-stratified targets we prove a minimax separation between the graded prior and its absence: over all estimators the risks of the graded and uniform target classes separate throughout an explicit window of sample sizes, by a factor that decays exponentially in the number of levels under geometric stratification. Both profiles are estimable offline, so the optimal grades solve a convex program certified before training begins. Because the grading is absorbed into the learned parameters after training, every GLLM compiles to a standard transformer of identical architecture and inference complexity.

</details>


### [207] [An Integrated Deep Learning and Statistical Framework for Whole-Network Gene--Environment Association with Leaf Vascular Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22763)
*Geran Zhao,Yangsheng Wang,Xiaotian Dai,Guifang Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合深度学习和统计学的集成框架，用于分析叶脉网络结构，通过EDTER模型提取全网络叶脉架构，并利用半参数稀疏典型相关分析(SSCCA)识别基因-地理互作，在杨树数据集中发现了3个与叶脉结构显著相关的基因-地理互作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基因-环境关联研究主要使用少量低维汇总特征来量化叶脉结构，丢弃了原始图像中包含的大部分结构信息，无法充分利用叶脉网络的复杂架构特征。

Method: 提出了四个方法论创新：(1)将完整叶脉架构表示为全网络图像表型；(2)微调基于深度学习的Edge Detection with Transformers (EDTER)模型，通过联合学习局部和全局上下文特征从RGB图像中准确提取全网络叶脉架构；(3)通过整合DiffusionEdge生成的边缘图和BSDS500构建新的标注叶脉图像数据库；(4)应用半参数稀疏典型相关分析(SSCCA)进行变量选择，并通过截断潜在高斯Copula模型处理稀疏、零膨胀的边缘图数据。

Result: 两个模拟研究验证了该框架在复杂度递增情况下的性能。应用于杨树真实数据集，识别出三个与叶脉架构显著相关的基因-地理互作因子，为叶脉结构的生物学机制提供了新见解。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了高维复杂图像表型的分析，为植物生物学研究提供了新的见解，建立了一个广泛适用的高维复杂图像表型方法论框架。

Abstract: Leaf veins exhibit remarkable diversity in architecture and patterning, yet existing gene--environment association studies have primarily quantified leaf venation using a small collection of low-dimensional summary traits, thereby discarding most of the structural information contained in the original images. We propose an integrated deep learning and statistical framework. The proposed framework achieves four methodological advances. First, it represents the complete leaf vascular architecture as a whole-network image phenotype. Second, it fine-tunes the deep learning-based Edge Detection with Transformers (EDTER) model to accurately extract whole-network leaf vascular architecture from RGB images by jointly learning local and global contextual features. Third, it constructs a new annotated leaf image database by integrating edge maps generated by DiffusionEdge with the Berkeley Segmentation Database (BSDS500). Fourth, it applies Semiparametric Sparse Canonical Correlation Analysis (SSCCA) to perform variable selection and model associations between repeatedly measured high-dimensional Bivariate image responses and high-dimensional predictors while simultaneously accommodating sparse, zero-inflated data represented by edge maps through a truncated latent Gaussian copula model. Two simulation studies demonstrate the performance of the proposed framework under increasing levels of complexity. Application to a real \emph{Populus} dataset identifies three significant gene--geography interactions associated with leaf vascular architecture, providing new biological insights and establishing a broadly applicable methodological framework for high-dimensional complex image phenotypes.

</details>


### [208] [Beyond Shapley: An Influence-Based Data Auditing Pipeline for LLM Alignment and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22766)
*Yunting Song,Matthew Watson,Peter Grabowski,Jun Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Shapley值近似的高效数据价值评估流水线，通过推理时的概率分布分析，有效识别对齐数据集中的人为标注错误、安全风险和结构冲突，大幅降低人工审计成本并揭示现有基准测试的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型对齐数据集规模的扩大，偏好和指令微调语料库中不可避免地积累了隐藏的结构性矛盾、安全风险和系统性人为标注错误。现有的数据审计方法（如语义去重或LLM-as-a-judge）难以捕捉单条记录的实际预测影响，且常遗漏深层的功能规则冲突。

Method: 提出一种可扩展的、仅推理的数据价值评估流水线，无需迭代模型重训练即可近似计算Shapley值。该方法将语义k-NN邻域映射为有向图，通过参考LLM的零样本和单样本条件对数似然变化直接评估数据效用，并将预测影响分数转化为局部优势度量，以隔离梯度冲突记录。

Result: 在HelpSteer2数据集上，人工审计搜索空间减少了99.1%，成功发现了多种失败模式下的错误标注记录；在Anthropic的HH-RLHF训练和评估分割中，发现了数千个隐藏的安全和事实偏好反转；揭示了当前基准测试完整性的严重脆弱性——高能力模型经常预测更安全或更有帮助的响应，却被客观上有缺陷的人类地面真值标签惩罚。
