In this setting, self-speculative decoding faces a new bottleneck: increasing the draft expert set improves accuracy but triggers extra expert loading, while cheap small-footprint drafts have low acceptance; moreover, verifying a multi-token block activates the union of target experts and is no longer close to one target step. We propose DraftExpert, an expansion-aware self-speculative decoding framework for expert-offloaded MoE inference. DraftExpert trains one lightweight accelerator-resident draft expert per layer by self-distilling residual, logit/token, and router-agreement signals from the frozen target MoE. At inference time, it uses a fixed-footprint shared+top-1+draft-expert drafter together with confidence--expansion truncation and target-expert prefetching, while final tokens are still exactly verified by the target model. On DeepSeek-V2-Lite and Moonlight-16B-A3B across CPU-GPU and Flash-NPU offload, DraftExpert improves decode throughput by 1.45x on average, raises draft acceptance to 84~87%, and achieves 86~88% prefetch hit rates.

</details>


### [350] [What do Reward Models Memorize?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24484)
*Ivo Verhoeven,Pushkar Mishra,Ekaterina Shutova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过反事实记忆测量方法研究判别式奖励模型的记忆行为，发现其存在记忆分配偏差、数据集捷径记忆和启发式过度泛化三个关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解判别式训练的奖励模型从人类偏好数据中究竟记住了什么，以及这些记忆如何影响模型在上下文依赖场景下的判断能力。

Method: 采用反事实记忆测量方法，在两个人类偏好数据集上系统分析判别式奖励模型的记忆行为，考察模型对不同类型偏好对的记忆分配模式。

Result: 发现三个关键问题：1）奖励模型错误地将记忆资源分配给简单、高边际的偏好对；2）记忆了数据集特定的捷径（如模型身份、用户采样策略）；3）在面对未见过的偏好对时，过度泛化简单的启发式相关性（如长度、顺从性）。

Conclusion: 从人类偏好数据进行的判别式训练会产生有偏见的奖励模型，这些模型尚不具备在上下文依赖场景中判断响应质量的能力，揭示了当前方法的关键局限性。

Abstract: This paper studies what discriminatively trained reward models (RMs) memorize by measuring counterfactual memorization on two human preference datasets. We show that RMs 1) misallocate memorization to easy, high margin preference pairs, 2) memorize dataset-specific shortcuts (e.g., model identity, user sampling strategy), and 3) overgeneralize simple heuristic correlates of human preference (e.g., length, compliance) when confronted with unseen preference pairs. Overall, our findings indicate that discriminative training of RMs from human preference data results in biased RMs not yet capable of judging response quality in context-dependent scenarios.

</details>


### [351] [UNIFUSION: Adapting Autoregressive Language Models into Discrete Diffusion under a Unified Reverse-Rate Objective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24507)
*Xiaoyi Jiang,Jingyuan Li,Yixuan Jiang,Wei Liu,Yi Zhu,Zuoqiang Shi,Pipi Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DiffusionGPT，一种将预训练的GPT2模型直接适配到均匀噪声扩散的方法。通过建立不同扩散语言模型（SEDD、MDLM/GIDD、M2S、Neural CTMC）之间的理论联系，推导出统一的x0接口，实现了模型检查点在不同 corruption kernel 间的转换。实验表明，该方法在生成困惑度和熵的权衡上表现优异，并在多个推理基准测试中取得了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要将预训练的自回归语言模型适配到掩码扩散，但无法让每个token在采样过程中都保持可编辑状态。直接将AR模型适配到均匀噪声扩散虽然能解决这个问题，但在不同corruption kernel之间转换检查点存在挑战，因为现有的扩散语言模型使用不同的目标和预测参数化方式。

Method: 1. 建立了SEDD、MDLM/GIDD、M2S和Neural CTMC之间的理论联系，将它们的条件损失表达为模型逆向率上的单一广义KL目标；2. 推导了从干净token预测到具体分数、后验均值和退出率/跳跃参数化的转换公式，得到支持在掩码和均匀核之间切换的共享x0接口；3. 提出了DiffusionGPT，一种简单的持续预训练方法，直接将预训练的GPT2检查点适配到均匀噪声扩散。

Result: 在124M和355M参数规模上，随着采样步数从16增加到256，DiffusionGPT持续改善生成困惑度和unigram熵的权衡关系。在256步时，DiffusionGPT-S和DiffusionGPT-M分别达到GenPPL/熵为97.783/5.2626和71.516/5.6669的性能对；在相同规模下，没有模型能同时在这两个指标上超越DiffusionGPT。在推理基准测试中，DiffusionGPT在WinoGrande、SIQA和BBH上达到了对比扩散模型中的最高准确率。

Conclusion: 本研究成功建立了不同扩散语言模型之间的统一理论框架，使得预训练的自回归模型能够有效适配到均匀噪声扩散。DiffusionGPT证明了直接适配方法的优越性，在生成质量和推理能力上都展现出竞争力，为扩散语言模型的发展提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Existing methods mainly adapt pretrained autoregressive (AR) language models to masked diffusion, whereas we directly adapt them to uniform-noise diffusion, where every token remains editable during sampling. However, adapting AR checkpoints across corruption kernels remains challenging because existing DLMs use different objectives and prediction parameterizations. We establish connections among SEDD, MDLM/GIDD, M2S, and Neural CTMC by expressing their conditional losses as a single generalized Kullback--Leibler objective over model reverse rates. We further derive conversions from clean-token predictions to concrete-score, posterior-mean, and exit-rate/jump parameterizations, yielding a shared \(x_0\) interface that supports switching between mask and uniform kernels. Building on these connections, we propose \ours{}, a simple continual pre-training approach for directly adapting pretrained GPT2 checkpoints to uniform-noise diffusion. Through systematic evaluation of 124M- and 355M-parameter models, we show that \ours{} steadily improves the trade-off between generative perplexity (GenPPL) and unigram entropy as the sampling budget increases from 16 to 256 steps. At 256 steps, \ours{}-S and \ours{}-M achieve GenPPL/entropy pairs of \(97.783/5.2626\) and \(71.516/5.6669\), respectively; no evaluated model at the same scale simultaneously outperforms \ours{} on both metrics. At both scales, \ours{} also achieves the highest WinoGrande, SIQA, and BBH accuracy among the compared diffusion models.

</details>


### [352] [Physics Transformer: Tailoring Transformer for General PDE Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24513)
*Guoze Sun,Rui Zhang,Jiankai Tang,Mengtao Yan,Runze Mao,Zhi X. Chen,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Physics Transformer，一种基于函数投影的Transformer架构，用于偏微分方程求解。通过自适应局部基函数投影构建物理表达性强的token，实现了对任意离散化的有效处理，在多个基准测试中达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer在求解偏微分方程时面临关键挑战：物理场是无限维函数的有限采样，不同于语言token或固定分辨率图像patch。需要设计一种tokenizer，能够尊重物理场的函数特性，并从任意离散化中构建物理表达性强的token。

Method: 提出Physics Transformer架构，将物理场视为连续函数并将离散化划分为保局部性的空间patch。在每个patch内动态学习自适应局部基函数，将采样场投影到这些基函数上获得紧凑的物理token。通过分解注意力机制实现空间和物理状态的高效全局交互，投影表示支持在任意查询位置的高效解码。

Result: 在多样化基准测试上进行广泛实验，包括二维PDE动力学和工业级三维CFD模拟。实验结果表明该方法能够准确捕捉细粒度物理结构，在预测性能上达到state-of-the-art水平，验证了函数投影作为PDE求解Transformer设计基础的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 函数投影是设计偏微分方程求解Transformer架构的实用且有效的基础，Physics Transformer成功地将物理场的函数特性融入token构建过程，为解决任意离散化下的PDE建模问题提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Transformer architectures have attracted increasing attention for solving partial differential equations (PDEs), owing to their flexibility in handling irregular discretizations and their ability to capture long-range physical dependencies. However, unlike discrete language tokens or fixed-resolution image patches, observed physical fields are finite samples of underlying infinite-dimensional functions. Consequently, effectively applying Transformers to PDEs requires a tokenizer that respects the functional nature of physical fields and constructs physically expressive tokens from arbitrary discretizations.To this end, we propose \methodname{Physics Transformer}, a function-projection-based Transformer architecture for physical field prediction. Physics Transformer treats a physical field as a continuous function and partitions its discretization into locality-preserving spatial patches. Within each patch, it dynamically learns a set of adaptive local basis functions and projects the sampled field onto these bases to obtain compact physics tokens. The resulting tokens capture diverse latent physical states while preserving fine-scale spatial structures, enabling efficient global interaction through factorized attention across space and physical states. The projected representation further supports efficient decoding at arbitrary query locations. Extensive experiments on diverse benchmarks, ranging from two-dimensional PDE dynamics to industrial-scale three-dimensional CFD simulations, demonstrate that Physics Transformer accurately captures fine-grained physical structures and achieves state-of-the-art predictive performance. These results establish function projection as a practical and effective foundation for designing Transformer architectures for PDE solving.

</details>


### [353] [Low-Rank Dependence Decomposition via Accelerated Symmetric Non-negative Matrix Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24518)
*Lavinia Ghita,Dhruv Desai,Jake Goldberg,Roman Yokunda Enzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过矩阵恒等式重构实现了大规模对称非负矩阵分解(SymNMF)，在单GPU上处理n≈10^5规模，多节点分布式扩展至n=10^6，发现不同优化器在不同矩阵谱结构下各有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称非负矩阵分解(SymNMF)能够从依赖矩阵中恢复潜在分组结构，但其稠密、二次内存消耗的目标函数限制了现有工作只能处理中等规模数据。金融风险因子估计需要在大规模投资组合上处理Pearson相关矩阵和尾部成对依赖矩阵，亟需突破规模瓶颈。

Method: 提出迹恒等式重构消除所有n×n中间矩阵，实现单GPU处理n≈10^5、多节点分布式处理n=10^6规模；系统评估了7类算法家族(超30种配置)，引入三种新方法：分段AdaGrad、行随机SVRG和块SVRG AdaptGrow；采用两阶段协议测试收敛性，并以球面K-means作为硬标签基线。

Result: 在n=10^5规模有11种方法收敛，其中6种保持高效(5种AdaGrad类+ADMM)；在n=10^6规模有5种AdaGrad类方法收敛。块SVRG AdaptGrow在平坦、病态的尾部依赖谱上最优(低迭代成本适合长因子分解)，全批次AdaGrad在主导低秩的相关谱上最优(短运行)。球面K-means在有角度聚类结构时更廉价，但在矩阵坍缩向单一公共因子时退化。

Conclusion: 通过算法创新和GPU并行化，SymNMF成功扩展至百万级规模，不同优化器适应不同矩阵谱特性：低迭代成本方法适合长因子分解的病态谱，全批次方法适合短运行的低秩谱。软因子分解在某些退化场景下仍然必要。

Abstract: Symmetric non-negative matrix factorization (SymNMF) recovers latent group structure from a dependence matrix, but its dense, quadratic-memory objective has confined prior work to moderate sizes. We present a large-scale GPU study of seven algorithm families (over 30 configurations) on absolute Pearson correlation and tail pairwise dependence matrices from Extreme Value Theory, two proxies for empirical risk-factor estimation on large portfolios. A trace-identity reformulation eliminates all $n \times n$ intermediates, so a single GPU reaches $n \approx 10^5$ and multi-node distribution scales to $n = 10^6$ and beyond. Under a two-phase protocol, eleven methods converge at moderate scale; six remain efficient enough at $n = 10^5$ (five AdaGrad-family plus ADMM), and five AdaGrad-family methods still converge at $n = 10^6$: AdaGrad, RMSprop, and three we introduce (Piecewise AdaGrad, Row-Stochastic SVRG, Block-SVRG AdaptGrow). At $n = 10^6$ the fastest solver tracks the matrix spectrum: Block-SVRG AdaptGrow wins on the flat, ill-conditioned tail-dependence spectrum, where its lower per-iteration cost decides a long factorization, and full-batch AdaGrad wins on the dominant-low-rank correlation spectrum, where the run is short. We also benchmark spherical K-means as a hard-label baseline: cheaper when angular cluster structure is present, yet provably degenerate once the matrix collapses toward a single common factor, where the soft factorization remains necessary.

</details>


### [354] [Stress-Testing EEG Foundation Models for Clinical Decoding: Dataset Identity and Targeted Negative Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24519)
*Marzieh Zare*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了6个EEG基础模型在5个临床任务上的表现，发现这些模型在跨人群迁移和临床解码任务中表现不佳，评估结论高度依赖评估单元、数据集偏移、基线强度和控制变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的EEG基础模型在临床解码中的应用日益增多，但其在跨人群迁移能力和对负向控制的鲁棒性方面尚不明确。现有研究缺乏对这些模型在真实临床场景中的系统评估。

Method: 在四个数据集上评估六个EEG基础模型（LaBraM、EEGMamba、CBraMod、REVE、BENDR、BIOT）在五个临床任务上的表现。使用冻结线性探测，采用留一被试、被试分组或明确的记录级划分。对REVE模型进行多种负向控制测试：随机初始化、随机特征、标签置换、打乱标签微调和投影敏感性分析。

Result: 在韩国痴呆症数据集上，冻结的REVE（AUROC 0.568）显著低于经典特征方法（0.769），随机初始化编码器也优于预训练REVE（0.659 vs 0.570）。数据集身份可从冻结嵌入中完美解码（AUROC 1.000），但诊断解码效果差（AUROC 0.528）。唯一的明确正向结果是在CHB-MIT癫痫检测任务上，REVE达到0.793 AUROC，比随机初始化编码器高9.2个百分点。

Conclusion: EEG基础模型的性能评估结论强烈依赖于评估单元选择、数据集偏移程度、基线对比方法的强度以及针对性的控制实验设计。当前EEG基础模型在临床任务上的表现并不理想，需要更严格的评估框架。

Abstract: Pretrained EEG foundation models are increasingly proposed for clinical decoding, but their transfer across populations and robustness to negative controls remain unclear. We benchmark six models (LaBraM, EEGMamba, CBraMod, REVE, BENDR, and BIOT) on five clinical tasks across four datasets using frozen linear probes with leave-one-subject-out, subject-grouped, or explicitly identified recording-level splits. Selected REVE findings are tested against random initialisation, random features, label permutation, scrambled-label fine-tuning, and projection sensitivity. On Korean dementia (CAUEEG, three-way), frozen REVE reaches 0.568 AUROC versus 0.769 for classical features; the ordering persists on a patient-disjoint held-out split (0.565 versus 0.768). Dataset identity is readily decoded from frozen embeddings (AUROC 1.000 at PCA-50; 0.9998 after band restriction and per-epoch z-scoring), whereas the same PCA-50 pipeline decodes Korean diagnosis at 0.528. A randomly initialised encoder also outperforms pretrained REVE on this task (0.659 versus 0.570). On Alzheimer's disease, Gaussian random projection and PCA of the same pretrained embeddings perform similarly, and classical features nominally exceed REVE at the subject level. The clearest controlled positive is cross-subject ictal detection on CHB-MIT (n=23), where REVE achieves 0.793 AUROC, 9.2 percentage points above a randomly initialised encoder. These results show that EEG foundation-model conclusions depend strongly on evaluation unit, dataset shift, comparator strength, and targeted controls.

</details>


### [355] [FlowCTS: On-policy Continuous Trajectory Supervision of Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24522)
*Kaiyang Ye,Yuan Ge,Junxiang Zhang,Bei Li,Ziming Zhu,Haishu Zhao,Xiaoqian Liu,Chenglong Wang,Jingbo Zhu,Zhengtao Yu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FlowCTS方法，将在线策略蒸馏扩展到流模型，通过时间加权的速度匹配上界实现轨迹监督，在多个指标上显著优于传统KL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏在大语言模型后训练中有效解决了稀疏奖励和曝光偏差问题，但其向流模型的扩展仍未被探索。传统KL-based OPD在流模型中存在时间监督不匹配的问题，需要专门针对流模型设计新的蒸馏方法。

Method: 提出Flow Continuous Trajectory Supervision (FlowCTS)，通过匹配从相同学生访问状态初始化的后续学生轨迹和参考轨迹。利用轨迹与速度场之间的积分关系，推导出时间加权的速度匹配上界，并将其离散化为由监督步数参数化的实际目标函数。

Result: 在多参考设置下，单状态FlowCTS-OPD相比vanilla KL-based OPD收敛更快。FlowCTS-OPD将GenEval从0.90提升至0.93，OCR从0.90提升至0.92，PickScore从22.75提升至23.06，在所有目标指标上均优于混合奖励RL基线。在非在线策略设置下，FlowCTS也持续优于vanilla SFT，特别是在OCR任务上。

Conclusion: FlowCTS成功将在线策略蒸馏扩展到流模型，揭示了vanilla KL-based OPD中由辅助SDE转移核引起的时间监督不匹配问题。增加监督步数表现出更丰富的轨迹信息与更大优化难度之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: While on-policy distillation (OPD) effectively addresses sparse rewards and exposure bias in large language model post-training, its extension to flow models remains underexplored. To this end, we propose Flow Continuous Trajectory Supervision (FlowCTS), which matches subsequent student and reference trajectories initialized from the same student-visited state. Using the integral relation between trajectories and velocity fields, we derive a temporally weighted velocity-matching upper bound and discretize it into practical objectives parameterized by the number of supervision steps. Under a multi-reference setup, single-state FlowCTS-OPD outperforms vanilla KL-based OPD with faster convergence. FlowCTS-OPD improves GenEval from 0.90 to 0.93, OCR from 0.90 to 0.92, and PickScore from 22.75 to 23.06, while outperforming a mixed-reward RL baseline across all target metrics. Further analysis reveals a clear temporal supervision mismatch in vanilla KL-based OPD arising from its auxiliary SDE transition kernels. Beyond on-policy setting,FlowCTS also consistently outperforms vanilla SFT , particularly on OCR, while increasing supervision steps exhibit a trade-off between richer trajectory information and greater optimization difficulty.

</details>


### [356] [From Machine Learning to Large-Scale EO Products: Best Practices for Making Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24532)
*Ghjulia Sialelli,Robin Young,Yuchang Jiang,Cesar Aybar,Linus Scheibenreif,Damien Robert,Clemens Mosig,Adam J. Stewart,Jan D. Wegner,Aleksis Pirinen,Olof Mogren,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文总结了从卫星数据到可运行地图产品整个流程的最佳实践，涵盖数据基础设施、预处理、数据集构建、模型训练、不确定性量化、地图生产分发和验证六个主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来机器学习和计算基础设施的发展推动了大规模地理空间地图的快速发展，但生成地图的门槛降低的同时，最佳实践尚未建立，管道早期的设计决策可能会悄悄地将错误传播到最终产品中，生成技术上合理且科学可信的产品仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 围绕六个相互关联的主题组织讨论：地球观测数据基础设施景观、数据选择和预处理、机器学习数据集构建和模型训练、不确定性量化、地图生产和分发、以及验证。

Result: 提供了一个从卫星数据到可运行地图产品整个管道的简明端到端最佳实践账户，并在在线指南中提供了更详细的深度内容。

Conclusion: 管道中的每个阶段选择都紧密耦合：预处理决策塑造训练信号，数据集设计控制模型学习和性能评估的可靠性，全球尺度推断引入计算和数据访问以及伪影缓解方面的工程挑战，不确定量化等需要专门的方法论关注。

Abstract: Recent years have seen a rapid expansion in the production of large-scale geospatial maps derived from Earth observation (EO) data, driven largely by advances in machine learning (ML) and large computing infrastructure. Although the barrier to generating such maps has dropped substantially, established best practices have yet to emerge, and design decisions made early in the pipeline can quietly propagate errors into the final product. Producing a technically sound and scientifically credible product remains challenging. Choices made at every stage are tightly coupled: preprocessing decisions shape the training signal, dataset design governs what the model can learn and how reliably its performance can be assessed, and global-scale inference introduces engineering challenges in compute and data access at scale, as well as artifact mitigation. Furthermore, uncertainty quantification and independent map validation each require dedicated methodological attention that is often underestimated. This paper presents a concise, end-to-end account of the recommended practices spanning the pipeline from satellite data to an operational map product. We organize the discussion around six interconnected themes: the EO data infrastructure landscape, data selection and preprocessing, ML dataset construction and model training, uncertainty quantification, map production and distribution, and validation. This paper is a condensed version of a longer guide that provides greater depth across all stages, accessible online at ghjuliasialelli.github.io/MLEO-Maps/.

</details>


### [357] [The K-SCAN Clustering Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24537)
*Filip Kosiorowski,Grzegorz Sroka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了K-SCAN混合聚类算法，结合了向量量化和密度分析，在大数据背景下实现了线性时间复杂度，同时保持了识别非线性簇的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大数据时代，传统密度聚类方法（如DBSCAN）虽能检测非线性簇且抗噪，但其二次时间复杂度限制了应用；而划分式算法（如K-Means）虽线性高效，却强制簇为球形且对异常值敏感。研究动机在于优化这两类方法之间的权衡。

Method: 提出K-SCAN混合算法：首先采用随机Mini-Batch K-Means进行初步向量量化，提取一组加权的微簇；随后对这些微簇进行基于密度的结构分析。

Result: 在多达10^6样本的数据集上验证了线性计算复杂度；相比层次算法BIRCH实现超过3倍加速；在噪声比例达55%时仍保持结构稳定性（ARI>0.99），能精确识别非线性结构。

Conclusion: K-SCAN成功平衡了计算效率与聚类质量，适用于大规模数据场景，但对高度异质局部密度的簇分离困难及可能过平滑仍是其局限性。

Abstract: In the Big Data era, the scalability of clustering algorithms constitutes a key challenge. Traditional density-based methods (e.g., DBSCAN) offer robustness to noise and the ability to detect non-linear clusters, yet their quadratic time complexity $O(N^2)$ drastically limits their applicability. Conversely, partitional algorithms (e.g., K-Means), with their linear complexity $O(N)$, impose sphericity on the resulting groups and fail in the presence of outliers. This paper presents K-SCAN -- a novel hybrid algorithm that optimizes this trade-off. The method integrates preliminary vector quantization (stochastic Mini-Batch K-Means) to extract a reduced set of weighted micro-clusters, followed by a subsequent density-based structural analysis. Empirical evaluation on datasets of up to $10^6$ samples confirms the linear computational complexity of the proposed solution. K-SCAN achieves more than a 3-fold speed-up over the hierarchical BIRCH algorithm, avoiding the costly management of tree-based structures. The method precisely identifies non-linear manifolds while maintaining structural stability (Adjusted Rand Index > 0.99), even with noise levels reaching 55\% of the data volume. The main limitation of the proposed algorithm, which could not be fully eliminated in the present study, remains its susceptibility to over-smoothing and its difficulty in separating clusters with highly heterogeneous local density. In complex visual spaces, this can lead to the loss of the finest topological details.

</details>


### [358] [EchoBridge: Long-Tail-Aware ECG-Echocardiography Text Alignment for Echocardiography-Derived Cardiac Findings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24553)
*Xiaocheng Fang,Jieyi Cai,Guangkun Nie,Haoyu Wang,Jiarui Jin,Yujie Xiao,Bo Liu,Chenyang He,Qinghao Zhao,Gaofeng Cheng,Hongyan Li,Shenda Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EchoBridge通过CSPP和APBC模块解决ECG表示学习中的模态特定因素纠缠和低患病率监督稀疏问题，在多数据集上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全局ECG-文本对齐方法可能纠缠模态特定因素，且长尾分布的发现类型为低患病率条件提供稀疏的正监督，限制了模型性能。

Method: 提出EchoBridge框架，包含：(1)互补共享-私有投影(CSPP)，将每个模态映射到共享和辅助私有投影，通过模态内正交性减少方向冗余，并双向对齐归一化共享投影；(2)自适应原型边界校准(APBC)，用类别特定原型、训练频率自适应角边距和球面Riesz斥力组织共享超球面。

Result: 在EchoNext-Mini、PKUPH和SHTMU数据集上，EchoBridge在无分类器推理场景下AUROC、AUPRC和F1分别比最强基线提高7.88、5.61和4.54个百分点；在所有域内和目标域探测预算及两个源域迁移队列中均达到最高点估计值；多数疾病类型（包括多个低患病率瓣膜发现）均获得性能提升。

Conclusion: EchoBridge有效解决了标准化超声心动图结论指导ECG表示学习中的关键挑战，实现了跨中心和跨领域的强泛化性能，尤其改善了低患病率条件的识别能力。

Abstract: Standardized echocardiography conclusions provide meaningful supervision for learning ECG representations of echocardiography-derived cardiac findings. Global ECG--text alignment may entangle modality-specific factors, while long-tailed finding distributions provide sparse positive supervision for low-prevalence conditions. We propose EchoBridge with Complementary Shared--Private Projection (CSPP) and Adaptive Prototype Boundary Calibration (APBC). CSPP maps each modality into shared and auxiliary private projections, reduces directional redundancy via within-modality orthogonality, and bidirectionally aligns normalized shared projections. APBC organizes the shared hypersphere with class-specific prototypes, training-frequency-adaptive angular margins, and spherical Riesz repulsion. We evaluate EchoBridge on EchoNext-Mini and independent PKUPH and SHTMU cohorts under four protocols: prompt-based inference without downstream classifier training, in-domain frozen linear probing, target-domain cross-center frozen linear probing, and source-only cross-center transfer, supplemented by finding-specific analyses. EchoBridge improves classifier-free AUROC, AUPRC, and F1 over the strongest baselines by 7.88, 5.61, and 4.54 points, respectively, and achieves the highest point estimates across all in-domain and target-domain probing budgets and both source-only transfer cohorts. Finding-specific analyses show gains for most conditions, including several low-prevalence valvular findings.

</details>


### [359] [LOCKS: Page-Local Compact Key Summaries for Efficient Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24555)
*Junsung Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LOCKS是一种基于谱摘要的KV缓存选择机制，在保持性能的同时显著降低长上下文LLM的推理延迟和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在长上下文推理时，每步解码都需要完整读取KV缓存，成为性能瓶颈。注意力键在局部低秩而全局高秩，现有方法难以高效选择相关页面。

Method: LOCKS为每个页面维护一个谱摘要（约为缓存大小的十分之一），通过重建页面内logits并使用log-sum-exp估计注意力质量，仅关注得分最高的页面，选择过程无需读取候选键值。

Result: 在LongBench-v1上性能接近完整缓存，在RULER上追踪读取全部键的oracle，在AIME26和MATH-500长形式推理任务上显著优于基线选择器。在2048 token预算下匹配FullKV在100K+上下文的聚合质量，仅关注约2%的token，在1M token时将每token解码延迟减半。

Conclusion: LOCKS作为vLLM的即插即用插件实现，支持完整CUDA图批处理解码，在保持长上下文推理质量的同时实现了显著的速度提升和内存效率。

Abstract: Serving large language models at long context is bottlenecked by the key-value (KV) cache, which is read in full at every decode step. Attention keys are locally low-rank though globally high-rank: shared low-rank bases discard page-specific directions that a page's own compact basis retains. LOCKS gives every page its own spectral summary (resident, about a tenth the cache's size), reconstructs within-page logits, estimates each page's attention mass by log-sum-exp, and attends only the top pages; selection itself reads no candidate keys or values. Selecting on this summary alone stays within about a point of the full cache on long-document QA (LongBench-v1), tracks the read-every-key oracle on retrieval-dense RULER down to the smallest budgets, and shows its largest margins on long-form reasoning (AIME26, MATH-500), where baseline selectors collapse. At its shipped $2048$-token budget LOCKS matches FullKV aggregate quality at $100$K$+$ context while attending about $2\%$ of the tokens, and halves per-token decode latency ($2.0\times$ at $1$M tokens) against dense attention. LOCKS ships as a drop-in plugin for unmodified vLLM, with batched decode running in full CUDA graphs.

</details>


### [360] [BettiSplit: Topology-Guided Privacy-Aware Split Learning Against Feature Inversion and Gradient Leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24556)
*Akarsh K. Nair,Muhammad Arifur Rahman,David Brown,Mufti Mahmud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BettiSafe框架，利用拓扑复杂度指导分割学习中的隐私保护分割点选择，相比深度启发式方法显著提升抗特征反演能力2-5倍，同时保持模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分割学习通过将神经网络分割在客户端和服务器之间实现协作训练，但不恰当的分割点选择会导致中间表示出现严重隐私泄露，现有基于网络深度的启发式方法无法准确捕捉隐私风险的分布特征。

Method: 提出基于持久Betti复杂度的拓扑引导框架，通过层间分析发现分割学习中的隐私风险在不同层间呈现高度非均匀分布，并在深度隐私关键分割点出现从可忽略重构到高达0.98 SSIM特征反演保真度的急剧转变；基于此提出BettiSafe分割选择策略和Betti正则化方法。

Result: Betti复杂度能够一致性地识别跨架构和数据集中具有高隐私泄露风险的表示区域；BettiSafe相比基于深度的启发式方法提升抗特征反演能力2-5倍并保持分类准确率；基于Betti的正则化在不损失模型效用的情况下将近似反演难度提升约5倍。

Conclusion: 拓扑复杂度作为结构描述符，为真实协作系统中的安全、自适应和表示感知的分割学习提供了新途径，实现了有利的隐私-效用权衡，为隐私保护协作学习系统的实际部署提供了解决方案。

Abstract: Split learning enables collaborative model training by partitioning neural networks across clients and servers. However, improper split placement can lead to severe privacy leakage through intermediate representations. In this work, we propose a topology-guided framework for privacy-aware split learning based on the persistent Betti complexity of smashed activations. Through comprehensive layer-wise analysis, we show that privacy risk in split learning is highly non-uniform across layers and exhibits sharp transition regions that are not captured by architectural depth alone. In particular, feature inversion fidelity increases from negligible reconstruction to as high as 0.98 SSIM at deeper, privacy-critical split points. We further demonstrate that Betti complexity consistently identifies representation regimes associated with elevated feature-space privacy leakage across architectures and datasets. Leveraging this observation, we introduce BettiSafe, a topology-guided split selection strategy that identifies privacy-sensitive layers without requiring explicit attack execution. BettiSafe improves resistance to feature inversion by 2 to 5 times compared to depth-based heuristics while preserving classification accuracy. In addition, Betti-based regularisation increases inversion difficulty by nearly 5 x without degrading model utility, enabling a favourable privacy utility tradeoff. Overall, our results highlight topological complexity as a promising structural descriptor for secure, adaptive, and representation-aware split learning in real-world collaborative systems

</details>


### [361] [Bit-Accurate FPGA Evaluation of Learned Feature Gating in a Fixed-Point Fourier-Feature Automatic Modulation Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24568)
*Gawthaman Senthilvelan,Luthira Abeykoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在FPGA上实现自动调制分类（AMC）时，学习型特征重加权（门控）机制的成本收益权衡，发现门控机制增加了硬件资源消耗和处理延迟，却未提升分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型特征重加权在软件中能提升自动调制分类性能，但在FPGA硬件实现时会引入额外的算术运算和延迟。本研究旨在量化评估这一软硬件实现之间的权衡，探究门控机制在硬件实现中的实际价值。

Method: 设计两种架构：紧凑型定点分类器（使用24个稀疏DFT能量特征、8个相位/统计特征和32-128-11多层感知机）以及插入学习型32元素8位输入依赖门控的分类器。采用训练后量化（PTQ）和量化感知训练（QAT）两种方法，使用两个匹配的训练种子训练，生成8个检查点。在Intel Cyclone V FPGA上编译并进行352,000次物理板级分类测试。

Result: 在四种匹配的门控对比中，无门控模型均达到更高的测试准确率。PTQ下门控与无门控差异为-0.784个百分点，QAT下为-0.616个百分点。硬件上门控平均增加1,318个自适应逻辑模块（ALM）、1,557个寄存器、4个DSP块和3,140个处理周期。所有352,000次板级预测与独立整数参考完全一致。

Conclusion: 对于本研究的特征表示和实现方式，学习型门控机制在FPGA上增加了硬件成本（资源占用和处理延迟），但并未改善分类准确率，因此在硬件实现中不具有优势。

Abstract: Learned feature reweighting can improve automatic modulation classification (AMC) in software, but the same operation introduces additional arithmetic and latency when implemented on an FPGA. This work measures that trade-off in a compact fixed-point classifier using 24 sparse DFT-energy features, 8 phase/statistical features, and a 32-to-128-to-11 multilayer perceptron. A second architecture inserts a learned 32-element, 8-bit, input-dependent gate before the classifier. Gated and ungated models are trained using post-training quantization (PTQ) and quantization-aware training (QAT) with two matched training seeds. The resulting eight checkpoints are compiled independently for an Intel Cyclone V FPGA and evaluated over 352,000 physical-board classifications. Ungated models achieve higher test accuracy in all four matched gate comparisons, with mean gated-minus-ungated differences of -0.784 percentage points under PTQ and -0.616 percentage points under QAT. The effect of QAT changes direction between the two training seeds. In hardware, the gate adds an average of 1,318 adaptive logic modules (ALMs), 1,557 registers, 4 DSP blocks, and 3,140 processing cycles. All 352,000 board predictions agree exactly with an independent integer reference, and 3,760 captured intermediate values from one training seed also match. For this feature representation and implementation, learned gating increases FPGA cost without improving classification accuracy.

</details>


### [362] [Evaluating Fuzz Testing for Reinforcement Learning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24577)
*Zhibin Kang,Hanmo You,Dong Wang,Haiming Zheng,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是首个系统性评估强化学习模糊测试方法的综合实证研究，从有效性、多样性、效率和实用性四个维度对5种先进方法进行了基准测试，发现不同方法各有所长，模糊测试生成的崩溃可用于提升智能体鲁棒性和安全监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习智能体越来越多地部署在机器人、自动驾驶、无人机控制等安全关键领域，意外行为可能导致严重后果。尽管模糊测试在探索RL智能体状态空间和暴露崩溃方面展现出潜力，但现有研究在评估设置、基线和度量指标上存在差异，难以对其相对有效性和实用性得出可靠结论。

Method: 提出首个综合性实证研究，从有效性、多样性、效率和实用性四个互补维度系统评估RL模糊测试方法。在三个复杂度递增的环境（MountainCar、BipedalWalker和CARLA）中，以统一配置对5种最先进方法及随机测试进行基准测试，并评估所检测崩溃在智能体鲁棒性改进和安全监测方面的下游价值。

Result: 研究发现：(1)以吞吐量为导向的方法（如MDPFuzz）在崩溃发现方面展现出卓越的有效性和效率；(2)明确设计用于鼓励探索的方法（如SeqDivFuzz）在发现多样化崩溃行为方面表现突出；(3)模糊测试生成的崩溃能够有效提升智能体鲁棒性，并实现准确的安全监测，具有强跨方法泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本研究为研究人员和实践者提供了可操作的指导，强调结合互补模糊测试策略和采用多层次多样性分析的优势，以实现更全面和实用的RL测试。研究结果为RL模糊测试方法的选择和应用提供了实证依据，有助于推动安全关键领域RL系统的可靠性保障。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents are increasingly deployed in safety-critical domains such as robotics, autonomous driving, and drone control, where unexpected behaviors may lead to severe real-world consequences. Fuzz testing has recently emerged as a promising method for exploring the vast state spaces of RL agents and exposing crashes. Although numerous RL fuzzing methods have been proposed, existing studies often differ in evaluation settings, baselines, and metrics, making it difficult to draw reliable conclusions about their relative effectiveness and practical usefulness. To address this gap, we present the first comprehensive empirical study that systematically evaluates RL fuzzing methods from four complementary perspectives: effectiveness, diversity, efficiency, and practical utility. We benchmark five state-of-the-art methods alongside random testing under unified configurations across three environments of increasing complexity (MountainCar, BipedalWalker, and CARLA), and further assess the downstream usefulness of detected crashes for agent robustness improvement and safety monitoring. Our results reveal several key insights. For instance,throughput-oriented methods like MDPFuzz demonstrate superior effectiveness and efficiency in crash discovery, while methods explicitly designed to encourage exploration like SeqDivFuzz excel at uncovering diverse crash behaviors. We also show that fuzzing-generated crashes can meaningfully improve agent robustness and enable accurate safety monitoring with strong cross-method generalization. Beyond these empirical findings, we distill actionable guidance for both researchers and practitioners, highlighting the benefits of combining complementary fuzzing strategies and adopting multi-level diversity analysis to achieve more comprehensive and practical RL testing.

</details>


### [363] [PYPM-GGD: Pitman-Yor Process Mixture with Generalized Gaussian Density using ADAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24583)
*Kart-Leong Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于恒定步长随机梯度上升的大规模非共轭后验贝叶斯非参数学习方法，通过自适应步长策略提升收敛性能，在MIT67和SUN397等大规模数据集上达到了最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机变分推断（SVI）方法依赖于共轭变分后验假设，难以处理非共轭后验的大规模学习问题；现有的蒙特卡洛方法虽能处理非共轭后验，但计算复杂度高，难以应用于复杂的贝叶斯非参数模型。

Method: 提出一种基于恒定步长随机梯度上升的新型学习方法，仅需变分后验可微分而不需要期望的闭式解；借鉴SVI和Adam的思想，引入自适应步长策略以确保随机设置下的收敛性并显著提升学习效率。

Result: 所提方法与ResNet特征兼容，在MIT67和SUN397等大类数数据集上表现良好；与深度聚类等最新方法相比，在聚类评估指标上达到或超越最先进方法的表现。

Conclusion: 该方法成功突破了SVI在非共轭后验学习上的局限，为大规模贝叶斯非参数学习提供了新思路，在处理大类数数据集时具有优异性能。

Abstract: Large scale Bayesian nonparametrics (BNP) learner such as Stochastic Variational Inference (SVI) can handle datasets with large class number and large training size at fractional cost. Like its predecessor, SVI rely on the assumption of conjugate variational posterior to approximate the true posterior. A more challenging problem is to consider large scale learning on non-conjugate posterior. Recent works in this direction are mostly associated with using Monte Carlo methods for approximating the learner. However, these works are usually demonstrated on non-BNP related task and less complex models such as logistic regression, due to higher computational complexity. In order to overcome the issue faced by SVI, we develop a novel approach based on the recently proposed constant stepsize stochastic gradient ascent to allow large scale learning on non-conjugate posterior. Unlike SVI, our new learner does not require closed- form expression for the variational posterior expectatations. Our only requirement is that the variational posterior is differentiable. In order to ensure convergence in stochastic settings, SVI rely on decaying step-sizes to slow its learning. Inspired by SVI and Adam, we propose the novel use of adaptive stepsizes in our method to significantly improve its learning. We show that our proposed methods is compatible with ResNet features when applied to large class number datasets such as MIT67 and SUN397. Finally, we compare our proposed learner with several recent works such as deep clustering algorithms and showed we were able to produce on-par or outperform the state-of-the-art methods in terms of clustering measures.

</details>


### [364] [Attribution and Uncertainty Behavior of Learned Residual Gyro Correction for Gyro-Stellar Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24608)
*Mariela De Lucas Álvarez,Melvin Laux,Arthur de Freitas Precht,Maurice Martin,Edoardo Caroselli,Frank Kirchner,Alexander Fabisch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于深度学习的陀螺仪偏差修正框架中的不确定性分解与可解释性，发现认知不确定性在区分名义和扰动工作条件方面表现优于偶然不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升混合学习状态估计组件的可靠性，需要深入理解深度学习模型中不确定性来源及其行为，特别是在不同工作条件下偶然不确定性和认知不确定性的协作机制，以支持下游监控和故障检测应用。

Method: 采用一维卷积神经网络从多传感器输入预测残差角速率修正，网络同时输出均值修正和依赖于输入的偶然不确定性，通过独立训练模型集成估计认知不确定性；应用梯度归因方法分析修正输出和不确定性输出，在名义条件和结构化扰动（加性和时间相关噪声）下进行训练与评估。

Result: 偶然不确定性随扰动强度增加而增大，但分布重叠且跨条件下校准不一致；认知不确定性在分布偏移时给出清晰信号，模型间分歧增大；揭示了轴向特异性行为以及结构化扰动对两类不确定性协作的影响。

Conclusion: 偶然不确定性和认知不确定性能够有效协作，认知不确定性在区分名义和扰动工作条件方面表现更优；研究结果为混合学习状态估计组件的行为提供了深入见解，并激励将不确定性用于下游监控和故障检测。

Abstract: This work investigates uncertainty decomposition and explainability in a deep learning-based framework for gyroscope bias correction. A 1-D Convolutional Neural Network is trained to predict residual angular rate corrections from multi-sensor inputs, including gyroscope and star tracker measurements. The bias corrections are sent to a flight-representative Gyro-Stellar Estimator. The network produces both mean corrections and input-dependent (heteroscedastic) aleatoric uncertainty, while epistemic uncertainty is estimated via an ensemble of independently trained models.
  The proposed approach is trained under nominal conditions and evaluated in both nominal and structured perturbations that include additive and temporally correlated noise. Gradient-based attribution methods are applied to both the correction and uncertainty outputs, enabling a decomposition of the evidence that drives state updates and uncertainty estimates. By aggregating attribution patterns across rotational axes and regimes, we reveal axis-specific behaviors and characterize how structured perturbations influence the collaboration between aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty.
  Uncertainty analysis shows that aleatoric uncertainty increases with perturbation intensity, but the distributions overlap and the calibration is not consistent across regimes. On the other hand, epistemic uncertainty gives a clear signal that gets clearer as the distributional shift happens, showing that the models disagree more. These results show that aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty work well together and that epistemic uncertainty is better at distinguishing between nominal and perturbed operating conditions. The results provide insight into the behavior of hybrid learning-based state estimation components and motivate the use of uncertainty for downstream monitoring and fault detection.

</details>


### [365] [Sparse Autoencoders Encode Both Concepts and Functions: The Downstream Geometry of Feature Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24645)
*Phu Gia Hoang,Anwoy Chatterjee,Tanmoy Chakraborty,Iryna Gurevych,Subhabrata Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FEGA框架分析SAE特征的干预效果几何结构，发现一致的一维效应罕见，区分了价值型和指针型特征，证明可解释特征不一定能提供稳定的引导方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）作为可解释性工具被广泛使用，但SAE特征与模型行为之间的联系不一致：具有清晰激活描述的特征可能产生微弱或意外的因果效应；引导效果在不同提示词间变化甚至与预期方向相反；基于激活的特征选择可能遗漏产生期望输出变化的特征。这些限制阻碍了SAE的大规模应用。

Method: 提出特征效应几何分析（FEGA）框架：跨上下文移除相同的活跃SAE特征，分析产生的logit变化云。该方法关注特征干预引起的模型logit变化的几何结构，而非模型内部的特征几何结构。通过分析不同SAE变体下特征的干预效果，区分价值型特征（与静态信息如事实属性相关）和指针型特征（与上下文依赖的操作相关）。

Result: 跨SAE变体，一致的一维效应罕见：很少有特征表现得像可重复使用的方向。价值型特征更常表现出结构化的低维效应，但这些效应通常跨越多个方向。指针型特征主要表现出扩散效应。

Conclusion: 特征可以具有可解释性和因果相关性，但不一定能提供稳定的引导方向。这一发现对SAE特征作为引导工具的应用提出了重要限制。

Abstract: The wide-scale use of sparse autoencoders (SAEs) as interpretability tools is limited by inconsistent links between SAE features and model behavior. Features with clear activation descriptions may have weak or unexpected causal effects; steering can vary across prompts or oppose the intended direction; and activation-based feature selection can miss features that produce the desired output change. Prior work has studied feature geometry inside the model, where features are computed. We instead study the geometry of changes in model logits caused by feature interventions. We introduce Feature-Effect Geometry Analysis (FEGA), an unsupervised framework that removes the same active SAE feature across contexts and analyzes the resulting cloud of logit changes. Across SAE variants, consistent one-dimensional effects are rare: few features behave like reusable directions. To interpret this variation, we distinguish value-like features, tied to static information such as factual attributes, from pointer-like features, associated with context-dependent operations. Value-like features more often exhibit structured, low-dimensional effects, although these effects typically span several directions. Pointer-like features, by contrast, predominantly exhibit diffuse effects. Our results show that a feature can be interpretable and causally relevant without providing a stable direction for steering.

</details>


### [366] [When Can You Correct Distribution Drift in Temporal Graph Generation? A Sharpening--Drift Tension and an Impossibility for Observation-Based Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24662)
*Tianpeng Li,Xuan Guo,Wenjun Wang,Wang Zhang,Pengfei Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 时序图生成模型在训练和部署期间存在不可避免的性能退化问题，其退化程度呈幂律关系，且基于观测数据的校正方法几乎无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序图生成模型在训练时间段之后部署时会严重退化。研究者希望理解这种退化是否可预测、是否普遍存在，以及是否可以通过观测数据进行修复。

Method: 理论分析：使用掩码流匹配损失分解，无需独立性假设即可将其分解为不可约熵和散度；证明任何基于过去观测的可测量校正器都无法消除统计量的条件方差。实证验证：在七种充分验证的条件下，验证漂移期边际误差与采样预算的关系，并比较oracle校正器、基于观测的校正器和趋势外推方法的性能。

Result: 退化表现为幂律关系（指数-0.605，R²=0.9977）；漂移期的误差下限比训练期高2.2到34.3倍，且不随采样预算50倍变化范围而改变（误差变化最多6%）；Oracle校正器可消除60%误差，最佳观测校正器仅恢复其中5.7%，趋势外推方法比不做任何处理更差。

Conclusion: 时序图生成模型的部署退化是不可约的、普遍的且无法从观测数据修复的。漂移呈现无趋势且均值回归特性，单步创新与漂移本身量级相当。理论上证明任何基于过去观测的校正器都面临不可消除的条件方差下限，实际校正效果极其有限。

Abstract: Generative models of temporal graphs are trained on one stretch of an evolving network and deployed on the next, and they degrade badly in the gap. We show this degradation is derivable, general, and not fixable from observations. The masked flow-matching loss decomposes exactly, with no independence assumption, into an irreducible entropy plus a divergence whose derivative along the training path is positive precisely for structures rare during training and common at deployment, diverging as their training probability goes to zero. Empirically the trade-off is a power law with exponent $-0.605$ ($R^2=0.9977$), and drift raises the sampler's error floor without changing how many steps reach it: across seven well-powered conditions the drift-period marginal error varies by at most $6\%$ over a $50\times$ range of sampling budgets, while the floor sits $2.2\times$ to $34.3\times$ above the in-period floor. Because the deployment period is observed, correction looks like a matter of measurement. It is not. We prove that any corrector measurable with respect to past observations leaves at least the conditional variance of the statistic it tracks, and that trend extrapolation beats trusting the last observation only when $μ^2>v(1-2ρ)$. Both premises are measurable and both go the wrong way: the drift is trendless and mean-reverting, with a one-step innovation as large as the drift itself. An oracle removes $60\%$ of the error, the best observation-based corrector recovers $5.7\%$ of that, and extrapolation is strictly worse than doing nothing clever.

</details>


### [367] [Explainable Reinforcement Learning via Physics-Aware Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24672)
*Shaker Al-Tamari,Waled Kadour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过策略蒸馏方法，将高性能连续控制深度强化学习系统（TD3）转化为可解释的浅层决策树模型，在倒立摆基准测试中实现了与教师模型等效的性能，揭示了连续到离散控制转换引发的Bang-Bang控制特性和双峰极限环现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人和汽车工程等安全关键领域，深度强化学习的部署受限于深度神经网络的黑箱特性，缺乏透明性给监管合规和人机信任带来重大挑战，需要开发可解释的高性能连续控制DRL系统。

Method: 采用策略蒸馏框架，以高性能TD3智能体作为不透明连续教师模型，将其策略蒸馏为基于浅层决策树的可解释学生代理。通过自定义物理感知特征和"噪声Oracle推演"技术生成数据集，实现策略迁移。

Result: 蒸馏过程获得了与专家教师等效的性能；控制理论分析揭示了从连续到离散规则控制的根本权衡：引发高频Bang-Bang驱动和稳定双峰极限环；仿真结果证实系统保持有界输入有界输出（BIBO）稳定性。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功实现了高性能连续控制DRL系统的全局和局部可解释性，同时保持了系统稳定性，为安全自主系统的开发提供了可行路径，但需注意连续到离散控制转换带来的固有控制特性变化。

Abstract: In safety-critical sectors such as robotics and automotive engineering, the deployment of Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) is often hindered by the black-box nature of deep neural networks. This lack of transparency poses significant challenges for regulatory compliance and human-agent trust. This paper presents an experimental study aimed at making high-performance continuous control DRL systems interpretable. A policy distillation framework is implemented using the classic Inverted Pendulum benchmark. A high-performance Twin Delayed DDPG (TD3) agent serves as an opaque, continuous teacher model, whose policy is distilled into an interpretable student surrogate based on a shallow Decision Tree. By leveraging a custom physics-aware feature and "Noisy Oracle Rollouts" for dataset generation, the distillation process achieves performance equivalent to the expert teacher. Furthermore, comparative control theory analysis reveals a fundamental trade-off: transitioning from continuous to discrete rule-based control induces high-frequency Bang-Bang actuation and a stable bimodal limit cycle. Simulation results indicate that Bounded-Input Bounded-Output (BIBO) stability is maintained while providing both global and local interpretability for safe autonomous systems.

</details>


### [368] [Causal-TS: A Python Library for Causal Discovery in High-Dimensional and Nonstationary Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24673)
*Mohammad Fesanghary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Causal-TS是一个开源Python库，为高维和非平稳多变量时间序列提供因果发现功能，包含四种专门算法、统一CI测试层和完整端到端流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维和非平稳多变量时间序列的因果发现缺乏统一、高效的开源工具，现有方法难以处理结构突变和不同时间段的异质性参数，需要GPU加速来提升计算效率。

Method: 提供四种专门算法（CDNOTS、CDNOTS+、CEDAR、GRACE）及对GES、Granger、LASSO-VAR、LGES的封装；采用统一的条件独立性（CI）测试层并借助PyTorch实现GPU加速；包含regime discovery pipeline通过可插拔变点检测器识别结构突变并针对每个regime进行因果发现；提供命令行界面、合成数据生成器和可选的DoWhy集成。

Result: 成功构建了pip可安装、在Python 3.10-3.12版本上测试通过的库，代码开源发布于GitHub，实现了从原始时间序列到因果效应估计的端到端流程。

Conclusion: Causal-TS为复杂时间序列的因果发现研究提供了第一个集GPU加速、regime discovery和多种算法于统一框架的开源解决方案，降低了研究门槛并提升了实用性。

Abstract: We describe Causal-TS, an open-source Python library for causal discovery in high-dimensional and nonstationary multivariate time series. Causal-TS provides four specialized algorithms-CDNOTS, CDNOTS+, CEDAR, and GRACE-along with wrappers for GES, Granger, LASSO-VAR, and LGES, all sharing a unified conditional independence (CI) test layer with GPU acceleration via PyTorch. A regime discovery pipeline detects structural breaks via pluggable changepoint detectors and runs discovery per regime with regime-specific parameters. A command-line interface, synthetic data generators, and optional DoWhy integration provide an end-to-end pipeline from raw time series to causal effect estimates. The library is pip-installable, tested on Python 3.10--3.12, and available at https://github.com/bloomberg/causal-ts.

</details>


### [369] [Global Convergence of DGM and PINN Algorithms for Solving Nonlinear PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24726)
*Justin Sirignano,Konstantinos Spiliopoulos,Samuel Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于半线性偏微分方程，使用梯度下降训练神经网络来最小化PDE残差目标函数能够收敛到PDE解，为DGM和PINNs方法提供了数学理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DGM和PINNs等科学机器学习方法在求解偏微分方程时，由于PDE残差目标函数的非凸性，神经网络可能仅收敛到局部最小值而非真正的PDE解。因此需要建立这些算法的数学基础，证明训练的神经网络确实能够收敛到PDE解。

Method: 针对半线性偏微分方程（关于解及其一阶导数非线性的PDE），从理论上证明使用梯度下降最小化PDE残差目标函数的神经网络训练过程能够收敛到PDE解。

Result: 证明了对于半线性偏微分方程，通过梯度下降训练以最小化PDE残差目标函数的神经网络能够收敛到PDE解，从而建立了这些算法的理论收敛性保证。

Conclusion: 该研究为DGM和PINNs方法的数学基础提供了重要理论支持，解决了这些方法长期存在的理论基础问题，证明了在特定PDE类别下的收敛性保证。

Abstract: The Deep Galerkin Method (DGM) and Physics Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have become widely-used methods for solving partial differential equations (PDEs) in the rapidly growing field of scientific machine learning. In these methods, a neural network is trained to approximate the PDE solution by using (stochastic) gradient descent to minimize the PDE residual of the neural network. Due to the non-convexity of the PDE residual objective function, the trained neural network may, in principle, only converge to a local minimizer of the objective function (which would not be a solution of the PDE). Therefore, there is a longstanding question regarding the mathematical foundations of these algorithms, and it is highly valuable to establish that the trained neural network will converge to the PDE solution. For a class of semi-linear PDEs (nonlinear in the solution and its first derivative), we prove that neural networks trained with gradient descent to minimize the PDE residual objective function will converge to the PDE solution.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [370] [CAPS: Fine-Tuning CCA Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22821)
*Raphael Zailer,Isaac Keslassy*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: CAPS通过分离速率计算与包调度，利用ACK时钟同步将各流的数据包相位锁定至瓶颈链路时隙，在保持高吞吐的同时将最坏情况下的队列占用降低5-10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代传输协议将速率计算与包调度耦合在同一反馈回路中，虽能收敛至接近最优速率，但会形成随流数量增长的驻留队列。为了解决这一队列堆积问题，论文主张将二者分离，从而将剩余队列问题转化为时序问题：只要各流数据包在正确的时隙到达瓶颈链路，即可在维持链路利用率的同时保持队列为空。

Method: 提出CAPS（Clocked ACK-Paced Synchronization）轻量级分布式调度层。该方法将每个发送方的传输相位锁定到ACK时钟驱动的瓶颈时隙，并为每流添加校正机制以补偿异构RTT差异，从而实现"正确时隙到达"的调度目标。

Result: 在哑铃拓扑（等RTT、异构RTT及双向流量场景）和胖树拓扑（incast、置换及全连接流量场景）下验证了CAPS机制。结果显示，CAPS在所有测试场景中将最坏情况队列占用降低5-10倍，且无吞吐损失。

Conclusion: 分离速率计算与包调度的设计思路有效；CAPS通过ACK时钟相位同步实现了空队列稳态，证明了轻量级分布式调度层可在维持高吞吐与公平性的同时显著降低延迟，为数据中心拥塞控制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Data-center congestion control targets high throughput, fair bandwidth allocation, and low latency. Modern transports couple rate computation and packet scheduling into a single feedback loop, converging to near-optimal rates but leaving standing queues that can scale with the number of flows. We argue that separating the two reveals a simpler design point. Given stable feasible rates, the residual queue problem reduces to a timing problem: if every flow's packets arrive at the bottleneck in the correct slot, the link stays busy and the queue stays empty. Clocked ACK-Paced Synchronization CAPS is a lightweight distributed scheduling layer that achieves this by phase-locking each sender's transmissions to ACK-clocked bottleneck slots, with a per-flow correction that compensates for heterogeneous RTTs. We characterize the phase-locked steady state for dumbbell topologies under equal RTT, heterogeneous RTT, and bidirectional traffic, and validate the mechanism on a fat-tree under incast, permutation, and all-to-all traffic. CAPS reduces worst-case queue occupancy by 5-10x across all tested scenarios without throughput loss.

</details>


### [371] [Twin-Fidelity-Aware Resolution of Direct xApp Conflicts in Open RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22857)
*Akram Almohammedi,Mohammed Balfaqih,Sam Darshi,Rami Langar,Wael Jaafar*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数字孪生保真度监测的轻量级仲裁器，用于解决O-RAN中节能与覆盖/吞吐量优化xApp之间的功率设置冲突，在数字孪生发生漂移时自动切换策略，实现了接近最优的吞吐量-功耗权衡性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN架构允许独立开发的xApp通过Near-RT RIC控制RAN功能。当具有冲突目标的xApp并发运行时，可能发出不兼容的动作导致网络性能下降。具体而言，节能(ES) xApp与覆盖/吞吐导向(CTO) xApp会为同一小区请求不同的下行发射功率设置，形成直接冲突。现有基于网络数字孪生(NDT)的方法在孪生漂移时会失效。

Method: 将冲突解决建模为在线选择两个提案的连续混合，最大化联合考虑吞吐量和功耗的能效效用。提出孪生保真度感知的硬切换仲裁器：使用指数加权移动平均监测预测效用与观测效用之间的误差。当误差低于阈值时，采用NDT选择的动作；否则切换至在线学习的最佳历史观测动作。该方法轻量级、免训练、无需最优策略的先验知识。

Result: 系统级5G评估表明：在各种运营商标能效优先级下实现了最接近最优的吞吐量-功耗权衡，归一化效用遗憾为0.017±0.006（对比COMIX式孪生选择器的0.159±0.052）。在严重NDT漂移(10dB)情况下，将效用遗憾从11.19±3.58降至0.55±0.25。

Conclusion: 在线孪生保真度监测能够实现稳健的数字孪生辅助xApp冲突解决，同时保持效用感知的吞吐量-功耗优化。该方法为O-RAN中多目标xApp协调提供了实用且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) allows independently developed xApps to control RAN functions through the Near-Real-Time RAN Intelligent Controller (Near-RT RIC). When xApps with conflicting objectives operate concurrently, they may issue incompatible actions that degrade network performance. This paper addresses a direct conflict in which an energy-saving (ES) xApp and a coverage/throughput-oriented (CTO) xApp request different downlink transmit-power settings for the same cell. We formulate conflict resolution as online selection of a continuous blend of the two proposals, maximizing an energy-aware utility that jointly considers throughput and power consumption. A network digital twin (NDT) predicts this utility for candidate actions before live deployment, but selecting the highest twin-predicted utility becomes ineffective when the twin drifts. We therefore propose a twin-fidelity-aware hard-switching arbiter that monitors the error between predicted and observed utilities using an exponentially weighted moving average. While the error remains below a threshold, the arbiter follows the NDT-selected action; otherwise, it switches to the best previously observed action learned online. The arbiter is lightweight, training-free, and requires no oracle knowledge of the optimal policy. System-level 5G evaluations show that it achieves the closest throughput-power trade-off to the optimum across operator energy priorities, yielding normalized utility regret of $0.017 \pm 0.006$, versus $0.159 \pm 0.052$ for a COMIX-style twin-based selector. Under severe NDT drift (10 dB), it reduces utility regret from $11.19 \pm 3.58$ to $0.55 \pm 0.25$. These results show that online twin-fidelity monitoring enables robust digital-twin-assisted xApp conflict resolution while preserving utility-aware throughput-power optimization.

</details>


### [372] [Invariant Discovery for Networked Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22944)
*Hongyu Hè,Alexander Krentsel,Sylvia Ratnasamy,Maria Apostolaki*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了Autogram系统，利用AI自动发现网络不变量，解决了手工编写困难和现有方法只能学习精确规则的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络不变量对验证、流量生成、遥测插补等应用至关重要，但手工编写需要形式逻辑和网络领域的双重专业知识，且现有自动挖掘工具仍需人工提供语法输入，只能学习精确规则，难以处理现实数据中的噪声。

Method: 提出将不变量搜索问题分解为两个阶段：首先利用大语言模型（LLM）进行AI驱动的语法“发现”，然后在已学习语法内进行统计驱动的“搜索”。这种设计结合了AI的语义推理能力和统计方法的形式化保证。

Result: 在公共和生产遥测数据上评估Autogram，系统能以高覆盖率和低误报率恢复专家推导的不变量，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: Autogram成功实现了AI辅助的自动化不变量发现，降低了专业门槛，为网络管理提供了新工具，同时指出了通向完全开放式发现仍存在的开放性问题。

Abstract: Invariants, the relations expected to hold among measured signals of a network, underpin applications from verification to traffic generation, telemetry imputation, and input validation, yet writing them by hand demands rare expertise in both formal logic and networking. Automatic miners can help but fall short on two fronts: they still require the hardest input (the grammar of admissible invariants) and they learn only exact, ``hard'' rules, struggling with real-world approximation caused by inherent noise in data. LLMs are tools that can provide semantic reasoning over data, but are non-deterministic and opaque in their learning. Our key idea is to partition the invariant search problem into an AI-driven grammar ``discovery'' problem, followed by a statistics-driven ``search'' problem within the learned grammar. Taken together, this allows non-deterministic, hallucination-prone AI to help produce auditable invariants with formal guarantees. We design and implement such a system, Autogram, and evaluate it on both public and production telemetry data, recovering expert-derived invariants with high coverage and low false positives. We close with discussion on open problems on the path toward fully open-ended discovery.

</details>


### [373] [Building AI That Works: ESnet's Pragmatic Approach to AI-Driven Operational Excellence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22948)
*Bin Dong,Sukhada Gholba,Brooklin Gore,Shawn Kwang,David Mitchell,Samuel Oehlert,Garrett Stewart,Brendan White,Luke Baker,Ed Balas,Britt Gathright,Chin Guok,Jon-Paul Heron,John MacAuley,Scott Richmond,Chris Robb,Chris Tracy,Kesheng Wu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: ORBIT是一个集成到ServiceNow中的智能AI系统，通过模块化架构和版本化技能设计，成功解决了ESnet网络运营中心的运营痛点，提高了事故解决效率，并实现了广泛采用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ESnet网络运营中心面临三大运营痛点：(1)从孤岛数据源检索速度缓慢；(2)事件工单冗长且难以解析；(3)轮班交接时上下文信息丢失。这些问题增加了认知负荷，延长了事件解决时间，因此需要一种能够自动化日常任务、跨源综合信息并提供可操作性洞察的AI系统。

Method: ORBIT采用模块化分层架构，包含：(1)集中式推理中心；(2)通过MCP工具访问ESnet数据源；(3)语义搜索层；(4)面向操作员的聊天界面。系统将任务逻辑构建为版本化、经过测试的"技能"结构，引导系统执行有边界的职责任务，提高了可靠性和可预测性，相比完全不受约束的智能体行为更具优势。

Result: ORBIT成功交付了全部六个初始任务，架构设计使得快速开发NOC工程师提出的两个额外任务成为可能。通用基础设施组件（尤其是聊天界面和LiteLLM模型网关）获得了强烈的自然采用，包括项目外的高请求量。技能方法实验表明，该方案可以减少任务完成步骤，同时消除观察到的错误模式。

Conclusion: ORBIT项目证明了智能AI系统在网络运营中心场景中的实用价值，通过合理的架构设计和技能管理方法，成功实现了运营痛点的解决，并展现了良好的可扩展性和采用度，为ESnet 7计划提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: The ORBIT (Operations Responses and Business Intelligence Toolkit) project was initiated to assess agentic AI for the upcoming ESnet 7 initiative and to address persistent operational pain points in the Network Operations Center (NOC) workflow. ESnet operators experience slow retrieval from siloed data sources, incidents described in lengthy and difficult-to-parse tickets, and context loss across shift handoffs. These challenges increase cognitive load and prolong incident resolution times. ORBIT therefore targets routine automation, cross-source synthesis, and actionable insights delivered directly within operators' existing tooling.
  ORBIT is an agentic AI system integrated into ServiceNow, ESnet's primary incident management platform. The design uses a modular, layered architecture comprising a centralized reasoning hub, tool access via MCPs for ESnet data sources, a semantic search layer, and an operator-facing chat interface. To manage the complexity and stochasticity of the AI toolchain, ORBIT follows industry best practices by structuring task logic as versioned, tested "skills" that guide the system in performing bounded responsibilities. This improves reliability and predictability compared to fully unconstrained agent behavior.
  Key results show that ORBIT successfully delivered all six initial tasks, and the architecture enabled rapid development of two additional tasks proposed by NOC engineers. We observed strong organic adoption of general-purpose infrastructure components, especially the chat interface and LiteLLM model gateway, including high request volumes from outside the project. Experiments with skills indicate that this approach can reduce task completion steps while eliminating observed error modes.

</details>


### [374] [ISAC-Enabled On-Demand UAV Charging for Wireless Rechargeable Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23572)
*Muhammad Umar Farooq Qaisar,Lin Zhang,Paolo Bellavista,Shehzad Ashraf Chaudhry,Shamsher Ullah,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于ISAC的无人机按需充电框架，通过优先级充电队列和时间分配的部分充电策略，有效延长无线可充电传感器网络的寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线可充电传感器网络（WRSN）面临节点能量受限问题，现有无人机（UAV）充电方案缺乏对节点紧急程度、服务成本和移动不确定性的综合考虑，需要一种能够动态响应网络状态和UAV移动性的智能充电调度机制。

Method: 1) 设计集成感知与通信（ISAC）使能的按需UAV充电框架，由中心基站协调；2) 构建优先级充电队列，融合剩余能量、流量负载、UAV行程估计和飞行方向对齐度等指标；3) 利用ISAC估计UAV距离、速度和位置，动态更新行程时间预测；4) 采用时间分配的部分充电策略，根据节点关键性分配悬停时间。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该框架在能量使用效率、行程距离和充电延迟方面相比代表性基准方法均有显著改善，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过ISAC实现感知与通信的双向耦合，有效应对移动不确定性，为物联网场景下的WRSN充电部署提供了实用指导，包括计算开销、可扩展性和参数选择等实践考量。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) equipped with wireless power transfer (WPT) extend the lifetime of wireless rechargeable sensor networks (WRSNs) by delivering energy on demand. This article presents an integrated sensing and communication (ISAC)-enabled on-demand UAV charging framework coordinated by a central base station. A prioritized charging queue captures node urgency and service cost through residual energy, traffic load, estimated UAV travel time, and flight-direction alignment. This bidirectional coupling ensures that scheduling decisions shape the UAV trajectory, while updated mobility estimates from ISAC dynamically reorder the queue. ISAC-assisted estimation of UAV distance, speed, and position updates travel-time predictions under mobility uncertainty. A time-allocated partial charging policy distributes limited hover time across queued nodes according to criticality. Simulations show gains in energy usage efficiency, travel distance, and charging delay compared with representative baselines. We discuss deployment considerations, including computational overhead, scalability, and parameter selection, to aid practitioners evaluating the framework for IoT scenarios.

</details>


### [375] [TRUAV: Distributed Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Trajectory Planning and Routing Enhancement in UAV-Aided IoT-Enabled VANETs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23734)
*Muhammad Umar Farooq Qaisar,Lin Zhang,Zhen Chen,Wajdy Othman,Shehzad Ashraf Chaudhry,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出TRUAV框架，基于独立表格Q学习的分布式多智能体强化学习方法，用于UAV辅助VANET的轨迹规划和路由增强，在保证网络性能的同时显著提升能效并降低中继延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集中式UAV轨迹规划方法需要持续的全局网络状态聚合，在带宽和能量受限的密集城市部署场景下不切实际，亟需一种仅依赖局部观测信息的分布式解决方案。

Method: 提出TRUAV分布式多智能体强化学习框架，每个UAV配备基于独立表格Q学习的本地智能体，仅利用车辆密度、数据包队列状态、邻居UAV位置等局部可观测信息进行决策；采用势博弈启发的奖励设计，在考虑能耗的同时促进智能体间的空间多样性和路由感知定位。

Result: 在包含200个移动车辆的大规模城市场景仿真中，TRUAV在网络覆盖率和数据包投递率方面达到与集中式深度强化学习方法相当的性能，同时在中继延迟和能效方面表现更优。

Conclusion: TRUAV框架成功实现了UAV轨迹规划的分布式控制，避免了全局状态交换开销，为分布式多智能体UAV辅助IoT系统提供了可行的解决方案，并指出该领域的未来研究挑战与方向。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) have emerged as a key enabler of next-generation Internet of Things (IoT) ecosystems, offering flexible aerial relaying to extend connectivity across dynamic vehicular ad hoc networks (VANETs) in smart city environments. However, conventional centralized approaches for UAV trajectory planning require continuous global network state aggregation, making them impractical under bandwidth and energy constraints typical of dense urban deployments. In this article, we present TRUAV, a distributed multi-agent reinforcement learning framework based on independent tabular Q-learning for joint UAV trajectory planning and routing enhancement in UAV-aided VANETs. Each UAV is equipped with a local Q-learning agent that operates purely on locally observable information, including vehicle density, packet queue states, and neighbor UAV positions, thereby eliminating the need for global state exchange. A potential-game-inspired reward design encourages spatial diversity and routing-aware UAV positioning among interacting agents while accounting for energy consumption. Numerical simulations over a large urban area with 200 mobile vehicles show that the proposed TRUAV framework achieves network coverage and packet delivery ratios comparable to centralized deep reinforcement learning methods, while also improving relay delay and energy efficiency. Finally, we discuss emerging challenges and future research directions for distributed multi-agent UAV-assisted IoT systems.

</details>


### [376] [Fake It No More: Evaluating L4S with SCReAM on Video Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23767)
*Nawel Alioua,Ryan Zanone,Cheng Xi,Elizabeth Belding*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过实验评估了L4S对SCReAM视频流量的影响，发现L4S显著降低了队列延迟，但降低了吞吐量；在丢包场景下带来更明显的QoE提升，并在不同视频复杂度下保持更稳定的QoE表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着低延迟、低损耗和可扩展吞吐量（L4S）技术的日益关注，交互式多媒体应用对更低延迟的需求迫切。现有研究缺乏对L4S在实际视频流量场景下对网络层指标和用户体验质量（QoE）影响的全面评估。

Method: 基于Mahimahi仿真器使用开源DualPI2实现，通过扩展SCReAM BW工具集成视频编解码器以生成真实视频流量。在移动网络轨迹、随机丢包和不同运动复杂度条件下，系统评估网络层指标（队列延迟、吞吐量）和QoE指标。

Result: 基准场景中，L4S将95百分位队列延迟中位数降低35%，但发送端吞吐量下降42%。1%丢包率下，L4S相比无丢包场景产生更显著的QoE增益。在不同视频内容复杂度下，L4S相比Classic队列维持更稳定的QoE表现。

Conclusion: 在评估L4S对终端用户应用性能的影响时，必须同时考虑QoE指标和网络层指标，单纯关注网络层指标可能无法全面反映实际用户体验。

Abstract: The growing interest in Low Latency, Low Loss, and Scalable Throughput (L4S) reflects the need for lower latency in interactive multimedia applications. In this paper, we use an open-source DualPI2 implementation over the Mahimahi emulator to evaluate the impact of L4S on SCReAM congestion controlled video traffic. To do so, we augment the SCReAM BW tool with a video codec, enabling the generation of video traffic in addition to its original synthetic RTP mode. We evaluate both network-level and Quality of Experience (QoE) metrics on a mobile network trace, under random packet loss, and with different motion-complexity levels. In our baseline scenario, L4S reduces the median per-run $95^{th}$ percentile queue delay by 35%, at the cost of a 42% drop in sender throughput. Under 1% packet loss, L4S yields more pronounced QoE gains compared to the lossless scenario, despite narrower network-level benefits. Across video content complexities, L4S also maintains more stable QoE than Classic. These results underscore the importance of evaluating QoE alongside network-level metrics when assessing the effect of L4S on end-user application performance.

</details>


### [377] [GNN-based Multi-Agent Control of Traffic Shockwaves in Sparse Vehicular Ad-hoc Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23792)
*Prachi Nandi,Madhuri Malakar,Sonakshi Satpathy,Pabitra Mohan Khilar*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络的多智能体强化学习框架，使联网自动驾驶车辆能够仅利用局部信息协同控制，有效抑制交通冲击波传播，在渗透率仅10%时即可减少80%的冲击波传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通冲击波是导致交通拥堵、燃油效率低下和事故率增加的主要原因之一。虽然联网自动驾驶车辆为缓解冲击波提供了机会，但现有控制策略大多依赖全局交通状态信息，在车辆自组织网络早期部署阶段难以实际应用，因此需要开发仅依赖局部信息的实用化控制方案。

Method: 提出了一种去中心化的多智能体强化学习框架，将图神经网络集成到联网自动驾驶车辆的控制架构中。该方法使车辆能够利用局部可用信息以及与邻近车辆的交互来学习协同控制策略，无需全局交通状态信息。

Result: 在可扩展的仿真环境中，基于真实高速公路交通条件进行了评估。仿真结果表明，即使在联网车辆渗透率仅为10%的情况下，所提出的基于GNN的MARL框架也能将交通冲击波的传播减少高达80%。

Conclusion: 所提出的去中心化GNN-MARL框架为早期VANET部署阶段提供了一种实用的解决方案，能够在低渗透率条件下显著抑制交通冲击波，展现了良好的实用性和有效性。

Abstract: Traffic shockwaves are stop-and-go waves that propagate upstream through the streams of vehicles and are one of the major causes of traffic congestion, fuel inefficiency, and increased accident rates in modern transportation systems. Although Connected and Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs) offer a promising opportunity to mitigate such shockwaves, most existing control strategies rely on global traffic state information, making them impractical for early-stage deployment of Vehicular Ad-hoc Networks (VANETs). In this paper, we propose a decentralized Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework that integrates a Graph Neural Network (GNN) to enhance the control architecture of connected and autonomous vehicles. The proposed approach enables vehicles to learn cooperative control policies using locally available information and interaction with neighboring vehicles. The effectiveness of the proposed scheme is evaluated using a scalable simulation environment under realistic highway traffic conditions. Simulation results show that the proposed GNN-based MARL framework can reduce the propagation of traffic shockwaves by up to 80\%, even when only 10\% of the vehicles are connected.

</details>


### [378] [HAPS-enabled Downlink Coverage Enhancement in Islands and Maritime Areas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23848)
*Hao Lin,Mustafa A. Kishk,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大规模高空平台站（HAPS）部署连接岛屿和海上用户的可行性，考虑了岛屿建筑群对HAPS的现实阴影效应，分析了覆盖性能并提出了降低计算复杂度的近似方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非地面网络（NTN）在下一代移动通信中具有关键作用，高空平台站（HAPS）作为为欠发达地区（农村、岛屿、海事区域）提供互联网连接的解决方案，传统基础设施部署成本高且困难。需要研究大规模HAPS部署的可行性及其在实际阴影效应下的覆盖性能。

Method: 1. 分析在岸（岛屿）和离岸（远海）用户的覆盖性能，HAPS与用户之间的信道分别遵循阴影Rician分布和Rician分布；2. 引入近岸用户在混合信道环境下的评估方法；3. 提出可降低计算复杂度的近似方法；4. 基于仿真结果分析岛屿边界距离对覆盖性能的影响。

Result: 揭示了岛屿边界距离（即海上用户的相对偏远程度）在不同HAPS密度下对覆盖性能的影响，强调了选择平衡的HAPS密度或先进HAPS部署方案的重要性。

Conclusion: 大规模HAPS部署连接岛屿和海上用户具有可行性，需要考虑现实阴影效应，选择合适的HAPS密度或部署方案以优化覆盖性能。

Abstract: Non-terrestrial networks (NTNs) are poised to play a critical role in next-generation mobile communications, offering enhanced flexibility, improved line-of-sight (LoS) conditions, and overcoming the limitations of terrestrial networks (TNs). Among NTN platforms, high altitude platform stations (HAPSs) have emerged as a promising solution to provide Internet connectivity to underserved regions, including rural areas, islands, and maritime zones, where traditional infrastructure deployment is costly and challenging to deploy. In this paper, we investigate the feasibility of large-scale HAPS deployment to connect island and maritime users, considering real-world shadowing effects on part of HAPSs caused by the presence of island building clusters. We first analyze the coverage performance of onshore (island) and offshore (remote sea) users, in which the channels between HAPSs and the user follow the shadowed Rician distributions and Rician distributions, respectively. Next, we introduce an evaluation method for nearshore users in a hybrid channel environment with HAPSs, and propose approximations that can reduce computational complexity. Based on the simulation results, we discuss how the distance from the island boundary (i.e. the relative remoteness of maritime users) affects coverage performance under different HAPS densities. We also emphasize the importance of choosing a balanced HAPS density or an advanced HAPS deployment scheme.

</details>


### [379] [6G: From Connectivity Infrastructure to Guaranteed Digital Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24185)
*David Soldani,Petrit Nahi,Awn Muhammad,Nikhil Dwivedi,Francesco Monaco,Francis Jebamani*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文主张6G网络应从厂商主导架构转向运营商主导架构，通过控制优先、结果导向、治理AI代理等原则，提出控制契约、保障经济、网络MCP平台和标准化路线图四大贡献，实现架构主权和服务保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 五代厂商主导的网络架构使运营商陷入被动依赖：无法完全修改平台，无法审计AI推理层。随着6G到来，这种结构性失衡需要被扭转，运营商应重新获得架构主权和可控性。

Method: 提出四个核心贡献：(1)控制契约-通过自有-联合-消费分类法分配架构主权；(2)保障经济-六层结果定价模型对齐IMT-2030场景；(3)运营商级网络MCP平台-通过受治理工具平面引入自主代理；(4)标准化路线图-涵盖AI代理、RAN、核心网等领域。采用三阶段路线图区分标准里程碑与部署目标。

Result: 构建了将运营商控制转化为可执行服务级别目标的框架；证明了自主代理可通过受治理工具平面以可审计方式进入服务架构；提供了从运营证据、标准推演到前瞻架构的多层次验证；明确了运营商部署目标与标准制定的分离路径。

Conclusion: 6G必须优先考虑控制权而非峰值速率，通过架构主权、保障经济和受治理AI代理实现运营商价值重塑，最终形成可审计、可执行、以客户结果为导向的网络架构新范式。

Abstract: Sixth-generation mobile networks are approaching a structural inflection point. Five generations of vendor-led architecture have left operators dependent on platforms they cannot fully modify and artificial-intelligence inference layers they cannot audit. This article argues that 6G should reverse that trajectory by reordering five priorities: control first; customer outcomes before peak rates; business guarantees before megabytes; software-driven operations with governed agentic artificial intelligence; and technology in service of those priorities. Four contributions operationalize the thesis. The Control Compact is an own-federate-consume taxonomy that allocates architectural sovereignty by strategic value. The Guarantee Economy is a six-tier outcome-priced model aligned with IMT-2030 usage scenarios and converts operator control into enforceable service-level objectives. An operator-grade Network MCP Platform shows how autonomous agents could enter the service-based architecture through a governed tool plane with auditable hooks for identity, charging, lawful intercept, enforcement, and digital-twin validation. A standardization section states Rakuten Mobile's public position on AI-agent scope, radio access, migration, non-terrestrial networks, physical layer, core, and spectrum. The framework distinguishes operational evidence from national-scale cloud-native Open RAN and core network deployments, standards-grounded extrapolation, and forward-looking architecture and commercial proposals. A three-phase roadmap separates standards milestones from operator deployment targets and identifies validation gates and stakeholder implications.

</details>


### [380] [DeepNC: A Fast GNN-based Pre-Verification Surrogate for TSN Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24398)
*Jiayi Zhu,Jing Lin,Zelong Tian,Feng He,Luxi Zhao*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出DeepNC，一种将网络演算原理与图神经网络结构化融合的预验证代理模块，通过高精度预测大幅减少正式验证调用次数，显著加速时间敏感网络的配置空间探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时间敏感网络（TSN）的安全关键领域中，网络演算（NC）等形式化验证是可调度性保证的核心。然而，在自动化配置空间探索过程中，重复的可调度性分析消耗了超过90%的配置时间，成为大规模TSN配置的主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出DeepNC，一种新颖的预验证代理模块，首次将网络演算原理结构化融合到图神经网络（GNN）中。DeepNC并非替代形式化验证，而是作为高速预验证过滤器，仅对有希望的候选配置保留计算昂贵的形式化验证。

Result: 广泛评估表明，DeepNC显著提升了最坏情况延迟预测精度：相比最先进的基于学习的方法，平均R²提升55.8%，平均MAPE降低65.3%。更重要的是，其高保真回归将配置空间探索期间的形式化验证调用次数减少了93.25%，同时将基于NC的验证加速超过两个数量级。

Conclusion: DeepNC成功地将网络演算原理与图神经网络相结合，作为高效的预验证过滤器，在保持高预测精度的同时，大幅降低了形式化验证的计算开销，为大规模TSN配置提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Time-Sensitive Networking (TSN) is critical to deterministic communication in safety-critical domains, with formal verification such as Network Calculus (NC) serving as the cornerstone for schedulability guarantees. However, during automated configuration-space exploration, repeated schedulability analysis consumes over 90% of the total configuration time, becoming the primary bottleneck for large-scale TSN configurations. To address this challenge, we propose DeepNC, a novel pre-verification surrogate module that pioneers the structural fusion of NC principles into a Graph Neural Network (GNN) for TSN configuration-space exploration. Rather than replacing formal verification, DeepNC acts as a high-speed pre-verification filter, reserving computationally expensive formal verification only for promising candidates. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that DeepNC significantly improves worst-case delay prediction accuracy over state-of-the-art learning-based methods, increasing the average $R^2$ by 55.8% and reducing the average MAPE by 65.3%. More importantly, its high-fidelity regression substantially reduces the number of formal verification calls during configuration-space exploration by 93.25%, while accelerating NC-based verification by more than two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [381] [Denial of Deadline: Network-Driven Accuracy Collapse in Distributed Inference Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24692)
*Jhonatan Tavori,Gur-Eyal Sela,Ion Stoica,Gil Zussman*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种针对快慢路径推理系统的新型攻击面——整形工作负载攻击（如Yo-Yo突发），可通过竞争共享资源导致慢路径预测超时被丢弃，造成精度崩溃，使目标跟踪质量平均下降7.0 HOTA点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推理系统普遍采用快慢路径架构，其中快路径在本地快速返回预测，慢路径在云端提供更高精度的结果。这种架构引入了新的协调层（包括路由器和合并器），暴露出新的攻击面。作者发现攻击者可通过构造特定工作负载模式，利用慢路径资源竞争使正常用户的预测超时，从而丧失慢路径带来的精度优势。

Method: 作者提出了“精度崩溃”（accuracy collapse）概念，并设计了整形工作负载攻击（如Yo-Yo突发）。在自动驾驶边缘-云端双层多目标跟踪流水线中进行验证，通过约4000个突发式请求模拟攻击场景，分析攻击对正常用户p99延迟和预测质量的影响，并评估不同视频片段和目标类别下的攻击效果差异。

Result: 攻击使正常请求的p99延迟从92ms增至2秒，慢路径预测几乎全部超时被丢弃；目标跟踪质量平均下降7.0 HOTA点，不同视频片段降幅为2.0-18.7 HOTA点；稀有类别（如停止标志）预测精度损失近半。攻击无需访问模型权重或受害者数据即可显著降低预测质量。

Conclusion: 该研究表明快慢路径推理系统的协调层存在新型安全漏洞，整形工作负载攻击可有效破坏系统精度优势。作者建议在未来研究中关注路由、合并、调度和资源隔离等环节的攻击与防御机制，以保障此类新兴推理架构的安全性。

Abstract: Inference systems increasingly combine a fast path that returns predictions within the application's latency deadline together with a higher-accuracy slow path that runs higher-compute methods on stronger, remote hardware, so its results can be returned on time and combined with the fast path predictions. Across several application domains, we abstract this inference architecture as a fast path, a slow path, and a coordination layer with two functions: a router that invokes the slow path and a merger that decides whether to incorporate its returned predictions. In this work, we show that this new coordination layer exposes a new attack surface: shaped workload attacks, e.g., Yo-Yo bursts, can exploit contention at shared resources along the slow path to push benign users' slow-path predictions past their latency deadlines. The merger then discards those predictions, while the fast path continues to return timely outputs. We refer to the resulting loss of slow-path accuracy benefits as accuracy collapse.
  We demonstrate accuracy collapse in a two-tier edge-cloud multi-object tracking pipeline in autonomous driving. In simulation, approximately 4,000 burst-shaped requests increase benign p99 latency from 92ms to 2s, nearly eliminating the benefit of the slow path's cloud inference, reducing object tracking quality by 7.0 HOTA points on average. We further find that accuracy degradation can significantly vary (2.0-18.7 HOTA points), depending on the video intervals that are targeted in the attack, and that certain rare classes (e.g., stop signs) lose nearly half of their pre-attack prediction accuracy. These results show that workload attacks can degrade prediction quality without needing either access to model weights or victim data, and motivate research on attacks and defenses for routing, merging, scheduling, and resource isolation in these emerging inference pipeline architectures.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [382] [$g$MAGNUS: Fast SpGEMM on GPUs for Irregular Matrices via Hierarchical Multisplit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22866)
*Jordi Wolfson-Pou,Ahmed Helal,Fabrizio Petrini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了gMAGNUS算法，通过行内重排序将重行分块处理，解决了GPU上不规则稀疏矩阵乘法中的内存溢出问题，实现了1.81-7.62倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU上不规则稀疏矩阵的SpGEMM计算中常存在"重行"（heavy rows），这些行的中间乘积过大，导致本地存储累加器溢出到全局存储器，严重影响性能。现有算法无法有效处理这一问题。

Method: 提出了gMAGNUS算法，核心创新包括：(1) 计算行内重排序，将重行细分为独立的块，使每块能在本地存储器中完全累加；(2) 采用新型外积和层次多分裂操作实现重排序；(3) 输入和系统感知设计，根据输入矩阵维度和本地存储器大小自动确定块数和多分裂层级。

Result: 在两个大规模数据集上的实验表明，gMAGNUS在Intel Ponte Vecchio和NVIDIA H200上相比5种主流算法（包括MKL和cuSPARSE）实现了1.81到7.62倍的几何平均加速。核心内核性能接近理论峰值。

Conclusion: gMAGNUS成功解决了GPU上不规则稀疏矩阵乘法中的重行问题，通过行内重排序和自适应分块策略显著提升了性能，在多种硬件平台上均展现出优越的计算效率。

Abstract: We present $g$MAGNUS, a novel algorithm for sparse matrix-matrix multiplication (SpGEMM) of irregular matrices on GPUs. Such matrices often contain many \emph{heavy rows}, those with large intermediate products that force local memory accumulators to spill to global memory. $g$MAGNUS addresses this by computing an intra-row reordering of intermediate products, subdividing heavy rows into independent chunks that can be accumulated completely in local memory. This reordering uses novel outer product and hierarchical multisplit operations. The algorithm is input- and system-aware, automatically determining the number of chunks and multisplit levels based on the input matrix dimensions and local memory size. Experimental results on two extensive datasets show that $g$MAGNUS achieves a geometric-mean speedup of 1.81 to 7.62$\times$ over five leading algorithms (including MKL and cuSPARSE) on Intel Ponte Vecchio and NVIDIA H200. Additionally, the core kernels of $g$MAGNUS are evaluated, achieving near-peak performance compared to their theoretical upper bound.

</details>


### [383] [Application-Driven Architecture Exploration for Cross-Layer Heterogeneous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23042)
*Yuchen Fan,Minghong Sun,Jikui Ma,Yunpeng Xu,Shunyu Mao,Liu He,Shunan Dong,Jiahao Yang,Yu Zhu,Xinhao Yang,Tianyan Zhong,Haoran Sun,Daoqi Liu,Zongle Huang,Xinyuan Lin,Huazhong Yang,Maokun Li,Yongpan Liu,Yu Wang,Zhenhua Zhu,Hongyang Jia,Shuwen Deng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了CHASE框架，用于探索跨层异构系统（XHS）架构设计空间。通过两级解耦搜索方法，避免了硬件-映射联合搜索的复杂性，在稀疏计算和LLM工作负载上实现了显著的性能提升和成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI和HPC基础设施日益需要支持包含密集张量计算、稀疏内核、大内存占用和通信密集型集合操作的工作负载组合。支持这些工作负载需要在加速器、内存层级、扩展网络和集群网络之间进行协调选择，由此产生的跨层异构系统（XHS）设计空间难以探索：硬件选择会改变合法的任务映射，而机架功率、交换机基数、布线和成本约束会使许多候选方案无效。

Method: CHASE采用分层类型图表示候选架构，拒绝违反部署约束的设计。为避免不可行的硬件-映射联合搜索，采用解耦的两级循环：内层映射器将硬件无关的工作负载DAG转换为拓扑感知的事件轨迹，校准的事件驱动模拟器评估每个映射；外层遥测引导的优化器演化硬件图。

Result: 在稀疏计算和LLM工作负载上的评估显示：映射器保持在与穷举最优值6.06%的范围内，同时平均减少60.5%的映射时间；计算模型误差平均为4.4-7.5%，通信验证复现了物理平台上的关键趋势；外层搜索在64次迭代内达到近全局最优。端到端案例研究表明稀疏工作负载偏好关键性感知的异构pod，而LLM推理偏好扩展孤岛；最终设计分别实现6.20倍和2.12倍的几何平均加速，同时降低成本和功耗。

Conclusion: CHASE框架成功解决了跨层异构系统设计空间探索的挑战，通过应用驱动的方法在物理可行的约束下找到接近全局最优的架构设计，为不同类型工作负载提供了定制化的系统设计方案，实现了性能、成本和功耗的协同优化。

Abstract: AI and HPC infrastructure increasingly serves workload portfolios that combine dense tensor computation, sparse kernels, large memory footprints, and communication-intensive collectives. Supporting these portfolios requires coordinated choices across accelerators, memory tiers, scale-up fabrics, and cluster networks. The resulting Cross-layer Heterogeneous System (XHS) design space is difficult to explore: hardware choices change legal task mappings, while rack power, switch radix, cabling, and cost constraints invalidate many candidates. We present CHASE, an application-driven framework that searches physically feasible XHS architectures through the workloads they must execute. CHASE represents candidates as hierarchical typed graphs and rejects designs that violate deployment constraints. It avoids intractable joint hardware-mapping search with a decoupled two-level loop: an inner mapper translates hardware-independent workload DAGs into topology-aware event traces, a calibrated event-driven simulator evaluates each mapping, and an outer telemetry-guided optimizer evolves the hardware graph. We evaluate CHASE on sparse-computing and LLM workloads. Its mapper remains within 6.06% of exhaustive optima while reducing mapping time by 60.5% on average relative to PEFT. Compute-model errors average 4.4-7.5%, and communication validation reproduces key trends across physical platforms. The outer search reaches near-global optima within 64 iterations. End-to-end case studies show that sparse workloads favor criticality-aware heterogeneous pods, whereas LLM inference favors scale-up islands; the resulting designs deliver 6.20$\times$ and 2.12$\times$ geomean speedups, respectively, while reducing cost and power relative to the baselines.

</details>


### [384] [Gleam: Adaptive Network-Efficient CUDA API Remoting for Cross-Device GPU Sharing over LANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23115)
*Zhihao Xu,Hao Zhong,Zeting Zhou,Yuhang Xu,Haoyu Tong,Wei Wang,Jinshan Chen,Keqiang He,Chong Zhu,Shengzhong Liu,Fan Wu,Guihai Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Gleam框架，通过CUDA API远程调用实现局域网内跨设备的GPU共享，解决了网络带宽、高频API调用和任务竞争等瓶颈问题，在AI推理任务中显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现异构个人设备上普适的AI推理，需要支持局域网内跨设备的高效GPU共享。虽然通过CUDA API远程调用可以实现分布式任务卸载，但网络约束（有限带宽、高频API调用、跨任务竞争）成为主要性能瓶颈，亟需解决。

Method: 提出Gleam框架，包含三个核心技术：1）通过自动模型权重缓存减少CUDA API远程调用的带宽开销，并通过异步执行缓解频繁API调用导致的累积延迟；2）设计运行时任务调度器，动态确定局域网客户端与服务器间的API远程调用配对，显式考虑网络条件和并行工作负载下的GPU资源竞争；3）引入专用机制确保分布式执行中的CUDA上下文一致性。
