Result: 在异构NVIDIA GPU和多样化AI工作负载上的广泛实验表明，Gleam持续优于最先进基线，在API远程调用效率上实现1.4-24.2倍的提升，系统吞吐量提升高达1.79倍。

Conclusion: Gleam成功实现了局域网内计算和通信高效的GPU共享，有效解决了网络约束瓶颈，为异构个人设备上的普适AI推理提供了可行的解决方案，显著提升了系统性能和资源利用效率。

Abstract: This paper aims to enable computation- and communication-efficient GPU sharing across devices within local area networks (LANs), facilitating ubiquitous AI inference on heterogeneous personal devices. We achieve distributed task offloading via CUDA API remoting. However, beyond raw computation, network constraints emerge as the primary bottleneck: limited bandwidth, high-frequency API invocations, and cross-task contention significantly hinder performance. To address these challenges, we propose Gleam, a novel and network-efficient framework for task-generic GPU sharing across local-area CUDA devices, with three key contributions. First, we reduce bandwidth overhead in CUDA API remoting through automatic model weight caching, and mitigate accumulated latency from frequent API calls by asynchronous execution. Second, we design a runtime task scheduler that dynamically determines API remoting pairs between LAN clients and servers, explicitly accounting for both network conditions and GPU resource contention under parallel workloads. Finally, we introduce dedicated mechanisms to ensure CUDA context consistency across distributed executions. Extensive experiments on heterogeneous NVIDIA GPUs and diverse AI workloads show Gleam consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving 1.4-24.2 times improvements in API remoting efficiency and up to 1.79 times higher system throughput.

</details>


### [385] [Libra: Taming Attention Workload Skew in Long-Context LLM Training with Bounded Sequence Pool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23250)
*Yan Wang,Xiulong Yuan,Kaiming Yang,Jiaxuan Peng,Pengju Lu,Mingzhen Li,Zhipeng Zhang,Chang Si,Zhixiang Ruan,Hongqing Chen,Linlang Jiang,Siyu Wang,Langshi Chen,Rui Men,Man Yuan,Guangming Tan,Yong Li,Weile Jia,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Libra系统，通过将序列和CP组分组到固定大小的序列池来解决长上下文LLM训练中的负载不平衡问题，无需随着DP规模扩大而扩大注意力交换池，在Qwen3-Turbo训练中实现了相比Ulysses最高2.54倍的端到端吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM训练存在负载均衡问题：虽然序列打包可以平衡内存和线性成本算子，但主导的注意力成本与序列长度平方和成正比，导致来自长尾语料库的等长打包序列承载显著不同的注意力负载，造成数据并行滞后者和流水线气泡。现有方法要么在序列或微批次粒度上平衡（异常值会主导分配），要么在随DP度增长的全局工作池上解耦注意力。

Method: Libra基于大数定律作为负载均衡的缩放原理，将打包序列及其CP组分组到固定大小的序列池中。随着DP扩展，Libra添加池而非扩大单个池，限制每次注意力交换。通过方差减少序列放置(Variance-Reduced Sequence Placement)将互补注意力负载的序列共置以减少池间偏斜。池内使用分块注意力池化(Tiled Attention Pooling)将序列头SH-Tiles分派到GPU，流水线运行时重叠分块交换与注意力计算。提供即插即用的上下文并行注意力算子和可插拔数据采样器，无需更改模型层、优化器或流水线调度。

Result: 在Qwen3-Turbo训练的256K和1M token工作负载上，Libra相比Ulysses实现最高2.54倍的端到端吞吐量提升，微基准测试中实现最高3.14倍的最坏步骤滞后注意力加速。Libra已在生产环境中运行数十万GPU小时，支持32K到1M token的任务，同时保持训练语义。

Conclusion: Libra通过将大数定律操作化为缩放原理，有效解决了长上下文LLM训练中的负载均衡问题，实现了显著的性能提升和良好的生产可用性，为大规模长上下文训练提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context LLM training suffers from a load-balancing problem that sequence packing does not solve. Packing samples into fixed-token sequences balances memory and linear-cost operators, but the dominant attention cost scales with the sum of squared sequence lengths. Thus, equally sized packed sequences drawn from a long-tailed corpus can carry substantially different attention workloads, creating data-parallel stragglers and pipeline bubbles. Existing approaches either balance at the granularity of sequences or microbatches, where an outlier can dominate an assignment, or disaggregate attention over a global worker pool whose communication domain grows with the data-parallel (DP) degree.
  We present Libra, which operationalizes the law of large numbers (LLN) as a scaling principle for load balancing: the attention-balancing pool need not grow with the DP degree. Libra groups packed sequences and their CP groups into fixed-size sequence pools. As DP scales out, Libra adds pools rather than enlarging each one, bounding every attention exchange. Variance-Reduced Sequence Placement makes this effective for finite, long-tailed workloads by co-locating sequences with complementary attention workloads to reduce residual inter-pool skew. Within each pool, Tiled Attention Pooling dispatches sequence-head SH-Tiles across GPUs, while a pipelined runtime overlaps tile exchange with attention.
  Libra exposes a drop-in context-parallel attention operator and a pluggable data sampler, requiring no changes to model layers, optimizers, or pipeline schedules. On Qwen3-Turbo training with 256K- and 1M-token workloads, Libra improves end-to-end throughput by up to 2.54x over Ulysses, with up to 3.14x worst-step straggler-attention speedup in microbenchmarks. Libra has run for hundreds of thousands of GPU-hours in production on jobs spanning 32K to 1M tokens while preserving training semantics.

</details>


### [386] [X-Stage: An Overlooked Pipeline Stage for Communication-Computation Overlap in DiT Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23264)
*Jianwen Xian,Zhiyuan Xu,Yuchen Li,Ziliang Lai,Kang He,Zhen Huang,Aichen Feng,Jinyan Chen,Yilin Zhang,Qinqin Chen,Chengru Song*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式扩散变换器（DiT）推理中的设备发起通信问题，识别出X-Stage这一软件可见的后发射流水线阶段，提出Burst-Gap模型预测反压发生，并基于该模型优化了两个通信-计算融合内核，分别实现了最高1.62x和1.43x的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式DiT推理中，持久GPU内核通过细粒度设备发起通信实现远程存储，并将数据移动与Tensor Core计算重叠。然而现有系统仅调度通信发射和接收数据的可用时间，却忽略了远程可见完成之前的后发射进度，导致发送端反压难以预测。

Method: （1）识别X-Stage——一个软件可见的后发射流水线阶段，并通过八GPU节点测量分析短远程存储突发与持续注入的行为差异；（2）建立轻量级Burst-Gap模型，以反压无关发射时间、有效排放率和未完成容量为参数预测发射开销、突发间恢复和反压发生时机；（3）基于模型重新设计两个通信-计算融合内核：DeepGEMM MegaMoE通过跨专家波交错Linear-1/Linear-2工作，Ulysses序列并行注意力通过输出块粒度融合All-to-All与FlashAttention。

Result: DeepGEMM MegaMoE在84种配置下相比Expert-Wave基线获得几何平均1.18x、最高1.62x的内核加速。FlashAttention-3和FlashAttention-4在序列并行注意力中分别达到最高1.43x和1.42x的发送端可见加速，长序列下稳态时间接近纯FlashAttention。

Conclusion: 后发射进度是一个可量化的调度杠杆，可用于塑造通信突发、避免反压并隐藏发送端开销。本文为分布式DiT推理中的细粒度通信调度提供了可预测的建模方法和实用的优化策略。

Abstract: Fine-grained, device-initiated communication lets persistent GPU kernels in distributed diffusion transformer (DiT) inference issue remote stores and overlap data movement with Tensor Core computation. Existing systems schedule when communication is issued and when received data becomes consumable, but omit post-issue progress before remote-visible completion, making sender backpressure hard to predict.
  We identify X-Stage, a software-visible post-issue pipeline stage. Measurements on an eight-GPU node with a recent NVIDIA architecture show that short remote-store bursts drain as the issuer resumes work, whereas sustained injection exhausts finite outstanding capacity and delays later issues. A lightweight Burst-Gap model parameterized by backpressure-free issue time, effective drain rate, and outstanding capacity predicts issue overhead, recovery between bursts, and the onset of backpressure.
  Guided by the model, we redesign two communication-computation fused kernels. For DeepGEMM MegaMoE, interleaving Linear-1 and Linear-2 work across expert waves places computation between concentrated remote-store bursts, yielding a 1.18x geometric-mean and 1.62x maximum kernel speedup over the Expert-Wave baseline across 84 configurations. For Ulysses sequence-parallel attention, tile-granular fusion of the post-attention All-to-All with FlashAttention lets an output-tile owner issue remote stores and resume computation without a dedicated communication warp or streaming multiprocessor. FlashAttention-3 and FlashAttention-4 reach maximum sender-visible speedups of 1.43x and 1.42x over serial execution, and at long sequences their steady-state times approach those of FlashAttention alone. These results establish post-issue progress as a measurable scheduling lever for shaping bursts, avoiding backpressure, and hiding sender-side overhead.

</details>


### [387] [SpecBox: Speculative Sandbox Scheduling for Efficient LLM Agent Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23933)
*Yihui Zhang,Tianyu Wo,Jinghao Wang,Xiaoyang Sun,Menghao Zhang,Cangzhou Yuan,Li Li,Chunming Hu,Albert Y. Zomaya,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecBox运行时系统，通过推测性沙箱预分配技术解决LLM代理中资源利用率与交互尾延迟之间的矛盾，显著降低延迟和内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理依赖模型上下文协议调用外部沙箱时，分散式沙箱部署在资源利用率和交互尾延迟之间存在根本性矛盾：持久长生命周期沙箱预留会产生过高的内存开销，而懒惰按需实例化会产生严重的冷启动惩罚，影响多租户多轮代理工作负载下的响应性能。

Method: SpecBox实现关键词匹配和流式语义嵌入来支持意图驱动的沙箱预热，在LLM令牌生成过程中识别待执行的工具需求，将沙箱引导与模型推理完全重叠；利用沙箱依赖图上的上下文感知随机预取来概率性预测未来沙箱切换；还引入语义结果缓存来消除冗余沙箱调用，以及带外共享内存传输平面来实现零拷贝数据传输。

Result: 在高并发多轮代理轨迹上评估，SpecBox相对于按需沙箱基线将P99端到端延迟降低2.9倍，相比永久预留沙箱部署将峰值内存消耗减少45.9%。

Conclusion: SpecBox通过推测性沙箱预分配及相关优化技术有效解决了LLM代理中沙箱部署的性能与资源权衡问题，显著提升了系统效率。

Abstract: As LLM agents increasingly rely on the Model Context Protocol (MCP) to invoke isolated external sandboxes, disaggregated sandbox deployment introduces a fundamental tension between resource utilization and interactive tail latency. Persistent long-lived sandbox reservations incur excessive memory overhead at scale, while lazy on-demand instantiation generates severe cold-start penalties that degrade response performance under multi-tenant, multi-turn agent workloads. To resolve this dilemma, we present SpecBox, a runtime built around speculative sandbox preallocation tailored for dynamic LLM agent execution pipelines.
  At its core, SpecBox implements keyword matching and streaming semantic embedding to enable intent-driven sandbox prewarming, which identifies pending tool execution demands mid-LLM token generation and fully overlaps sandbox bootstrapping with model inference. To extend prewarming windows across sequential agent steps, the framework leverages context-aware stochastic prefetching atop a sandbox dependency graph to probabilistically forecast future sandbox switches ahead of execution. We complement these speculative mechanisms with two orthogonal optimizations: a semantic result cache that prunes redundant repeated sandbox invocations, and a dedicated out-of-band shared-memory transport plane that bypasses conventional network serialization to deliver zero-copy artifact transfers. Evaluated on high-concurrency multi-turn agent traces, our prototype demonstrates that SpecBox cuts P99 end-to-end latency by up to $2.9\times$ relative to the on-demand sandbox baseline, while slashing peak memory consumption by $45.9\%$ compared to permanently reserved sandbox deployments.

</details>


### [388] [Consensus In Asynchrony: Strictly Formal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24095)
*Ivan Klianev*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展Attiya等人的形式化框架，解决了完全异步环境中确定性崩溃容错共识的理论矛盾，发现单阶段协议导致了结论差异，并提出了能容忍多重崩溃故障的新算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 存在一个理论矛盾：一方面证明了完全异步环境中确定性崩溃容错共识是可能的，另一方面Attiya等人重新确认了FLP不可能性结果。需要填补这一基本理论空白。

Method: 采用严格形式化框架，扩展Attiya等人的推理方法，形式化证明协议正确性。关键发现是单个协议阶段的差异导致了相反结论。

Result: 发现单阶段协议操作将Attiya等人的发现与完全相反的结论区分开来；提出了能够容忍多重崩溃故障的新算法；提供了严格的形式化正确性证明。

Conclusion: 成功解决了完全异步环境中确定性崩溃容错共识的理论矛盾，填补了理论基础空白，为该领域提供了新的理论见解和算法工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we resolve the apparent contradiction between the proven possibility of deterministic crash-tolerant consensus in a fully asynchronous environment and the reconfirmation of the FLP impossibility result by Attiya, Castañeda, and Rajsbaum. With the use of a strictly formal framework that extends their reasoning, we close this fundamental gap in theory. Specifically, we demonstrate that a single protocol phase separates their findings from reaching the exact opposite conclusion. Another important outcome is a novel algorithm with ability to tolerate multiple crash faults. We provide a rigorous, strictly formal proof of correctness to validate our results.

</details>


### [389] [Certified Parallel-in-Time Sinkhorn for Dynamic Entropic Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24741)
*Xinyang Wen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TemporalSinkhorn是一种并行时间执行器，通过批处理未来候选解和修复机制，显著提升了动态应用中熵最优传输问题的求解速度（1.15x-4.315x加速），同时保证输出准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态应用（如最优传输流匹配）需要反复求解相关的熵最优传输问题，但传统分布式Sinkhorn算法采用顺序迭代且每次迭代后同步，导致计算效率低下。

Method: 提出TemporalSinkhorn并行时间执行器：1）批处理未来候选解及其修复；2）采用居中行分片证书仅接受确定性安全前缀；3）剩余候选解共享打包Sinkhorn更新；4）在线投影遗忘率设置审计里程碑；5）后验残差检查从深度低估中恢复。

Result: 在4块A100 GPU上，n=2048时：遗忘引导里程碑相比每次打包迭代审计减少1.15x-1.47x墙钟时间；相比顺序软c变换热启动，时间执行在六个合成流上快1.42x-3.55x；在流匹配小批次流上快3.054x-3.632x；在RTX 4060笔记本GPU固定核测试中获4.315x几何平均加速。所有测试零边际容忍违规。

Conclusion: TemporalSinkhorn成功实现了动态熵最优传输问题的并行时间加速，预测可改变工作分配但不会授权不准确输出，为端到端流匹配集成、优化求解器比较和多节点验证奠定基础。

Abstract: Dynamic applications, including optimal-transport Flow Matching, repeatedly solve related entropic optimal transport problems, yet conventional distributed Sinkhorn processes frames sequentially and synchronizes after every iteration. We present TemporalSinkhorn, a parallel-in-time executor that batches future candidates and their repairs without making output accuracy speculative. A centered, row-sharded certificate accepts only a deterministic safe prefix. The remaining candidates share packed Sinkhorn updates; an online projective forgetting rate places audit milestones, while a posteriori residual checks recover from every depth underestimate. Prediction can therefore change work placement but cannot authorize an inaccurate output.
  On 4 A100 GPUs, a 60-run, five-seed grid at n = 2048 shows that forgetting-guided milestones reduce wall time by 1.15x-1.47x relative to auditing every packed iteration in five statistically resolved regime cells. Against a sequential soft c-transform warm start, temporal execution is 1.42x-3.55x faster across six synthetic streams, with zero marginal-tolerance violations. On Flow Matching minibatch streams, temporal execution is 3.054x-3.632x faster than sequential carry at n = 2048, with no tolerance violations. A separate fixed-kernel test on an RTX 4060 Laptop GPU gives a 4.315x geometric-mean speedup. These are complementary deployment studies rather than a controlled hardware comparison. End-to-end Flow Matching integration, optimized-solver comparisons, and multi-node validation remain open.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [390] [Spectral Dynamics of Semantic Drift in Clinical Multi-Agent Language Model Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22758)
*Amritesh Banerjee*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究证明，多智能体诊断系统中常用的无标度和小世界网络拓扑会导致语义通信失败，提出基于图谱拉普拉斯算子的动态谱监控方法来确保系统可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于无标度网络（如BA网络）和小世界网络（如WS网络）的多智能体诊断框架假设其通信拓扑具有最优效率，但这一假设缺乏对语义数据的严格量化验证。在医疗诊断等安全关键场景中，结构瓶颈可能导致严重的幻觉传播和系统不可靠性，因此需要重新评估通信拓扑的有效性。

Method: 1. 将多智能体通信不确定性轨迹映射到768维Bio_ClinicalBERT嵌入空间，通过分析性各向同性方差代理进行量化分析。
2. 对比研究Barabási-Albert (BA)网络、Watts-Strogatz (WS)网络和Erdős-Rényi随机图配置的语义漂移特性。
3. 利用相变矩阵分析局部稠密派系对幻觉数据的影响。
4. 提出动态谱监控方法：通过对图拉普拉斯算子连续特征分解，施加代数连通性的严格下界约束（$λ_{2_{min}}$），确保全局状态扩散，时间复杂度为$O(N^3)$。

Result: 1. 局部稠密派系会将幻觉数据限制在局部，阻止全局共识，使系统永久逼近熵饱和阈值$H_{\infty} \approx 5.947$。
2. 终端余弦相似度严重退化53.29%，完全覆盖原始真值。
3. 高聚类架构中终端语义漂移呈现51.81%的灾难性方差放大（$ρ= 1.5181$），远超Erdős-Rényi配置（$ρ= 1.0766$），证明系统完全不可预测。
4. 中心节点主导的系统不仅不能减少错误，反而自主复合局部幻觉。

Conclusion: 在集成迭代式大语言模型的多智能体诊断框架中，必须将拓扑稳定性视为不可妥协的量化要求。动态谱监控通过约束代数连通性可有效保障全局状态扩散，从而确保自主医疗诊断系统的可靠性与安全性。

Abstract: The integration of iterative LLMs within multi-agent diagnostic frameworks requires a rigorous quantitative reevaluation of underlying communication topologies. Frequently used architectural paradigms depend on scale-free or small-world networks, assuming optimal communication efficiency. Our study mathematically dismantles that assumption for semantic data. By mapping multi-agent communication uncertainty trajectories onto a 768-dimensional Bio_ClinicalBERT embedding space via an analytical isotropic variance proxy using Barab'asi--Albert (BA) and Watts--Strogatz (WS) networks, we prove that structural bottlenecks compromise diagnostic safety. Our phase transition matrices illustrate that localized dense cliques confine hallucinated data, preventing global consensus and forcing the system toward a permanent entropy saturation threshold of $H_{\infty} \approx 5.947$. As a result, we measure a severe terminal cosine similarity degradation of 53.29%, completely overwriting the original ground-truth. Moreover, the terminal semantic drift reveals a catastrophic variance amplification of 51.81% ($ρ= 1.5181$) in highly clustered architectures, proving total system unpredictability when compared to Erdős--R'enyi configurations ($ρ= 1.0766$). Instead of reducing errors, hub-centric systems autonomously compound localized hallucinations. By introducing dynamic spectral monitoring operating at an $\mathcal{O}(N^3)$ time complexity and imposing a strict lower bound on algebraic connectivity ($λ_{2_{min}}$) via the continuous eigen-decomposition of the graph Laplacian, we present a mathematically rigorous technique to ensure global state diffusion. Securing the reliability of autonomous medical diagnostics necessitates treating topological stability as a non-negotiable quantitative imperative.

</details>


### [391] [Emergent Behaviour in Financial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23311)
*Omar Inverso,Emilio Tuosto,Dragisa Zunic*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文分析了复杂系统中涌现行为的自动化推理挑战，以电子金融市场为案例，识别并结构化了复杂性的来源，提出了一个面向市场机制形式化规范与分析的系统化研究纲领。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统中的涌现行为研究在多个学科中受到关注，形式化方法社区也开始意识到需要跨学科合作并开发相应的计算机辅助推理技术。然而，复杂性系统的独特特征使得自动化推理面临巨大挑战，因此需要系统性地识别和处理这些复杂性来源。

Method: 以电子金融市场为驱动案例，识别并结构化需要处理的复杂性来源；避免评估特定技术解决方案或框架（这通常需要简化假设并偏离研究现象本身），而是阐述处理自动化分析中主要技术方面的可能替代方案，同时保持对领域的坚实且具体的解释。

Result: 识别并结构化了涌现现象计算支持分析中需要解决的复杂性来源；提出了处理自动化分析中主要技术方面的可能替代方案；构建了市场机制形式化规范与分析的系统化研究纲领框架。

Conclusion: 通过保持对领域的具体解释并避免过度简化的技术假设，可以为市场机制的形式化规范与分析建立更系统化的研究方法，为复杂系统涌现行为的自动化推理研究提供了清晰的路线图。

Abstract: Some properties of so-called complex or collective systems can be observed to emerge from the interactions of elementary agents. This phenomenon, known as emergent behaviour, has long since been studied in the most diverse disciplines, with recent growing awareness from the formal methods community about the opportunity of opening up to seemingly distant disciplines with appropriate technology for computer-aided reasoning. Different peculiar elements of complexity make automated reasoning on these systems particularly challenging. We consider electronic financial markets to drive our discussion. We identify and structure the sources of complexity to tackle in order to provide computational support for the analysis of emergent phenomena. We refrain from evaluating the suitability of specific technical solutions or frameworks of preference, which would as usual require simplifying assumptions and divert from the actual phenomenon of interest. Rather, we elaborate on possible alternatives to handle some of the main technical aspects involved in automated analysis, while retaining a solid and concrete interpretation of the domain, and in doing so outline a more systematic research program for the formal specification and analysis of market mechanisms.

</details>


### [392] [MulRobBench: A Decision-Level Benchmark for Safe and Security-Policy-Compliant Multimodal UAV Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23870)
*Belal S. Alsinglawi,Weizheng Wang,Junyi Wu,Yi Jiang,Lianhai Lin,Merouane Debbah,Izzat Alsmadi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了MulRobBench，这是一个面向智慧城市环境下视觉-语言-动作（VLA）无人机智能体的离线、协议条件化基准，用于评估物理证据、协议约束与动作风险在关键决策中的耦合情况，并在17个多模态模型上进行了系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV与多模态基准主要关注感知、导航、协作与推理能力，但缺乏对关键决策中物理证据、协议约束与动作风险是否保持耦合的评估。随着智慧城市空域使UAV从被动感知平台转变为必须遵循操作规则的赛博物理决策者，亟需一个能够在降质观测与模糊语言条件下评估决策可信度的基准。

Method: 提出MulRobBench基准，将真实UAV多模态观测、协议级安全策略和动作级赛博物理安全性集成于统一评估框架。基准包含3,024个样本，覆盖17个任务分类节点和12个评分维度，分为四个阶段：操作语境理解、多模态证据仲裁、降质感知推理和风险感知动作规划。评估结合语义评分与结构诊断，涵盖政策合规、格式合规、不安全动作、解析失败及维度有效性。同时进行了20锚点的模态消融研究以验证视觉与文本输入对决策的影响。

Result: 在17个多模态模型的评估中，最佳语义协议-决策得分仅为0.5141，最佳严格平均评分维度准确率仅为0.1599。受控的20锚点模态消融研究显示每个模型改变4-15个动作选择，证实视觉与文本输入均影响决策。分析识别出模态信任选择、约束提取、眩光、数据缺失和操作员简写是导致决策不稳定的主要原因。

Conclusion: MulRobBench为在现实操作约束下进行可信多模态UAV决策提供了可复现的基准，揭示了当前模型在协议合规与风险感知决策方面的显著不足，并为未来研究指明了需要重点关注的决策不稳定因素。

Abstract: Smart-city airspace is transforming Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) from passive sensing platforms into cyber-physical decision makers that must follow operational rules under degraded observations and ambiguous language. Existing UAV and multimodal benchmarks evaluate perception, navigation, collaboration, and reasoning, but few assess whether physical evidence, protocol constraints, and action risk remain coupled during critical decisions. We introduce MulRobBench, an offline, protocol-conditioned benchmark for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) UAV agents in smart-city environments. MulRobBench integrates real UAV multimodal observations, protocol-level security policies, and action-level cyber-physical safety into a unified evaluation framework. The benchmark contains 3,024 samples spanning 17 task taxonomy nodes and 12 scoring dimensions across four stages: operational context understanding, multimodal evidence arbitration, degradation-aware reasoning, and risk-aware action planning. Evaluation combines semantic scoring with structural diagnostics, including policy compliance, format compliance, unsafe actions, parsing failures, and dimension-level validity. Across 17 multimodal models, the best semantic protocol-decision score reaches only 0.5141, while the best strict mean scoring-dimension accuracy is 0.1599. A controlled 20-anchor modality-ablation study changes 4-15 action selections per model, confirming that both visual and textual inputs influence decisions. Analysis identifies modality-trust selection, constraint extraction, glare, missing data, and operator shorthand as the primary causes of decision instability. MulRobBench provides a reproducible benchmark for trustworthy multimodal UAV decision making under realistic operational constraints.

</details>


### [393] [A Comparative Study of MCP and A2A for Inter-Agent Coordination in LLM-Based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23884)
*Ionut Predoaia,Tuong Manh Vu,Konstantinos Barmpis,Dimitris Kolovos,Antonio García-Domínguez*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文通过实际软件工程任务对比了MCP和A2A两种多智能体协调协议。研究发现MCP实现轻量、复杂度低，但需应用层显式管理状态；A2A提供丰富的协议级抽象支持状态化协调，但实现复杂度显著更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着由异构、工具和LLM介导的智能体组件组成的智能体系统在工业界的快速兴起，智能体间协调和协议设计面临实际问题。现有缺乏从多智能体系统工程角度对MCP和A2A协议进行实现导向的比较研究。

Method: 基于文献和工业合作伙伴讨论提取一组需求（智能体可发现性、多部分消息、多轮对话、异步通信、可观察性、互操作性和访问控制），针对同一软件工程任务分别实现MCP和A2A的多智能体系统，并从多智能体系统工程视角进行实证比较。

Result: MCP可在受限的LLM系统中通过较轻量的实现模型支持智能体间协调，协调复杂度较低，但对话状态管理和任务生命周期处理须在应用层显式实现。A2A通过协议级的任务和生命周期抽象，原生支持状态化、多轮协调，但实现和协调复杂度显著更高。

Conclusion: 研究结果作为设计观察而非普适性结论，强调了两种协议在抽象层次与协调职责分布上的权衡，提示协议抽象塑造了当代智能体系统中协调职责的分布方式，具体选择需根据系统需求权衡轻量性与原生功能丰富度。

Abstract: Recent industry practice has seen the rapid emergence of agentic systems composed of heterogeneous, tool- and LLM-mediated agent components, raising practical questions about inter-agent coordination and protocol design. This paper presents an implementation-grounded comparison of the Model Context Protocol (MCP) and the Agent2Agent (A2A) protocol, from a multi-agent systems engineering perspective, using an inter-agent coordination scenario involving LLM-based agents. We evaluate an MCP-based and an A2A-based multi-agent implementation of the same software engineering task against a set of requirements derived from prior literature and discussions with industry partners, including agent discoverability, multi-part messaging, multi-turn conversations, asynchronous communication, observability, interoperability, and access control. The results evidence that MCP can support inter-agent coordination in constrained LLM-based systems through a comparatively lightweight implementation model with lower coordination complexity, although coordination concerns such as conversational state management and task lifecycle handling must be implemented explicitly at the application layer. In contrast, A2A provides richer native support for stateful, multi-turn coordination through protocol-level abstractions for tasks and lifecycle management, but this comes with substantially greater implementation and coordination complexity. Given the narrow scope of the evaluated coordination pattern, these findings are presented as design observations from an empirical experience report rather than general claims of protocol suitability or superiority across broader classes of MAS, highlighting trade-offs and how protocol abstractions shape the distribution of coordination responsibilities in contemporary agentic systems.

</details>


### [394] [Moral Hazard in Multi-Agent Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23982)
*Dane Malenfant*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Dialogue Moral Hazard Game，用于测试语言代理在隐藏行动结构下的合作行为。研究发现基础模型普遍存在“表面合作”问题，且优化方法可能只改变结果而不改善合作机制，强调了机制层面评估的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当 socially valuable effort 成本高、难以观测且主要惠及他人时，合作往往会失败。现有研究缺乏一个可控的实验框架来系统性地分析和诊断语言代理在此类 hidden-action 结构下的合作失败原因。

Method: 1. 提出 Dialogue Moral Hazard Game：一个基于 Holmström 团队道德风险模型的文本游戏，代理可选择保留即时奖励或支付查询成本以揭示主要帮助下游代理的安全信息。
2. 评估七种开源权重语言模型，并将行为分解为六个维度：查询使用、信息传递、本地奖励保留、不安全选择、格式有效性、团队成功率。
3. 应用四种诊断性更新机制：监督微调(SFT)、RLOO、序列SFT+RLOO和GEPA提示优化。

Result: 基础模型普遍表现出两类问题：(1) 保留本地奖励但团队失败；(2) 发起查询却未传递能改变最终决策的信息。不同优化方法效果异质：OLMo-7B展现出最清晰的机制一致的权重级改进，而GEPA有时提高团队成功率的同时减少或消除了成本高昂的查询行为。

Conclusion: 优化方法可能在不恢复预期合作机制的情况下改变聚合奖励，这暴露了仅报告团队成功率的局限性。研究呼吁未来的评估应报告机制层面行为，而非仅关注团队成功指标，以更准确地理解和改进语言代理的合作能力。

Abstract: Cooperation can fail when socially valuable effort is costly, weakly observable, and mainly benefits others. Drawing on Holmström's team moral-hazard model, we introduce the Dialogue Moral Hazard Game, a controlled textual game that operationalizes this hidden-action structure for language agents. In each episode, an agent can preserve an immediate local reward or pay a query cost to reveal a hidden safety fact that primarily helps another agent's downstream decision. We evaluate seven open-weight language models and decompose behavior into query use, realized information transfer, local-reward preservation, unsafe choice, format validity, and team success. Base models commonly preserve local reward without team success or query without communicating information that changes the final decision. We then use supervised fine-tuning, RLOO, sequential SFT+RLOO, and GEPA prompt optimization as diagnostic update mechanisms. Their effects are heterogeneous: OLMo-7B shows the clearest mechanism-consistent weight-level improvement, whereas GEPA sometimes improves team success while reducing or eliminating costly queries. Thus, optimization can shift aggregate reward without recovering the intended cooperative mechanism, motivating evaluations that report mechanism-level behavior rather than team success alone.

</details>


### [395] [Decentralised Consensus Learning Networks: SME Rotation Without Centralised Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24416)
*Florin Neagu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了一种去中心化、基于共识的多智能体学习框架，通过同伴验证而非预设奖励来涌现专业知识。大规模实验表明SME轮换具有鲁棒性和规模不变性，高维信念空间中专家角色会自然集中到与集体信念最一致的智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI学习系统主要依赖中心化奖励信号，这强加了单一的"正确"或"有价值"知识的外部定义，限制了知识发现的多样性和自主性。

Method: 提出去中心化多智能体学习框架：智能体通过加权的社交共识更新信念；信任根据同伴一致性推断的能力分配而非依赖真实标签；SME身份动态分配为能力排名的顶级百分位而非固定标签。在84次仿真中测试30-10000个智能体、多种图拓扑、稀疏大规模网络、标量和向量信念表示、维度扫描(D=1-500)等。

Result: 阶段1显示SME轮换鲁棒、持久、拓扑不变和规模不变：90-100%智能体获得SME身份，多数专业度更替发生在信念收敛后且随网络规模增加。阶段2和3显示向量信念引入异质收敛和级联动力学，揭示5种不同动力学区域。高维度(D=150-200)时网络达到稳定部分共识，专业知识集中到单一智能体；ETA敏感性分析表明此集中由信念维度驱动而非随机噪声。

Conclusion: 高维共识空间中，与集体信念最一致的智能体自然涌现为公认专家，这是去中心化学习的涌现属性，为构建无中心化奖励的AI学习系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Centralised reward signals dominate modern AI learning systems, but they impose a single external definition of correct or valuable knowledge. We present a decentralised, consensus-based multi-agent learning framework in which expertise emerges through peer validation rather than prescribed reward. Agents update beliefs via weighted social consensus, while trust is allocated according to competence inferred from peer consistency instead of ground truth. Subject-matter expert (SME) status is assigned dynamically as a top-percentile competence rank rather than a fixed label.
  We evaluate the framework across 84 simulation runs spanning 30 to 10,000 agents, multiple graph topologies, sparse large-scale networks, scalar and vector belief representations, dimensionality sweeps (D=1-500), multi-seed robustness tests, and parameter sensitivity analyses. Phase 1 shows that SME rotation is robust, persistent, topology-invariant, and scale-invariant: 90-100% of agents attain SME status, with most expertise turnover occurring after belief convergence and increasing with network size. Phases 2 and 3 show that vector beliefs introduce heterogeneous convergence with cascade dynamics and reveal five distinct dynamical regimes as belief dimensionality increases. At high dimensionality (D=150-200), the network reaches stable partial consensus while expertise becomes increasingly concentrated in a single agent. ETA sensitivity analysis demonstrates that this concentration is driven by belief dimensionality rather than stochastic noise. We interpret this behaviour as an emergent property of decentralised learning: in complex high-dimensional consensus spaces, the agent most consistently aligned with the collective belief naturally emerges as the recognised expert.

</details>
